## XIII A Bag of Tricks We Don’t Have / 第十三章 我们没有的锦囊妙计

===Original===
He flinched inwardly, and tightened his eye-muscles on the edge of the monocle to keep from flinching physically as well, trying to freeze out of his face the consternation he felt.

“That’s bad, Kent,” he said. “Very bad. I’d been counting heavily on Dr. Gomes to design a bomb of our own.”

“Well, general, if you please.” That was Air-Commodore Leslie Hargreaves. “You say you suspect that King Orgzild has developed a nuclear bomb. If that’s true, it’s a horrible danger to all of us. But I find it hard to believe that the Keegarkans could have done so, with their resources and at their technological level. Now, if it had been the Kragans, that would have been different, but.…”

“Paula, you’d better carry on and explain what you told me, and add anything else you can think of that might be relevant.… Is that sound-recorder turned on? Then turn it on, somebody; we want this taped.”

Paula rose and began talking: “I suppose you all understand what conditions are on Niflheim, and how these Ulleran native workers are employed; however, I’d better begin by explaining the purpose for which these nuclear bombs were designed and used.…”

===Chinese===
他心中一凛，赶忙绷紧眼肌夹住单片眼镜的边缘，免得身子也跟着一颤，一面竭力把内心的惊惶从脸上冻结下去，不露分毫。

“情况不妙，肯特（Kent），”他说，“非常不妙。我原本满心指望戈麦斯（Gomes）博士能给我们设计出一枚自己的炸弹来。”

“哦，将军，恕我直言。”说话的是空军准将莱斯利·哈格里夫斯（Air-Commodore Leslie Hargreaves）。“您说您怀疑奥格齐尔德（Orgzild）王已经造出了核弹。如果属实，那对我们大家都是可怕的危险。可我很难相信基加克人（Keegarkans）凭他们那点资源、那样的技术水平能办得到。倘若换作克拉冈人（Kragans），那又另当别论，可是……”

“保拉（Paula），还是由你接着讲吧，把你对我说的那番话向大家解释清楚，再补充些你能想到的相关情况。……录音机开了没有？没开就请哪位去打开；这一段我们要录下来。”

保拉站起身，开始讲道：“想必各位都清楚尼福尔海姆（Niflheim）那边是什么条件，也清楚这些乌勒尔（Uller）土著工人是如何被雇用的；不过，我还是先交代一下设计和使用这些核弹的目的吧。……”

===Original===
He smiled; she realized that he needed time to think, and she was stalling to provide it. He drew a pencil and pad toward him and began doodling in a bored manner, deliberately closing his mind to what she was saying. There were two assumptions, he considered: first, that King Orgzild already possessed a nuclear bomb which he could use when he chose, and, second, that in the absence of Dr. Gomes, such a bomb could only be produced on Gongonk Island after lengthy experimental work. If both of these assumptions were true, he had just heard the death-sentence of every Terran on Uller. The first he did not for a moment doubt. The reasons for making it were too good. He dismissed it from further consideration and concentrated on the second.

“… what’s known as a Nagasaki-type bomb, the first type of plutonium-bomb developed,” Paula was saying. “Really, it’s a technological antique, but it was good enough for the purpose, and Dr. Gomes could build it with locally available materials.…”

That was the crux of it. The plutonium bomb, from a military standpoint, was as obsolete as the flintlock musket had been at the time of the Second World War. He reviewed, quickly, the history of weapons-development since the beginning of the Atomic Era. The emphasis, since the end of the Second World War, had all been on nuclear weapons and rocket-missiles. There had been the H-bomb, itself obsolescent, and the Bethe-cyle bomb, and the subneutron bomb, and the omega-ray bomb, and the nega-matter bomb, and then the end of civilization in the Northern Hemisphere and the rise of the new civilization in South America and South Africa and Australia. Today, the small-arms and artillery his troops were using were merely slight refinements on the weapons of the First Century, and all the modern nuclear weapons used by the Terran Federation were produced at the Space Navy base on Mars, by a small force of experts whose skills were almost as closed to the general scientific and technical world as the secrets of a medieval guild. The old A-bomb was an historical curiosity, and there was nobody on Uller who had more than a layman’s knowledge of the intricate technology of modern nuclear weapons. There were plenty of good nuclear-power engineers on Gongonk Island, but how long would it take them to design and build a plutonium bomb?

“… also has a good understanding of Lingua Terra,” Paula was saying. “He and Dr. Murillo conversed bilingually, just as I’ve heard General von Schlichten and King Kankad talking to one another. I haven’t any idea whether or not Gorkrink could read Lingua Terra, or, if so, what papers or plans he might have seen.”

“Just a minute, Paula,” he said. “Colonel Grinell, what does your branch have on this Gorkrink?”

===Chinese===
他微微一笑；她明白他需要时间思考，便故意放慢话头替他争取。他把铅笔和便笺簿拉到手边，一副百无聊赖的样子信手涂鸦，存心不去听她讲什么。他心里盘算，此处有两条假定：其一，奥格齐尔德王已经拥有一枚核弹，想什么时候用就什么时候用；其二，戈麦斯博士既已不在，这样的炸弹只有在贡贡克岛（Gongonk Island）经过漫长的试验之后才造得出来。倘若两条假定都成立，那么他刚才听到的，就是乌勒尔星上每一个地球人（Terran）的死刑判决。第一条，他片刻也不曾怀疑——立此假定的理由实在太充分了。他把它撇开，不再细想，集中考虑第二条。

“……也就是所谓的长崎型炸弹，最早研制出来的那类钚弹，”保拉正说着，“说实在的，这在技术上已是老古董，但用于这个目的绰绰有余，而且戈麦斯博士用当地弄得到的材料就能造出来。……”

问题的症结正在这里。从军事角度看，钚弹之落伍，犹如第二次世界大战时代的燧发枪。他飞快回顾了原子纪元（Atomic Era）开端以来的武器发展史。自第二次世界大战结束以来，发展的重心全在核武器和火箭导弹上。先是氢弹——它本身也已过时——然后是贝特循环炸弹（Bethe-cycle bomb）、亚中子弹、欧米伽射线弹、负物质弹，再往后，便是北半球文明的终结，和南美、南非、澳大利亚新文明的崛起。到今天，他部队所用的轻武器和火炮，不过是第一世纪（First Century，原子纪元）武器的一点小改良；而地球联邦（Terran Federation）所用的一切现代核武器，都是在火星的太空海军基地里、由一小批专家生产的——他们的技艺对于整个科学技术界而言，几乎像中世纪行会的秘技一样封闭。老式原子弹已成历史奇物，整个乌勒尔星上，对现代核武器那套复杂工艺，没有一个人懂得比外行更多。贡贡克岛上有的是优秀的核动力工程师，可要他们设计并造出一枚钚弹，得花多久？

“……而且对地球语（Lingua Terra）也掌握得很好，”保拉正说着，“他和穆里略（Murillo）博士两人交谈时双语并用，就像我听到过的冯·施利希滕将军（General von Schlichten）和坎卡德王（King Kankad）交谈那样。戈克林克（Gorkrink）识不识地球语文字，识的话又见过哪些文件或图纸，我就无从知晓了。”

“稍等一下，保拉，”他说。“格里内尔上校（Colonel Grinell），你们情报口手里有没有关于这个戈克林克的材料？”

===Original===
“He’s the son of King Orgzild, and the daughter of Prince Jurnkonk,” Grinell said. “We knew he’d signed on for Nif, two years ago, but the story we got was that he’d fallen out of favor at court and had been exiled. I can see, now, that that was planted to mislead us. As to whether or not he can read Lingua Terra, my belief is that he can. We know that he can understand it when spoken. He could have learned to read at one of those schools Mohammed Ferriera set up, ten or fifteen years ago.”

“And Dr. Gomes and Dr. Murillo and Dr. Livesey left papers and plans lying around all over the place,” Paula added. “If he went to Niflheim as a spy, he could have copied almost anything.”

“Well, there you have it,” von Schlichten said. “When Gorkrink found out that plutonium can be used for bombs, he began gathering all the information he could. And as soon as he got home, he turned it all over to Pappy Orgzild.”

“That still doesn’t mean that the Kee-geeks were able to do anything with it,” Air-Commodore Hargreaves argued.

“I think it does,” von Schlichten differed. “As soon as Orgzild would hear about the possibility of making a plutonium bomb, he’d set up an A-bomb project, and don’t think of it in terms of the old First Century Manhattan Project. There would be no problem of producing fissionables—we’ve been scattering refined plutonium over this planet like confetti.”

===Chinese===
“他是奥格齐尔德王的儿子，同时是朱恩孔克亲王（Prince Jurnkonk）的女儿，”格里内尔说。“我们知道他两年前签约去了尼夫（Nif，尼福尔海姆的简称），但当时听到的说法是，他在宫廷失了宠，遭放逐了。如今我看明白，那套说辞是故意放出来迷惑我们的。至于他识不识地球语文字，我的看法是：识。我们知道他能听懂地球语口语。他可能是在穆罕默德·费雷拉（Mohammed Ferriera）十年十五年前兴办的那类学校里学会认字的。”

“再说，戈麦斯博士、穆里略博士、利夫西（Livesey）博士他们，文件图纸到处乱摊，”保拉补充道，“他若是抱着间谍使命去的尼福尔海姆，差不多什么都可能抄到手。”

“唉，事情一目了然，”冯·施利希滕说。“戈克林克一发现钚能用来造炸弹，便开始搜罗一切搜罗得到的情报。一回到家，就全数交给了老爹奥格齐尔德（Pappy Orgzild）。”

“可那仍然不等于基加土佬（Kee-geeks）就能拿它派上用场，”空军准将哈格里夫斯反驳道。

“我看恰恰等于，”冯·施利希滕持不同意见。“奥格齐尔德一听说有造钚弹的可能，就会立刻上马原子弹工程——可别拿老第一世纪的曼哈顿计划来套。裂变材料的生产根本不成问题——这些年我们把精炼钚撒遍这颗行星，跟撒彩纸屑似的。”
===Original===
“Well, an A-bomb’s a pretty complicated piece of mechanism, even if you have the plans for it,” Kent Pickering said. “As I recall, there have to be several subcritical masses of plutonium, or U-235, or whatever, blown together by shaped charges of explosive, all of which have to be fired simultaneously. That would mean a lot of electrical fittings that I can’t see these geeks making by hand.”

“I can,” Paula said. “Have you ever seen the work these native jewelers do? And didn’t you tell me about a clockwork thing they have at the university here, to show the apparent movements of the sun.…”

“That’s right,” von Schlichten said. “And what they couldn’t make, they could have bought from us; we’ve sold them a lot of electrical equipment.”

“All right, they could have built an A-bomb,” Buhrmann said. “But did they?”

“We assume they tried to. Gorkrink got back from Nif on the *Canberra*, three months ago,” von Schlichten said. “If Orgzild decided to build an A-bomb, he wouldn’t give the signal for this uprising until he either had one or knew he couldn’t make one, and he wouldn’t give up trying in only three months. Therefore, I think we can assume that he succeeded, and had succeeded at the time he sent Gorkrink here to get that four tons of plutonium we let him have, and, incidentally, to tell Ghroghrank to pass the word to have Sid Harrington poisoned according to plan.”

===Chinese===
“话虽如此，原子弹终究是件相当复杂的机械，就算图纸在手也一样，”肯特·皮克林（Kent Pickering）说。“依我记得的原理，得有好几块亚临界的钚块，或者铀-235 之类，靠炸药聚能装药轰拢到一处，而且必须全部同时起爆。那就意味着一大批电气部件，我实在想象不出这些土佬（geeks）能靠手工做出来。”

“我倒想象得出，”保拉说。“你见过这些土著珠宝匠的手艺没有？还有，你不是跟我说过，这儿的大学里有他们造的一件机械装置，用来演示太阳的视运动。……”

“不错，”冯·施利希滕说。“他们造不出来的，也尽可以从我们手里买；我们卖给过他们大量电气设备。”

“好吧，就算他们造得出原子弹，”布尔曼（Buhrmann）说，“可他们到底造了没有？”

“我们不妨假定他们试过。三个月前，戈克林克乘「堪培拉」号（Canberra）从尼夫回来，”冯·施利希滕说。“奥格齐尔德若是决定造原子弹，那么不等到手一颗、或者确知造不出来，他是不会发出这场起义的信号的，而短短三个月他绝不会放弃尝试。所以我认为可以断定：他成功了，而且在他派戈克林克来这里——来取我们拱手让他拿走的那四吨钚，顺便让格罗格林克（Ghroghrank）传话按计划毒杀席德·哈灵顿（Sid Harrington）——的时候，就已经成功了。”

===Original===
“Then why didn’t he just use it on us at the start of the uprising?” Meyerstein wanted to know.

“Why should he? Getting rid of us is only the first step in Orgzild’s plan,” Grinell said. “Back as far as geek history goes, the Kings of Keegark have been trying to conquer Konkrook and the Free Cities and make themselves masters of the whole Takkad Sea area. Let Konkrook wipe us out, and then he can move in his troops and take Konkrook. Or, if we beat off the geeks here, as we seem to be doing, he can bomb us out and then move in on Konkrook. I think that as long as we’re fighting here, he’ll wait. The more damage we do to Konkrook, the easier it’ll be for him.”

“Then we’d better start dragging our feet on the Konkrook front,” Laviola said. “And get busy trying to build a bomb of our own.”

Von Schlichten looked up at the big screen, on which the battle of Konkrook was being projected from an overhead pickup.

“I’ll agree on the second half of it,” von Schlichten said. “And we’ll also have to set up some kind of security-patrol system against bombers from Keegark. And as soon as *Procyon* gets here, we’ll have to send her out to hunt down and destroy those two Boer-class freighters, the *Jan Smuts* and the *Kruger*. And we’ll have to arrange for protection of Kankad’s Town; that’s sure to be another of Orgzild’s high-priority targets. As to the action against Konkrook, I’ll rely on your advice, Them. Can we delay the fall of the city for any length of time?”

===Chinese===
“那他何不趁起义一开始就拿它招呼我们？”迈耶斯坦（Meyerstein）想知道缘由。

“他何必呢？除掉我们，不过是奥格齐尔德全盘计划的第一步，”格里内尔说。“自有土佬历史记载以来，基加克的历代国王无不想征服孔克鲁克（Konkrook）和各自由城邦，称霸整个塔卡德海（Takkad Sea）地区。让孔克鲁克把我们斩尽杀绝，他再挥师而入，坐收孔克鲁克。又或者，如果我们像眼下这样把这里的土佬打退，他可以用炸弹把我们炸上西天，再进兵孔克鲁克。我想，只要我们还在这里打，他就会按兵不动。我们对孔克鲁克毁得越狠，他的事就越好办。”

“那我们最好在孔克鲁克战线上开始磨洋工，”拉维奥拉（Laviola）说，“同时抓紧造一枚我们自己的炸弹。”

冯·施利希滕抬头望向大屏幕，屏幕上正放映着一部俯拍摄像机传来的孔克鲁克战况。

“后半句我同意，”冯·施利希滕说。“另外，我们还必须建立一套警戒巡逻体系，防备从基加克（Keegark）来的轰炸机。等「南河三」号（Procyon）一到，就得派她出去追歼那两艘布尔级货船——「扬·斯穆茨」号（Jan Smuts）和「克鲁格」号（Kruger）。还得部署坎卡德城（Kankad’s Town）的防护；那里必定也是奥格齐尔德的重点打击目标之一。至于孔克鲁克方面的战事，地米（Them），我要倚重你的意见。我们能不能把这座城的陷落拖延上些时日？”

===Original===
M’zangwe shook his head. “When we divert contragravity to security-patrol work, the ground action’ll slow up a little, of course. But the geeks are about knocked out, now.”

“The hell with it, then. I doubt if we’d be able to buy much time from Orgzild by delaying victory in the city, and we’ll probably need the troops as workers over here.” He turned to Pickering. “Dr. Pickering, what sort of a crew can you scrape together to design a bomb for us?” he asked.

“Well, there’s Martirano, and Sternberg, and Howard Fu-Chung, and Piet van Reenen, and.…” He nodded to himself. “I can get six or eight of them in here in about twenty minutes; I’ll have a project set up and working in a couple of hours. There has to be somebody qualified on duty at the plant, all the time, of course, but.…”

“All right, call them in. I want the bomb finished by yesterday afternoon. And everybody with you, and you, yourself, had better revert to civilian status. This isn’t something you can do by the numbers, and I don’t want anybody who doesn’t know what it’s all about pulling rank on your outfit. Go ahead, call in your gang, and let me know what you’ll be able to do, as soon as possible.”

He turned to Hargreaves. “Les, you’ll have charge of flying the security patrols, and doing anything else you can to keep Orgzild from bombing us before we can bomb him. You’ll have priority on everything second only to Pickering.”

===Chinese===
姆赞圭（M’zangwe）摇了摇头。“等我们把反重力（contragravity）力量抽去执行警戒巡逻，地面战事当然会放慢一些。不过现在，土佬们已经差不多被打趴下了。”

“那就随它去吧。靠推迟城破的时日，我怀疑我们从奥格齐尔德手里买不来多少时间，况且这边多半还需要把部队当劳工使。”他转向皮克林。“皮克林博士，你能给我们拼凑一支什么样的队伍来设计炸弹？”他问。

“嗯，有马蒂拉诺（Martirano），有斯滕伯格（Sternberg），有霍华德·傅中（Howard Fu-Chung），有皮特·范雷嫩（Piet van Reenen），还有……”他自顾自点了点头。“二十分钟左右，我能叫六到八个人过来；再给几个小时，我就能把工程组建起来开工。当然，厂里随时都得留一名够格的人值班，不过……”

“好，去叫他们来。我要这颗炸弹昨天下午就造好。还有，你的人手——包括你自己——最好都恢复平民身份。这种事没法照着条令按部就班地干，我可不想让哪个不知就里的人拿军衔在你的班组里压人。去吧，把你那帮人叫来，尽早告诉我你们能做到什么程度。”

他转向哈格里夫斯。“莱斯（Les），警戒巡逻的飞行由你统管，另外再想尽一切办法，别让奥格齐尔德抢在我们炸他之前先炸了我们。各项需求的优先权，你排第二，仅次于皮克林。”

===Original===
Hargreaves nodded. “As you say, general, we’ll have to protect Kankad’s, as well as this place. It’s about five hundred miles from here to Kankad’s, and eight-fifty miles from Kankad’s to Keegark.…”

He stopped talking to von Schlichten, and began muttering to himself, running over the names of ships, and the speeds and payload capacities of airboats, and distances. In about five minutes, he would have a programme worked out; in the meantime, von Schlichten could only be patient and contain himself. He looked along the table, and caught sight of a thin-faced, saturnine-looking man in a green shirt, with a colonel’s three concentric circles marked on the shoulders in silver-paint. Emmett Pearson, the communications chief.

“Emmett,” he said, “those orbiters you have strung around this planet, two thousand miles out, for telecast rebroadcast stations. How much of a crew could be put on one of them?”

Pearson laughed. “Crew of what, general? White mice, or trained cockroaches? There isn’t room inside one of those things for anything bigger to move around.”

“Well, I know they’re automatic, but how do you service them?”

===Chinese===
哈格里夫斯点了点头。“遵命，将军。除了此地，坎卡德城也必须保护。从这儿到坎卡德城约五百英里，从坎卡德城到基加克八百五十英里。……”

他不再跟冯·施利希滕说话，开始自顾自地低声嘟哝，逐个念着一艘艘舰船的名字、一艘艘飞艇（airboat）的速度和载重、一段段里程。大约五分钟后，他就能把整套方案盘算出来；这期间，冯·施利希滕只能耐着性子等。他顺着长桌望去，瞥见一个瘦削脸膛、神情阴鸷的男人，穿一件绿衬衫，肩头用银漆画着上校的三道同心圆记号——埃米特·皮尔逊（Emmett Pearson），通讯主管。

“埃米特，”他说，“你环绕这颗行星布下的那些轨道器，在两千英里高空充作电视差转站——其中一座上面，能安排下多少工作人员？”

皮尔逊笑了。“什么工作人员，将军？小白鼠，还是驯化的蟑螂？那玩意儿里头，稍大一点的东西都转不开身。”

“得了，我知道它们是全自动的，可你们怎么检修维护？”

===Original===
“From the outside. They’re only ten feet through, by about twenty in length, with a fifteen-foot ball at either end, and everything’s in sections, which can be taken out. Our maintenance-gang goes up in a thing like a small spaceship, and either works on the outside in spacesuits, or puts in a new section and brings the unserviceable one down here to the shops.”

“Ah, and what sort of a thing is this small spaceship, now?”

“A thing like a pair of fifty-ton lorries, with airlocks between, and connected at the middle; airtight, of course, and pressurized and insulated like a spaceship. One side’s living quarters for a six-man crew—sometimes the gang’s out for as long as a week at a time—and the other side’s a workshop.”

That sounded interesting. With contragravity, of course, terms like “escape-velocity” and “mass-ratio” were of purely antiquarian interest.

“How long,” he asked Pearson, “would it take to fit that vehicle with a full set of detection instruments—radar, infrared and ultraviolet vision, electron-telescope, heat and radiation detectors, the whole works—and spot it about a hundred to a hundred and fifty miles above Keegark?”

===Chinese===
“从外面弄。它们直径不过十英尺，长约二十英尺，两头各带一个直径十五英尺的圆球，所有设备都分段组装，可以拆下来。我们的维护组乘一种类似小飞船的东西上去，要么穿着太空服在外面作业，要么换上新的分段，把坏的运回下面的车间。”

“啊，那么这种小飞船，如今又是个什么样的家伙？”

“就好比两辆五十吨的载重卡车，中间以气闸相连，腰部对接；当然是气密的，又像飞船一样增压、隔热。一侧是六人乘员组的生活舱——有时维护组出去一待就是整整一个星期——另一侧是工作间。”

这听起来很有意思。有了反重力，“逃逸速度”“质量比”之类的字眼，自然就纯属古董学的趣味了。

“要多久，”他问皮尔逊，“才能给那个载具装上一整套探测仪器——雷达、红外与紫外视觉仪、电子望远镜、热探测器和辐射探测器，全套家伙——并把它部署在基加克上空一百至一百五十英里处？”

===Original===
“That I couldn’t say, general,” Emmett Pearson replied. “It’d have to be a shipyard job, and a lot of that stuff’s clear outside my department. Ask Air-Commodore Hargreaves.”

“Les!” he called out. “Wake up, Les!”

“Just a second, general.” Hargreaves scribbled frantically on his pad. “Now,” he said, raising his head. “What is it, sir?”

“Emmett, here, has a junior-grade spaceship that he uses to service those orbital telecast-relay stations of his. He’ll tell you what it’s like. I want it fitted with every sort of detection device that can be crammed into or onto it, and spotted above Keegark. It should, of course, be high enough to cover not only the Keegark area, but Konkrook, Kankad’s, and the lower Hoork and Konk river-valleys.”

===Chinese===
“这我说不准，将军，”埃米特·皮尔逊答道。“那得进船厂改装，而且那些设备大多根本不归我这一行管。问空军准将哈格里夫斯吧。”

“莱斯！”他喊道。“醒一醒，莱斯！”

“稍等一秒，将军。”哈格里夫斯在便笺上发疯似的写着。“好了，”他抬起头说，“什么事，长官？”

“咱们这位埃米特有一艘低档小飞船，用来检修他那批轨道电视中继站。它什么样，他会告诉你。我要你给它装上塞得进、挂得上的每一种探测设备，部署在基加克上空。高度自然要够，不仅覆盖基加克地区，还要覆盖孔克鲁克、坎卡德城，以及霍尔克河（Hoork）与孔克河（Konk）的下游河谷。”

===Original===
“Yes, I get it.” Hargreaves snatched up a phone, punched out a combination, and began talking rapidly into it in a low voice. After a while, he hung up. “All right, Mr. Pearson—Colonel Pearson, I mean. Have your space-buggy sent around to the shipyard. My boys’ll fix it up.” He made a note on another piece of paper. “If we live through this, I’m going to have a couple of supra-atmosphere ships in service on this planet.… Now, general, I have a tentative setup. We’re going to need the *Elmoran* for patrol work south and east of Konkrook, and the *Gaucho* and *Bushranger* to the north and northeast, based on Kankad’s. We’ll keep the *Aldebaran* at Kankad’s, and use her for emergencies. And we’ll have patrols of light contragravity like this.” He handed a map, with red-pencil and blue-pencil markings, along to von Schlichten. “Red are Kankad-based; blue are Konkrook-based.”

===Chinese===
“明白，我懂了。”哈格里夫斯一把抓起电话，拨出一串号码，压低嗓门飞快地说起来。过了一会儿，他挂断电话。“好，皮尔逊先生——我是说皮尔逊上校。把你那辆太空小车送到船厂去。我的小伙子们会把它拾掇利索。”他在另一张纸条上记了一笔。“这次要是能活下来，我非给这颗行星配上几艘超大气层飞船不可。……好了将军，我有了个初步方案。「埃尔莫兰」号（Elmoran）用于孔克鲁克以南和以东的巡逻；「高乔」号（Gaucho）和「丛林游骑兵」号（Bushranger）部署北面和东北，以坎卡德城为基地。「毕宿五」号（Aldebaran）留守坎卡德城，专备应急。另外再像这样布轻型反重力巡逻。”他把一张用红蓝铅笔标过的地图递给冯·施利希滕。“红色以坎卡德城为基地，蓝色以孔克鲁克为基地。”

===Original===
“That looks all right,” von Schlichten said. “There’s another thing, though. We want scout-vehicles to cover the Keegark area with radiation-detectors. These geeks are quite well aware of radiation-danger from fissionables, but they’re accustomed to the ordinary industrial-power reactors, which are either very lightly shielded or unshielded on top. We want to find out where Orgzild’s bomb-plant is.”

“Yes, general, as soon as we can get radiation detectors sent out to Kankad’s, we’ll have a couple of fast aircars fitted with them for that job.”

“We have detectors, at our laboratory and reaction-plant,” Kankad said. “And my people can make more, as soon as you want them.” He thought for a moment. “Perhaps I should go to the town, now. I could be of more use there than here.”

Kent Pickering, who had been talking with his experts at a table apart, returned.

“We’ve set up a programme, general,” he said. “It’s going to be a lot harder than I’d anticipated. None of us seem to know exactly what we have to do in building one of those things. You see, the uranium or plutonium fission-bomb’s been obsolete for over four hundred years. It was a classified-secret matter long after its obsolescence, because it hadn’t been rendered any the less deadly by being superseded—there was that A-bomb that the Christian Anarchist Party put together at Buenos Aires in 378 AE, for instance. And then, after it was declassified, it had been so far superseded that it was of only antiquarian interest; the textbooks dealt with it only in general terms. The principles, of course, are part of basic nuclear science; the ‘secret of the A-bomb’ was just a bag of engineering tricks that we don’t have, and which we will have to rediscover. Design of tampers, design of the chemical-explosive charges to bring subcritical masses together, case-design, detonating mechanism, things like that.

===Chinese===
“看起来没问题，”冯·施利希滕说。“不过还有一事。我们要派侦察载具携辐射探测器覆盖基加克地区。这些土佬对裂变材料的辐射危险心里很有数，但他们见惯的是普通的工业动力堆——那种顶部要么屏蔽单薄、要么干脆不加屏蔽的反应堆。我们得查明奥格齐尔德的炸弹工厂在什么地方。”

“是，将军。辐射探测器一运到坎卡德城，我们就给两辆快速飞车装上，派去执行这项任务。”

“探测器，我们自己的实验室和反应堆那边就有，”坎卡德王说。“需要多少，我的人随时可以再造。”他沉吟片刻。“或许我现在该回城里去了。在那边，我比在这里更有用处。”

肯特·皮克林一直在旁边一张桌旁跟他的专家们商议，这时走了回来。

“方案已经定出来了，将军，”他说。“这可比我想象的艰难得多。我们这些人里，似乎没有一个确切知道造那种东西都要做些什么。要知道，铀或钚裂变弹已经淘汰四百多年了。在它落伍之后很久，它一直属于保密事项，因为被更新换代并不减损它分毫致命——比如原子纪元 378 年，基督教无政府党（Christian Anarchist Party）就在布宜诺斯艾利斯拼装过一颗。后来等它解密，它又被远远抛在后面，只剩了古董价值；教科书只用泛泛数语带过。原理嘛，当然属于核科学基础的一部分；所谓『原子弹的秘密』，不过是一整套我们没有掌握、必须重新发掘出来的工程诀窍。反射层的设计，把亚临界面块轰拢到一处的化学炸药件的设计，弹壳设计，起爆装置，诸如此类。

===Original===
“The complete data on even the old Hiroshima and Nagasaki types is still in existence, of course. You can get it at places like the University of Montevideo Library, or Jan Smuts Memorial Library at Cape Town. But we don’t have it here. We’re detailing a couple of junior technicians to make a search of the library here on Gongonk Island, but we’re not optimistic. We just can’t afford to pass up any chance, even when it approaches zero-probability.”

Von Schlichten nodded. “That’s about what I’d expected,” he said. “I suppose Gomes got his data out of one of the dustier storage-stacks at Jan Smuts or Montevideo, in the first place.… Well, I still want that bomb finished by yesterday afternoon, but since that’s impractical, you’ll have to take a little—but as little as possible—longer.”

“What are we going to do about publicity on this?” Howlett, the personnel man, asked. “We don’t want this getting out in garbled form—though how it could be made worse by garbling I couldn’t guess—and having the troops watching the sky over their shoulders and going into a panic as soon as they saw something they didn’t understand.”

“No, we don’t. I’ve seen a couple of troop-panics,” von Schlichten said. “There can’t be anything much worse than a panic.”

“I think the Terrans ought to be told the worst,” Hargreaves said. “And told that our only hope is to get a bomb of our own built and dropped first. As to the Kragans.… What do you think, King Kankad?”

===Chinese===
“当然，连老式的广岛型、长崎型的完整资料都还在世上。蒙得维的亚大学图书馆，或者开普敦的扬·斯穆茨纪念图书馆这类地方，都能查到。可我们这里没有。我们已派两名初级技术员去彻底搜一遍贡贡克岛上这座图书馆，但不抱乐观。只是任何机会我们都赔不起、一个也不能放过——哪怕它趋近于零的可能。”

冯·施利希滕点了点头。“和我预想的差不多，”他说。“我猜，戈麦斯当初就是从斯穆茨或者蒙得维的亚哪个积灰最厚的馆藏书堆里，把资料翻出来的。……好吧，我还是要那颗炸弹昨天下午完工；不过既然这不现实，你们就多花一点——但只是一点点——时间吧。”

“这件事对外怎么交代？”人事主任豪利特（Howlett）问道。“我们可不想让它走样流传出去——虽然我不明白走样之后还能糟到哪里去——也不想让部队一边打仗一边提防着脑后的天空，一见到什么看不懂的东西就恐慌发作。”

“对，不想。部队恐慌我见识过两回，”冯·施利希滕说。“天底下再没有比恐慌更糟的事了。”

“我认为，最坏的情况也应该告诉地球人，”哈格里夫斯说。“并且告诉他们，我们唯一的生路，就是抢先造出自己的炸弹、抢先投下。至于克拉冈人……您意下如何，坎卡德王？”

===Original===
“Tell them that we are building a bomb to destroy Keegark; that we are running short of ammunition, and that it is our only hope of finishing the war before the ammunition is gone,” Kankad said. “Tell them something of what sort of a bomb it is. But do not tell them that King Orgzild already has such a bomb. Old Kankad, who made me out of himself, told me about how our people fled in panic from the weapons of the Terrans, when your people and mine were still enemies. This thing is to the weapons they faced then as those weapons were to the old Kragans’ spears and bows.… And when the geeks from Grank come here, tell them that we are winning and that if they fight well, they can share the loot of Konkrook and Keegark.”

Von Schlichten looked up at the big screen. Already, Themistocles M’zangwe had ordered the Channel Battery to reduce fire; the big guns were firing singly, in thirty-second-interval salvos. There was less bombing, too; contragravity was being drawn out of the battle.

“Well, we all have things to do,” he said, “and I think we’ve discussed everything there is to discuss. Anybody think of anything we’ve forgotten?… Then we’re adjourned.”

He and Paula Quinton took the elevator to the roof, and sat side by side, silently watching the conflagration that was raging across the channel and the nearer flashes of the big guns along the island’s city side.

“Wednesday night, I thought we were all cooked,” Paula told him. “Cleaning up the north in two days seemed like an impossibility, too. Maybe you’ll do it again.”

===Chinese===
“告诉他们，我们正在造一颗炸毁基加克的炸弹；告诉他们，我们的弹药快用尽了，这是弹药耗尽之前结束战争的唯一希望，”坎卡德王说。“那是颗什么样的炸弹，也可以跟他们讲个大概。但不要告诉他们，奥格齐尔德王手里已经有了一颗这样的东西。由自身生育出我的那位老坎卡德（Old Kankad）告诉我，当年你们我两族还在互相为敌的时候，我们的族人如何在地球人的武器面前惊惶溃逃。这件东西之于他们当年面对的那些武器，恰如那些武器之于老一辈克拉冈人的长矛弓箭。……等格兰克（Grank）的土佬到了这边，就告诉他们：我们正在打胜仗；只要他们奋勇作战，孔克鲁克和基加克的战利品便有他们一份。”

冯·施利希滕抬头望向大屏幕。地米斯托克利·姆赞圭（Themistocles M’zangwe）已下令海峡炮台（Channel Battery）减缓火力；巨炮改为单发射击，每隔三十秒一轮齐射。轰炸也稀疏了；反重力兵力正陆续撤出战场。

“好了，大家手头都有事，”他说，“该议的我们也都议到了。还有谁想起什么漏掉的没有？……没有，那就散会。”

他和保拉·昆顿（Paula Quinton）乘电梯上了屋顶，并肩坐下，默默望着海峡对岸熊熊燃烧的大火，望着岛上市区一侧较近处大炮迸出的闪光。

“星期三夜里，我还以为我们全都在劫难逃，”保拉对他说。“两天之内扫平北方，当时看来同样是不可能的事。也许这一回，你能再做到一次。”

===Original===
“If I pull this one out of the fire, I won’t be a general; I’ll be a magician,” he said. “Pickering’ll be a magician, I mean; he’s the boy who’ll save our bacon, if it’s saveable.” He looked somberly across the flame-reflecting water. “Let’s not kid ourselves; we’re just kicking and biting at the guards on the way up the gallows-steps.”

“Well, why stop till the trap’s sprung?” she asked. “What’ll happen to these people on this planet, after we’re atomized?”

“That I don’t want to think about. Kankad’s Town will get the second bomb; Orgzild won’t dare leave the Kragans after he’s wiped us out. Yoorkerk and Jonkvank, in the north, will turn on Keaveney and Shapiro and Karamessinis and Hid O’Leary and wipe them out. And when the next ship gets in here and they find out what happened, they’ll send the Federation Space Navy, and this planet’ll get it worse than Fenris did. They’ll blast anything that has four arms and a face like a lizard.…”

Half a dozen aircars lifted suddenly from the airport and streaked away to the northeast. As they went past, in the light of the burning city, he could see that at least three of them had multiple rocket-launchers on top. In a matter of seconds, a gun-cutter raced after them, and a second, which had been over Konkrook, jettisoned a bomb and turned away to follow.

“Maybe that’s it,” Paula said.

===Chinese===
“要是我这回真能火中取栗，那我就不是将军，是魔术师了，”他说。“确切说，皮克林才是魔术师；要是咱们这几条命救得回来，救命的正是那小子。”他神色阴郁地望过映着火光的水面。“咱们别自欺欺人了；我们不过是走上绞刑台阶的时候，冲着押送的卫兵又踢又咬罢了。”

“那为什么不等陷阱弹开再停手呢？”她问。“等我们化为一团原子之后，这颗行星上的人会落得什么下场？”

“那个，我不愿去想。第二颗炸弹会落到坎卡德城；把我们斩尽杀绝之后，奥格齐尔德绝不敢留下克拉冈人。北方的约克克（Yoorkerk）和琼克万克（Jonkvank）会反手扑向基夫尼（Keaveney）、夏皮罗（Shapiro）、卡拉梅西尼斯（Karamessinis）和希德·奥利里（Hid O’Leary），把他们斩尽杀绝。等下一艘船进港、查明这里发生的一切，他们就会派出联邦太空海军（Federation Space Navy），这颗行星的下场会比芬里斯（Fenris）更惨。凡是长着四条胳膊、一张蜥蜴脸的家伙，他们都要轰个精光。……”

六辆飞车（aircar）蓦地从机场升起，朝东北方向疾掠而去。它们掠过时，借着燃烧城市的火光，他看得出其中至少三辆顶部装着多联装火箭发射器。只几秒钟工夫，一艘炮艇（gun-cutter）疾追上去；原先在孔克鲁克上空的另一艘炮艇抛下炸弹，转向跟了上去。

“也许就是它了，”保拉说。

===Original===
“Well, if it is, we won’t be any better off anywhere else than here,” he told her. “Let’s stay and watch.”

After what seemed like a long time, however, a twinkle of lights showed over the East Konk Mountains. They weren’t the flashes of explosions; some were magnesium flares, and some were the lights of a ship.

“That’s *Procyon*, from Grank,” he said. “Everybody gets a good mark for this—detection stations, interceptors, gun-cutters. If that had been it, there’d have been a good chance of stopping it.” He felt better than he had since Pickering had told him that Lourenço Gomes was dead. “It’s a good thing Gorkrink didn’t pick up any dope on guided missiles, while he was at it. As long as they have to deliver it with contragravity, we have a chance.”

They rose from the balustrade where they had been sitting, and, for the first time, he discovered that he had had his left arm over her shoulder and that she had had her right hand resting on the point of his right hip, just above his pistol. He picked up the folder of papers she had been carrying, and put her into the elevator ahead of him, and it was only when they parted on the living-quarters level that he recalled having followed the older protocol of gallantry rather than the precedence of military rank.

===Chinese===
“嗯，若是它，我们躲到别的什么地方去也不会更安全，”他对她说。“就留在这儿看着吧。”

然而，过了似乎很长的时间，东孔克山脉（East Konk Mountains）上空才亮起一片闪烁的光点。那不是爆炸的闪光；有的是镁光照明弹，有的是一艘船的航行灯。

“是「南河三」号，从格兰克来的，”他说。这一回人人都该记一功——探测站、拦截机、炮艇。方才来的若是真家伙，倒大有希望把它拦下来。自打皮克林告诉他洛伦索·戈麦斯（Lourenço Gomes）死了之后，他心里头一回松快了些。“幸亏戈克林克顺手没有捎带弄到导弹方面的门道。只要他们只能靠反重力载具投弹，我们就还有一线机会。”

两人从坐着的矮栏上起身，他这才头一回发觉：自己的左臂一直搂着她的肩，她的右手一直搭在他右侧胯骨尖上，不偏不倚正在手枪上方。他拾起她一直夹着的文件夹，让她先一步进了电梯；直到在住舱层分手，他才省悟，自己方才遵循的是古老的骑士献殷勤的老礼数，而非军衔高下的规程。

## XV A Place in My Heart for Hildegarde / 第十五章 我心中永远给希尔德加德留着一个位置

===Original===
The company fleet hung off Keegark, at fifteen thousand feet, in a belt of calm air just below the seesawing currents from the warming Antarctic and the cooling deserts of the Arctic. There was the *Procyon*, from the bridge of which von Schlichten watched the movements of the other ships and airboats and the distant horizon. The *Aldebaran* was ten miles off, to the west, her metal sheathing glinting in the red light of the evening sun. There was the *Northern Star*, down from Skilk, a smaller and more distant twinkle of reflected light to the north of *Aldebaran*. The *Northern Lights* was off to the east, and between her and *Procyon* was a fifth ship; turning the arm-mounted binoculars around, he could just make out, on her bow, the figurehead bust of a man in an ancient tophat and a fringe of chin-beard. She was the *Oom Paul Kruger*, captured by the *Procyon* after a chase across the mountains northeast of Keegark the day before. And, remote from the other ships, to the south, a tiny speck of blue-gray, almost invisible against the sky, and a smaller twinkle of reflected sunlight—a garbage-scow, unflatteringly but somewhat aptly rechristened *Hildegarde Hernandez*, which had been altered as a bomb-carrier, and the gun-cutter *Elmoran*. With the glasses, he could see a bulky cylinder being handled off the scow and loaded onto the improvised bomb-catapult on the *Elmoran*’s stern. Shortly thereafter, the gun-cutter broke loose from the tender and began to approach the fleet.

“General, I must protest against your doing this,” Air-Commodore Hargreaves said. “There’s simply no sense in it. That bomb can be dropped without your personal supervision aboard, sir, and you’re endangering yourself unnecessarily. That infernal machine hasn’t been tested or anything; it might even let go on the catapult when you try to drop it. And we simply can’t afford to lose you, now.”

“No, what would become of us, if you go out there and blow yourself up with that contraption?” Buhrmann supported him. “My God, I thought Don Quixote was a Spaniard, instead of a German!”

“Argentino,” von Schlichten corrected. “And don't try to sell me that Irreplaceable Man line, either. Them M'zangwe can replace me, Hid O'Leary can replace him, Barney Mordkovitz can replace him, and so on down to where you make a second lieutenant out of some sergeant. We've been all over this last evening. Admitted we can't take time for a long string of test-shots, and admitted we have to use an untested weapon; I'm not sending men out under those circumstances and staying here on this ship and watch them blow themselves up. If that bomb's our only hope, it's got to be dropped right, and I'm not going to take a chance on having it dropped by a crew who think they've been sent out on a suicide mission. What happened to the *Gaucho* when she blew the *Smuts* up is too fresh in everybody's mind. But if I, who ordered the mission, accompany it, they'll know I have some confidence that they'll come back alive.”

===Chinese===
公司舰队悬停在基加克（Keegark）外海上空一万五千英尺处，占据着一层平静气流带，恰在从回暖的南极与转凉的北极荒漠涌来、来回拉锯的气流之下。「南河三」号（Procyon）便在其中，冯·施利希滕（von Schlichten）立在舰桥上，望着其他舰船和飞艇（airboat）的调动，望着遥远的地平线。「毕宿五」号（Aldebaran）在西面十英里外，金属蒙皮在夕照的红光里闪闪烁烁。「北极星」号（Northern Star）刚从斯基尔克（Skilk）南来，在「毕宿五」号以北，是一点更小、更遥远的反光。「北极光」号（Northern Lights）在东面；在它与「南河三」号之间还有第五艘船——他转动手臂上架着的双筒望远镜，才勉强辨认出它船首那尊半身船首像：一个戴古式大礼帽、下巴蓄着一圈胡须的男人。那是「奥姆·保罗·克鲁格」号（Oom Paul Kruger），前一天被「南河三」号追过基加克东北的群山之后俘获。再往南，远离诸舰之处，还有一小点蓝灰色，衬着天色几乎看不见，旁边一星更小的反光——那是一艘垃圾驳船，被人不无刻薄却也颇为贴切地重新命名为「希尔德加德·埃尔南德斯」号（Hildegarde Hernandez），已改装成运弹船；旁边是炮艇「埃尔莫兰」号（Elmoran）。透过望远镜，他看得见一个粗大的圆筒正从驳船上吊运下来，装上「埃尔莫兰」号船尾那具临时赶制的炸弹弹射器。不多时，炮艇脱离这艘补给船，向舰队驶来。

“将军，我必须抗议您这么做，”空军准将哈格里夫斯（Air-Commodore Hargreaves）说。“这简直毫无道理。那颗炸弹用不着您亲自登艇监督投放，长官，您这是在无谓地拿自己冒险。那台要命的机器没经过任何测试什么的；说不定您要投它的时候，它就在弹射器上先炸了。而眼下，我们实在损失不起您。”

“可不是嘛，您要是飞到那儿去，让那玩意儿把自己炸上了天，我们可怎么办？”布尔曼（Buhrmann）帮腔道。“我的天，我还以为堂吉诃德是西班牙人，不是德国人呢！”

“是阿根廷人（Argentino），”冯·施利希滕纠正道。“还有，也别拿『不可替代之人』那套说辞来劝我。地米·姆赞圭（Them M'zangwe）可以顶替我，希德·奥利里（Hid O'Leary）可以顶替他，巴尼·莫德科维茨（Barney Mordkovitz）可以顶替他，如此层层顺延，直到从哪个军士里提拔出一个少尉来。这些话我们昨晚都说尽了。就算我们抽不出时间搞一连串试射，就算我们只能动用一件未经测试的武器，我也不能在这种情形下把手下人派出去，自己却待在这艘舰上，眼看他们把自己炸上天。那颗炸弹既然是我们唯一的指望，就必须投得准，我可不打算赌运气，把它交给一支自以为奉命去送死的机组。当初「高乔」号（Gaucho）炸掉「扬·斯穆茨」号时出了什么事，人人都还记忆犹新。可要是下令执行这次任务的我亲自随行，他们就知道，我有几分把握他们能活着回来。”

===Original===
“Well I'm coming along, too, general,” Kent Pickering spoke up. “I made the damned thing, and I ought to be along when it's dropped, on the principle that a restaurant-proprietor ought to be seen eating his own food once in a while.”

“I still don't see why we couldn't have made at least one test shot, first,” Hans Meyerstein, the Banking Cartel man, objected.

“Well, I'll tell you why,” Paula Quinton spoke up. “There's a good chance that the geeks don't know we have a bomb of our own. They may believe that it was something invented on Niflheim for mining purposes, and that we haven't realized its military application. There's more than a good chance that the loss of the *Jan Smuts* has temporarily demoralized them. Personally, I believe that both King Orgzild and Prince Gorkrink were aboard her when she blew up. That's something we'll never know, positively, of course. That ship and everything and everybody in her were simply vaporized, and the particles are registering on our geigers now. But I'm as sure as I am of anything about these geeks that one or both of them accompanied her.”

“Paula knows what she's talking about,” King Kankad jabbered in the Takkad Sea language which they all understood. “Just like Von saying that he has to go on our cutter, to encourage the crew. They always insist that their kings and generals go into battle, particularly if something important is to be done. They think the gods get angry if they don't.”

“And we have to hit them now,” von Schlichten said. “They still have a couple of bombs left. We haven't been able to locate them with detectors, but those geeks Kankad's men caught on that commando-raid, last night, say that there were at least three of them made. We can't take a chance that some fanatic may load one into an aircar and make a kamikaze-raid on Gongonk Island.”

===Chinese===
“我也一起去，将军，”肯特·皮克林（Kent Pickering）开口道。“那玩意儿是我造的，投放的时候我就该在场——按的是这么个原则：饭店老板也该偶尔让人看见吃自家的饭菜。”

“我还是不明白，我们为什么事先连一次试射都做不到，”银行卡特尔（Banking Cartel）方面的汉斯·迈耶斯坦（Hans Meyerstein）反对道。

“好吧，我来告诉你为什么，”保拉·昆顿（Paula Quinton）开了口。“很有一种可能：这些土佬（geeks）根本不知道我们也有自己的炸弹。他们也许以为那不过是尼福尔海姆（Niflheim）方面为采矿目的发明的东西，我们并没有意识到它的军事用途。「扬·斯穆茨」号的损失，十之八九已让他们一时丧胆。就我个人而言，我相信船爆炸时，奥格齐尔德王（King Orgzild）和戈克林克亲王（Prince Gorkrink）都在船上。当然，这一点我们永远无法确知。那条船连同船上的一切、一切人都化成了气体，那些微粒此刻还在我们的盖革计数器上留读数呢。不过，关于这些土佬，我别的都不敢说，这一点却敢断定：他们两个之中至少有一个随船同殒。”

“保拉说的在理，”坎卡德王（King Kankad）用大家都听得懂的塔卡德海（Takkad Sea）土语叽里呱啦地说。“这就像冯非说不可、要上我们的炮艇去给艇员鼓劲，一个道理。他们地球人素来坚持，他们的国王和将军非上阵打仗不可，尤其是有大事要办的时候。他们以为不这样，神明就要发怒。”

“而且我们必须现在就动手，”冯·施利希滕说。“他们手里还剩两颗炸弹。我们用探测器一直没能定位，可昨晚坎卡德的人搞突击侦察抓来的那些土佬招供说，那种炸弹至少造了三颗。万一哪个狂热分子把一颗装上飞车，对贡贡克岛（Gongonk Island）来一次神风式的自杀袭击，这个险我们冒不起。”

===Original===
The *Elmoran* ran alongside, with her Maasai-warrior figurehead and the black cylinder on her catapult aft. Somebody had painted, on the bomb: **Dire Dawn** by Hildegarde Hernandez. Compliments of the author to H.M. King Orgzild of Keegark. A canvas-entubed gangway was run out to connect the ship with the cutter. Von Schlichten and Kent Pickering went down the ladder from the bridge, the others accompanying them. As he stepped into the gangway, Paula Quinton fell in behind him.

“Where do you think you're going?” he demanded.

“Along with you,” she replied. “I'm your adjutant, I believe.”

“You definitely are not going along. Personally, I don't believe there's any danger, but I'm not having you run any unnecessary risks. …”

===Chinese===
「埃尔莫兰」号靠了上来，船首立着马赛（Maasai）武士的雕饰，船尾弹射器上架着那个黑色圆筒。有人在炸弹上漆了字：**《凶险的黎明》**（**Dire Dawn**），希尔德加德·埃尔南德斯著。作者敬赠基加克国王奥格齐尔德陛下。一条帆布筒舷梯搭了出来，把军舰和炮艇连在一起。冯·施利希滕和肯特·皮克林从舰桥的舷梯下来，其余人陪着。他刚跨进舷梯，保拉·昆顿便跟在了他后面。

“你这是要上哪儿去？”他质问道。

“跟您一起去，”她答道。“我想，我是您的副官。”

“你绝对不能去。就我个人而言，我不信这有什么危险，可我不能让你去冒任何不必要的险。……”

===Original===
“Von, I don't know much about the way Terrans think, except about fighting and about making things,” Kankad told him. “And I don't know anything at all about the kind of Terrans who have young. But I believe this is something important to Paula. Let her go with you, because if you go alone and don't come back, I don't think she will ever be happy again.”

He looked at Kankad curiously, wondering, as he had so often before, just what went on inside that lizard-skull. Then he looked at Paula, and, after a moment, he nodded.

“All right, colonel, objection withdrawn,” he said.

===Chinese===
“冯，地球人怎么想事情，我不大懂，只懂打仗和造东西那一路，”坎卡德对他说。“至于会生养后代的那种地球人，我就更一窍不通了。可我相信，这件事对保拉非同小可。让她跟你去吧。你要是独自去了回不来，我看她往后再也不会快活了。”

他好奇地打量坎卡德，和从前无数次一样，纳闷那颗蜥蜴脑壳里究竟在转些什么念头。然后他望向保拉，片刻之后，点了点头。

“好吧，上校，异议撤回，”他说。

===Original===
Aboard the *Elmoran*, they gave the bomb a last-minute inspection and checked the catapult and the bombsight, and then went up on the bridge.

“Ready for the bombing mission, sir?” the skipper, a Lieutenant (j.g.) Morrison, asked.

“Ready if you are, lieutenant. Carry on; we're just passengers.”

“Thank you, sir. We'd thought of going in over the city at about five thousand for a target-check, turning when we're halfway back to the mountains, and coming back for our bombing-run at fifteen thousand. Is that all right, sir?”

===Chinese===
登上「埃尔莫兰」号后，他们对炸弹做了临投前的最后检查，校验了弹射器和轰炸瞄准具，然后上了舰桥。

“轰炸任务准备停当了吗，长官？”艇长、海军中尉莫里森（Lieutenant (j.g.) Morrison）问道。

“你们就绪我就绪，中尉。只管继续；我们不过是搭船的。”

“谢谢长官。我们想的是：先以五千英尺左右的高度飞临城市上空核对目标，飞到半途、快到山区时转弯，再以一万五千英尺的高度进入轰炸航路。这样可以吗，长官？”

===Original===
Von Schlichten nodded. “You're the skipper, lieutenant. You'd better make sure, though, that as soon as the bomb-off signal is flashed, your engineer hits his auxiliary rocket-propulsion button. We want to be about fifteen miles from where that thing goes off.”

The lieutenant (j.g.) muttered something that sounded unmilitarily like, “You ain't foolin', brother!”

“No, I'm not,” von Schlichten agreed. “I saw the *Jan Smuts* on the TV-screen.”

===Chinese===
冯·施利希滕点了点头。“你是艇长，中尉。不过你务必办妥一件事：炸弹投出信号一闪，你的轮机员就得按下辅助火箭推进按钮。那玩意儿炸响的时候，我们要在十五英里开外。”

这位海军中尉咕哝了一句听着很不军人的话，好像是：“这可不是闹着玩的，老兄！”

“我确实没哄你，”冯·施利希滕应道。“「扬·斯穆茨」号炸掉时的情形，我在电视屏幕上亲眼见过。”

===Original===
The *Elmoran* pointed her bow, and the long blade of the figurehead warrior's spear, toward Keegark. The city grew out of the ground-mist, a parti-colored blur at the delta of the dry Hoork River, and then a color-splashed triangle between the river and the bay and the hills on the landward side, and then it took shape, cross-ruled with streets and granulated with buildings. As they came in, von Schlichten, who had approached it from the air many times before, could distinguish the landmarks—the site of King Orgzild's nitroglycerin plant, now a crater surrounded by a quarter-mile radius of ruins; the Residency, another crater since Rodolfo MacKinnon had blown it up under him; the smashed *Christiaan De Wett* at the Company docks; King Orgzild's Palace, fire-stained and with a hole blown in one corner by the *Aldebaran*'s bombs. … Then they were past the city and over open country.

“I wish we had some idea where the rest of those bombs are stored, sir,” Lieutenant Morrison said. “We don't seem to have gotten anything significant when we flew reconnaissance with the radiation detectors.”

“No, about all that was picked up was the main power-plant, and the radiation-escape from there was normal,” Pickering agreed. “The bombs themselves wouldn't be detectable, except to the extent that, say, a nuclear-conversion engine for an airboat would be. They probably have them underground, somewhere, well shielded.”

“Those prisoners Kankad's commandos dragged in only knew that they were in the city somewhere,” von Schlichten considered. “How about midway between the Palace and the Residency for our ground-zero, lieutenant? That looks like the center of the city.”

===Chinese===
「埃尔莫兰」号将船头——连同船首像武士手中长矛的那道长刃——指向基加克。城市从地面雾霭中生长出来：先是干涸的霍尔克河（Hoork River）三角洲上一团五颜六色的模糊，继而成为河流、海湾与内陆侧群山之间一块泼满色彩的三角形，随后显出了形状，街道纵横如格线，建筑物细密如颗粒。临近时，曾多次从空中接近此城的冯·施利希滕辨认得出各处地标——奥格齐尔德王的硝化甘油厂旧址，如今成了一个弹坑，四周环绕着半径四分之一英里的废墟；总督府（Residency），自罗多尔福·麦金农（Rodolfo MacKinnon）把它连自己一同炸上天之后，也成了又一个弹坑；公司船坞里被打烂的「克里斯蒂安·德韦特」号（Christiaan De Wett）；奥格齐尔德王的王宫，带着火燎的污痕，一角被「毕宿五」号的炸弹炸出一个窟窿。……随后他们飞过城市，来到旷野上空。

“真希望我们能知道其余那几颗炸弹藏在哪儿，长官，”莫里森中尉说。“我们挂着辐射探测器飞侦察的时候，似乎没有收获任何要紧的迹象。”

“是啊，探测到的东西差不多就只有主发电厂，那里的辐射泄漏也属正常，”皮克林表示同意。“炸弹本身是探测不出来的，除非它有点像飞艇的核转换发动机那样的外泄。他们多半把炸弹藏在地下什么地方，屏蔽得严严实实。”

“坎卡德的突击队拖回来的那些俘虏，只晓得炸弹就在城里什么地方，”冯·施利希滕斟酌道。“咱们的爆心投影点定在王宫和总督府的正中间怎么样，中尉？那地方看着像全城的正中心。”

===Original===
The cutter turned and started back, having risen another ten thousand feet. Morrison passed the word to the bombardier. The city, with the sea beyond it now, came rushing at them, and von Schlichten, standing at the front of the bridge, discovered that he had his arm around Paula's waist and was holding her a little more closely than was military. He made no attempt to release her, however.

“There's nothing to worry about, really,” he was assuring her. “Pickering's boys built this thing according to the best principles of engineering, and the stuff they got out of that big-economy-size shilling-shocker all checked mathematically. …”

The red light on the bridge flashed, and the intercom shouted, “*Bomb off!*” He forced Paula down on the bridge deck and crouched beside her.

“Cover your eyes,” he warned. “You remember what the flash was like in the screen when the *Jan Smuts* blew up. And we didn't get the worst of it; the pickup on the *Gaucho* was knocked out too soon.”

He kept on lecturing her about gamma-rays and ultraviolet rays and X-rays and cosmic rays, trying to keep making some sort of intelligent sounds while they clung together and waited, and, with the other half of his mind, trying not to think of everything that could go wrong with that jerry-built improvisation they had just dumped onto Keegark. If it didn't blow, and the geeks found it, they'd know that another one would be along shortly, and. …

===Chinese===
炮艇又爬升了一万英尺，转身折返。莫里森把话传给投弹手。城市连同它身后的海洋迎面扑来；站在舰桥前端的冯·施利希滕发觉，自己的手臂已搂住保拉的腰，搂得比军纪容许的略紧了些。不过他丝毫没有松开的意思。

“真的没什么可担心的，”他一个劲儿地向她保证。“皮克林的小伙子们照最优良的工程原理造出了这东西，从那件『特大号廉价惊险小说』（big-economy-size shilling-shocker）里弄出来的东西，数学上也全部核对无误。……”

舰桥上的红灯一闪，舰内通话器大喝一声：“炸弹投出！”他把保拉按倒在舰桥甲板上，伏在她身边。

“捂住眼睛，”他提醒道。“你总记得「扬·斯穆茨」号炸掉时，屏幕上那道闪光是什么模样。可我们看到的还不是最厉害的；「高乔」号上的摄像机没过多久就被震坏了。”

他絮絮不休地给她讲伽马射线、紫外线、X 射线和宇宙射线，两人相拥等待的当口，他竭力让嘴里持续发出些像样的聪明声响，同时用另一半心思，竭力不去想他们刚扔到基加克上去的那件东拼西凑的急就章可能出的种种岔子。万一它不炸，又被土佬们找到了，他们就会明白，第二颗马上就要到，而且……

===Original===
An invisible hand caught the gun-cutter and hurled her end-over-end, sending von Schlichten and Paula sprawling at full length on the deck, still clinging to one another. There was a blast of almost palpable sound, and a sensation of heat that penetrated even the airtight superstructure of the *Elmoran*. An instant later, there was another, and another, similar shock. Two more bombs had gone off behind them, in Keegark; that meant that they had found King Orgzild's remaining nuclear armament. There were shattering sounds of breaking glass, and heavy thumps that told of structural damage to the cutter, and hoarse shouts, and lurid cursing as Morrison and his airmen struggled with the controls. The cutter began losing altitude, but she was back on a reasonably even keel. Von Schlichten rose, helping Paula to her feet, and found that they had been kissing one another passionately. They were still in each other's arms when the pitching and rolling of the cutter ceased and somebody tapped him on the shoulder.

He came out of the embrace and looked around. It was Lieutenant (j.g.) Morrison.

“What the devil, lieutenant?” he demanded.

“Sorry to interrupt, sir, but we're starting back to *Procyon*. And here, you'll want this, I suppose.” He held out a glass disc. “I never expected to see it, but at that it took three A-bombs to blow you loose from your monocle.”

“Oh, that?” Von Schlichten took his trademark and set it in his eye. “I didn't lose it,” he lied. “I just jettisoned it. Don't you know, lieutenant, that no gentleman ever wears a monocle while he's kissing a lady?”

===Chinese===
一只无形的巨手攫住炮艇，把它掀得团团翻滚，冯·施利希滕和保拉被摔得直挺挺趴在甲板上，仍紧紧抱着彼此。一声几乎摸得着的爆响炸开，一股热浪袭来，连「埃尔莫兰」号密闭的上层结构都挡它不住。转眼间，又一次冲击，再一次冲击，接连而至。又有两颗炸弹在他们身后的基加克炸了；这就是说，他们这一炸，正端掉了奥格齐尔德王余下的核武库。玻璃碎裂声四处炸响，沉闷的重击声诉说着炮艇结构受损，嘶哑的喊声，不堪入耳的咒骂，莫里森和他的艇员们拼力与操纵系统搏斗。炮艇开始掉高度，但总算恢复了大体平稳。冯·施利希滕站起身，扶保拉起来，才发现两人方才一直在热烈地亲吻。炮艇的颠簸摇晃止住时，两人还在彼此怀里，这时有人拍了拍他的肩膀。

他从拥抱中脱身，环顾四周。是海军中尉莫里森。

“搞什么名堂，中尉？”他质问道。

“抱歉打搅，长官，我们这就转回「南河三」号了。还有，这个您多半要吧。”他递过来一枚玻璃圆片。“我原本以为再也见不着它了——不过话说回来，居然得动用三颗原子弹，才能把您的单片眼镜从脸上震落。”

“哦，这个？”冯·施利希滕接过他的招牌标志，安进眼窝。“我没丢它，”他撒谎道。“我只是把它抛弃了。中尉，难道您不知道？绅士亲吻淑女的时候，从来不戴单片眼镜。”

===Original===
He looked around. They were at about eight hundred to a thousand feet above the water, with a stiff following wind away from the explosion area. The 90 mm gun, forward, must have been knocked loose and carried away; it was gone, and so was the TV-pickup and the radar. Something, probably the gun, had slammed against the front of the bridge—the metal skeleton was bent in, and the armor-glass had been knocked out. The cutter was vibrating properly, so the contragravity-field had not been disturbed, and her jets were firing.

“It was the second and third bombs that did the damage, sir,” Morrison was saying. “We'd have gone through the effects of our own bomb with nothing more than a bad shaking—of course, on contragravity, we're weightless relative to the air-mass, but she was built to stand the winds in the high latitudes. But the two geek bombs caught us off balance. …”

“You don't need to apologize, lieutenant. You and your crew behaved splendidly, lieutenant-commander, best traditions, and all that sort of thing. It was a pleasure, commander, hope to be aboard with you again, captain.”

===Chinese===
他环顾四周。他们处在离水面约八百到一千英尺的高度，一股劲风从爆炸区方向顺尾推送，让他们远远离开。舰首那门 90 毫米炮想必是被震松掀走了；它没了，电视摄像装置和雷达也没了。有什么东西，多半正是那门炮，狠狠砸在舰桥正面——金属骨架撞得凹陷进去，防弹玻璃也砸飞了。炮艇震动如常，可见反重力场没有受到扰动，喷气引擎也在正常喷发。

“肇祸的是第二颗和第三颗炸弹，长官，”莫里森正说着。“若只挨我们自己那颗炸弹的效应，无非狠狠震一震罢了——当然，靠反重力悬停时，我们相对于气团是失重的，何况这艇本来就造得扛得住高纬度的狂风。可土佬那两颗炸弹打了我们个措手不及。……”

“用不着道歉，中尉。你和你的人员表现出色，少校，尽显最佳传统，如此等等。同船实为乐事，中校，盼日后再度搭乘，上校。”

===Original===
They found Kent Pickering at the rear of the bridge, and joined him looking astern. Even von Schlichten, who had seen H-bombs and Bethe-cycle bombs, was impressed. Keegark was completely obliterated under an outward-rolling cloud of smoke and dust that spread out for five miles at the bottom of the towering column.

There had been a hundred and fifty thousand people in that city, even if their faces were the faces of lizards and they had four arms and quartz-speckled skins. What fraction of them were now alive, he could not guess. He had to remind himself that they were the people who had burned Eric Blount and Hendrik Lemoyne alive; that two of the three bombs that had contributed to that column of boiling smoke had been made in Keegark, by Keegarkans, and that, with a few causal factors altered, he was seeing what would have happened to Konkrook. Perhaps every Terran felt a superstitious dread of nuclear energy turned to the purposes of war; small wonder, after what they had done on their own world.

For one thing, he thought grimly, the next geek who picks up the idea of soaking a Terran in thermoconcentrate and setting fire to him will drop it again like a hot potato. And the next geek potentate who tries to organize an anti-Terran conspiracy, or the next crazy caravan-driver who preached *znidd suddabit*, will be lynched on the spot. But this must be the last nuclear bomb used on Uller. …

===Chinese===
他们在舰桥后部找到肯特·皮克林，和他一道朝船尾望去。连见识过氢弹和贝特循环炸弹（Bethe-cycle bomb）的冯·施利希滕，也为之动容。基加克已彻底湮没在一团向外翻滚的烟尘之下；烟尘在那根高耸烟柱的底部铺展开来，宽达五英里。

那座城里曾住着十五万人，纵然他们的脸是蜥蜴的脸，长着四条手臂，皮肤上嵌满石英斑点。其中眼下还有几分之一活着，他猜不出。他不得不提醒自己：正是这些人，把埃里克·布朗特（Eric Blount）和亨德里克·勒莫因（Hendrik Lemoyne）活活烧死；汇成那根沸腾烟柱的三颗炸弹中，有两颗正是基加克人（Keegarkans）在基加克造的；而只要改动几个偶然因素，他此刻所见的，本来就该是孔克鲁克（Konkrook）的下场。也许每一个地球人（Terran），对核能转用于战伐之事，都怀着一种近乎迷信的恐惧；想想他们在自己那个世界上干下的种种，这不足为怪。

首先，他冷峻地想道：下一个起了念头、要把地球人浸进热凝燃料（thermoconcentrate）里点火的土佬，准会像抓了烫手山芋一样，赶紧把这念头丢开。而下一个企图纠集反地球人阴谋的土佬君王，或是下一个狂热鼓吹*znidd suddabit*的疯癫商队车夫，都会被当场私刑处死。但是，这必须是乌勒尔（Uller）用掉的最后一颗核弹。……

===Original===
Drunkard's morning-after resolution! he told himself contemptuously. The next time, it will come easier, and easier still the time after that. After you drop the first bomb, there is no turning back, any more than there had been after Hiroshima, four-hundred-and-fifty-odd years ago. Why, he had even been considering just where, against the mountains back of Bwork, he would drop a demonstration bomb as a prelude to a surrender demand.

You either went on to the inevitable catastrophe, or you realized, in time, that nuclear armament and nationalism cannot exist together on the same planet, and it is easier to banish a habit of thought than a piece of knowledge. Uller was not ready for membership in the Terran Federation; then its people must bow to the Terran Pax. The Kragans would help—as proconsuls, administrators, now, instead of mercenaries. And there must be manned orbital stations, and the Residencies must be moved outside the cities, away from possible blast-areas. And Sid Harrington's idea of encouraging the natives to own their own contragravity-ships must be shelved, for a long time to come. Maybe, in a century or so. …

Kankad had a good idea, at that, a most meritorious idea. He was sold on it, already, and he doubted if it would take much salesmanship with Paula, either. Already, she was clinging to his arm with obvious possessiveness. Maybe their grandchildren, and the Kankad of that time, would see Uller a civilized member of the Federation. …

===Chinese===
酒鬼宿醉次晨的悔誓罢了！他鄙夷地对自己说。下一回，下手会更容易；再下一回，还要容易。投下第一颗炸弹之后，就没有回头路了——正如四百五十多年前广岛之后，从来没有过回头路。可不是么，他甚至已经在盘算，究竟要在布沃克（Bwork）背后山麓的哪一处，投下一颗示威炸弹，作为勒令投降的前奏。

你要么一路走向那不可避免的浩劫，要么及时醒悟：核武装与民族主义，无法在同一颗行星上并存；而革除一种思维习惯，总比抹掉一门知识容易。乌勒尔还没有资格加入地球联邦（Terran Federation）；那么它的子民就必须躬身服从地球治世（Terran Pax）。克拉冈人（Kragans）会出力相助——如今是以总督、行政官的身份，而非雇佣兵。还得设置有人驻守的轨道站；各地的总督府都必须迁出城外，躲开可能的爆轰区。至于席德·哈灵顿（Sid Harrington）那个鼓励土著拥有自己的反重力飞船的主意，则必须束之高阁，还要搁很久很久。也许，要等上一个世纪上下。……

话虽如此，坎卡德倒真出了个好主意，一个极其可嘉的主意。他自己已经全盘信服了，而且他怀疑，要说服保拉，也费不了多少推销的口舌。她已经带着明明白白的占有姿态，紧紧挽着他的手臂了。也许，他们的孙儿孙女，还有那时的坎卡德，将有幸看到乌勒尔成为联邦的一个文明成员。……

===Original===
They paused, as the gun-cutter nuzzled up to the *Procyon* and the canvas-entubed gangway was run out and made fast, looking back at the fearful thing that had sprouted from where Keegark had been.

“You know,” Paula was saying, echoing his earlier thought, “but for that female pornographer, that would have been Konkrook.”

He nodded. “Yes. I hope you won't mind, but there will always be a place in my heart for Hildegarde.”

Then they turned their backs upon the abomination of Keegark's desolation and went up the gangway together, looking very little like a general and his adjutant.

===Chinese===
炮艇挨上「南河三」号，帆布筒舷梯搭出、系牢，两人驻足回首，望着那件从基加克旧址上拔地而起的可怖之物。

“你知道吗，”保拉正说着，应和他先前的念头，“若不是那位女色情作家，那里本来会是孔克鲁克。”

他点了点头。“是啊。希望你别介意——我心中将永远给希尔德加德留着一个位置。”

随后，他们背对基加克废墟那可憎的惨状，一同登上舷梯——瞧那模样，实在不大像一位将军和他的副官了。

# VIII Authority of Governor-General von Schlichten / 第八章 冯·施利希滕总督的权威

===Original===
There was fresh intelligence from Konkrook, by the time he returned to the telecast station. Mutiny had broken out there among the laborers and native troops, who outnumbered the Terrans and their Kragan mercenaries on Gongonk Island by five thousand to five hundred and fifteen hundred respectively. The attempt to relieve Jaikark’s palace had been called off before the relief-force could be sent; there was heavy and confused fighting all over the island, and most of the combat contragravity and about half the Kragan Rifles had had to be committed to defend the Company farms across the Channel, on the mainland, south of the city. There had also been an urgent call for help from Colonel Rodolfo MacKinnon, in command of Company troops at the Keegark Residency, and another from the Residency at Kwurk, one of the Free Cities on the eastern shore of Takkad Sea.

He called Keegark; a girl, apparently one of the civilian telecast technicians, answered.

“We must have help, General von Schlichten,” she told him. “The native troops, all but two hundred Kragans, have mutinied. They have everything here except Company House—docks, airport, everything. We’re trying to hold out, but there are thousands of them. Our Takkad Native Infantry, soldiers of King Orgzild’s army, and townspeople. They all seem to have firearms.…”

“What happened to Eric Blount and your Resident-Agent, Mr. Lemoyne?”

“We don’t know. They were at the Palace, talking to King Orgzild. We’ve tried to call the Palace, but we can’t get through, general, we must have help.…”

===Chinese===
等他回到电视广播站时，孔克鲁克传来了新情报。那里的劳工和土著部队哗变了，哗变者多达五千之众，压过贡贡克岛上的地球人及其克拉冈（Kragan）雇佣兵——后者分别只有五百人和一千五百人。增援贾伊卡克（Jaikark）王宫的行动，还没等增援部队派出便告取消；全岛到处都在进行激烈而混乱的战斗，大部分战斗反重力载具和约半数的克拉冈来复枪团不得不投入海峡对岸、城南面大陆上公司农场的防守。基加克（Keegark）驻扎官府的公司部队指挥官罗道夫·麦金农（Rodolfo MacKinnon）上校发来紧急求援；塔卡德海东岸自由城邦之一的库尔克（Kwurk）的驻扎官府也来了第二份。

他接通基加克；接听的是个姑娘，看样子是平民电视技术员中的一个。

「我们必须得到支援，冯·施利希滕将军（General von Schlichten），」她对他说。「土著部队，除两百名克拉冈人外，全部哗变了。除了公司大楼，这里的一切都到了他们手里——码头、机场，一切的一切。我们正设法死守，可他们有几千人。有我们的塔卡德土著步兵，有奥格齐尔德王（Orgzild）军队的士兵，还有城里市民。他们似乎人人都有火器。……」

「埃里克·布朗特（Eric Blount）和你们的驻扎代理勒莫因（Lemoyne）先生出了什么事？」

「不知道。他们在王宫，正与奥格齐尔德王会谈。我们试过呼叫王宫，可打不通。将军，我们必须得到支援。……」

===Original===
A call came in, a few minutes later, from Krink, five hundred miles to the northeast across the mountains; the Resident-Agent there, one Francis Xavier Shapiro, reported rioting in the city and an attempted palace-revolution against King Jonkvank, and that the Residency was under attack. By way of variety, it was the army of King Jonkvank that had mutinied; the Sixth North Uller Native Infantry and the two companies of Zirk cavalry at Krink were still loyal, along with the Kragans.

There was a pattern to all this. Von Schlichten stood staring at the big map, on the wall, showing the Takkad Sea area at the Equatorial Zone, and the country north of it to the pole, the area of Uller occupied by the Company. He was almost beginning to discern the underlying logic of the past half-hour’s events when Keaveney, the Skilk Resident, blundered into him in a half-daze.

“Sorry, general, didn’t see you.” His face was ashen, and his jowls sagged. Von Schlichten wondered if there could be another spectacle so woebegone as a back-slapping extrovert with the bottom knocked out of him. “My God, it’s happening all over Uller! Not just here at Skilk; everywhere where we have a residency or a trading-station. Why, it’s the end of all of us!”

“It’s not quite that bad, Mr. Keaveney.” He looked at his watch. It was now nearly an hour since the native troops here at Skilk had mutinied. Insurrections like this usually succeeded or failed in the first hour. It was a little early to be certain, but he was beginning to suspect that this one hadn’t succeeded. “If we all do our part, we’ll come out of it all right,” he told Keaveney, more cheerfully than he felt, then turned to ask Brigadier-General Mordkovitz how the fighting was going at the native-troops barracks.

“Not badly, general. Colonel Jarman’s got some contragravity up and working. They blew out all four of the Tenth N.U.N.I.’s barracks; the Tenth and the Zirks are trying to defend the cavalry barracks. Some of our Kragans managed to slip around behind the cavalry stables. They’re leading out hipposaurs, and sniping at the rear of the cavalry barracks.”

===Chinese===
几分钟后又有来电，来自克林克（Krink）——隔着群山在东北方五百英里处。当地的驻扎代理、一个名叫弗朗西斯·泽维尔·夏皮罗（Francis Xavier Shapiro）的人报告：城里发生暴乱，有人企图发动宫廷政变推翻琼克万克王（Jonkvank），驻扎官府正遭攻击。换个花样的是，这次哗变的恰是琼克万克自己的军队；驻克林克的北乌勒尔土著步兵第六团（North Uller Native Infantry）和两连齐尔克（Zirk）骑兵倒同克拉冈人一道依然效忠。

这一切自有脉络可循。冯·施利希滕站在墙上那幅大地图前凝视：图上是赤道带的塔卡德海地区，及其向北直到极地的疆土——公司占领下的乌勒尔区域。他正要看出这半小时种种变故背后的逻辑，斯基尔克（Skilk）驻扎官基夫尼（Keaveney）晕晕乎乎地一头撞到了他身上。

「对不起，将军，没瞧见您。」他面如死灰，腮帮子耷拉着。冯·施利希滕暗想，一个见人就拍肩的热络外向汉子，一朝被人抽掉了底气，那副愁苦相，天底下怕是再找不出第二份。「天哪，整个乌勒尔都在出事！不光是我们斯基尔克；凡是我们设有驻扎官府或商站的地方，处处皆是。唉，这是我们大家的末日到了！」

「还没糟到那个地步，基夫尼先生。」他看了看表。斯基尔克这里土著部队哗变至今已近一个小时。这类起义，通常在头一个小时内便见分晓。此刻下断言还为时稍早，但他已开始怀疑这一次并未得手。「只要各人都尽本分，我们会平安过关的，」他对基夫尼说，口气比自己心里实际的感觉轻快些，随即转身去问准将莫德科维茨（Brigadier-General Mordkovitz），土著部队营房那边战况如何。

「不算坏，将军。贾曼（Jarman）上校已经让一些反重力载具升空作业。他们炸塌了第十北乌勒尔土著步兵团全部四座营房；第十团和齐尔克兵正试图守卫骑兵营房。我们有几名克拉冈人设法绕到了骑兵马厩后面。他们正往外牵河马龙（hipposaurs），朝骑兵营房后部放冷枪。」

===Original===
“That’ll give us some cavalry of our own; a lot of these Kragans are good riders. … How about the repair-shops and maintenance-yard and lorry-hangars? I don’t want these geeks getting hold of that equipment and using it against us.”

“Kormork’s outfit are trying to take back the lorry-hangars. Jarman’s got a couple of airjeeps and a combat-car helping them.”

“… won’t be one of us left by this time tomorrow,” Keaveney was wailing, to Paula Quinton and another woman. “And the Company is finished!”

“We’d better get him a drink, or a cup of coffee, general,” Mordkovitz suggested. “With a knockout-drop in it.”

Colonel Cheng-Li, the Intelligence officer, seemed to have somewhat the same idea. He approached Keaveney and tried to quiet him. At the same time, a woman in black slacks and an orange sweater—the one whose pursuers had been overrun by the Kragans at the beginning of the fighting—approached von Schlichten.

===Chinese===
「这样我们自己也就有骑兵了；这些克拉冈人里不少都是好骑手。……修理车间、维修场和卡车机库那边怎么样？我可不想让这帮土佬（geeks）把那些装备夺到手，掉过来打我们。」

「科尔莫克（Kormork）的人正在设法夺回卡车机库。贾曼派了两辆飞行吉普（airjeep）和一辆战斗车支援他们。」

「……到明天这个时候，我们一个也剩不下了，」基夫尼正对保拉·昆顿（Paula Quinton）和另一位妇人哭嚎。「公司也完蛋了！」

「将军，我们最好给他弄杯酒，或者一杯咖啡，」莫德科维茨建议道，「里头加一剂蒙汗药。」

情报军官郑立（Cheng-Li）上校似乎也打着差不多的主意。他走向基夫尼，想让他安静下来。与此同时，一个穿黑便裤和橙色毛衣的女人——就是开打之初追她的那伙人被克拉冈人冲垮的那位——朝冯·施利希滕走来。

===Original===
“General, King Kankad’s calling,” she said. “He’s on the screen in booth four.”

“Right.” To avoid any possibility of misunderstanding, he slipped his geek-speaker into his mouth before entering the booth. Kankad’s face was looking out of the screen at him, with Phil Yamazaki, the telecast operator at Kankad’s Town, standing behind him.

“Von!” The Kragan spoke almost as though in physical pain. “What can I do to help? I have twenty thousand of my people here who are capable of bearing arms, all with firearms, but I have transport for only five hundred. Where shall I send them?”

Von Schlichten thought quickly. Keegark was finished; the Residency stood in the middle of the city, surrounded by two hundred thousand of King Orgzild’s troops and subjects. Since Ullerans were bisexual, the total population, less the senile, crippled, and very young, was the military potential. Sending Kankad’s five hundred warriors and his meager contragravity there would be the same as shoveling them into a furnace. The people at Keegark would have to be written off, like the twenty Kragans at Jaikark’s palace.

“Send them to Konkrook,” he decided. “Them M’zangwe’s in command, there; he’ll need help to hold the Company farms. Maybe he can find additional transport for you. I’ll call him.”

===Chinese===
「将军，坎卡德王（King Kankad）来电，」她说，「他在四号通话间的屏幕上。」

「好。」为杜绝一切可能的理解偏差，进通话间之前，他先把土佬语发声器（geek-speaker）放进口中。坎卡德王的脸正从屏幕里望着他，坎卡德城的电视通话员菲尔·山崎（Phil Yamazaki）站在他身后。

「冯！」这位克拉冈人说话的样子，几乎像身上在疼。「我能帮上什么忙？我这里有两万名能够持械的族人，人人有枪，可运输工具只够载五百人。把他们派到哪里去？」

冯·施利希滕飞快地盘算。基加克是完了；驻扎官府矗立在城中央，四周是奥格齐尔德王二十万的军队和臣民。乌勒尔人雌雄同体，总人口除去老朽、残废和幼童，个个都是兵源。把坎卡德那五百战士和他那点单薄的反重力载具派到那里去，无异于把他们铲进一座熔炉。基加克的人只能放弃了，就像贾伊卡克王宫那二十名克拉冈人一样。

「派到孔克鲁克去，」他拍板道。「地米斯托克利·姆赞圭（Themistocles M’zangwe）在那里指挥；守住公司农场，他需要人手。也许他能为你们再腾出些运输工具。我这就给他去电话。」

===Original===
“I’ll send off what force I can, at once,” Kankad promised. “How does it go with you at Skilk?”

“We’re holding, so far,” he replied. “Paula is with me, here; she sends her friendship.”

Captain Inez Malavez, the woman officer in charge of the station, put her head into the booth.

“General! Immediate-urgency message from Colonel O’Leary,” she said. “Native laborers from the mine-labor camp are pouring into the mine-equipment park. Colonel O’Leary’s used all his rockets and M.G.-ammunition trying to stop them.”

“Call you back, later,” von Schlichten told Kankad. “I’ll see what Them M’zangwe can do about transport; get what force you can started for Konkrook at once.”

===Chinese===
「我立刻把能派出的部队派出去，」坎卡德允诺道。「你们斯基尔克那边打得怎么样？」

「到目前为止还守得住，」他答道。「保拉和我在一起；她向你致意。」

主管本站的女军官伊内丝·马拉维兹（Inez Malavez）上尉把头探进通话间。

「将军！奥利里（O’Leary）上校的特急电文，」她说。「矿工劳工营的土著劳工正涌向矿山设备场。奥利里上校的火箭弹和机枪弹药都打光了，也没能拦住他们。」

「回头再联系，」冯·施利希滕对坎卡德说。「我去看看地米·姆赞圭在运输上能想什么办法；你能凑出多少部队，立刻向孔克鲁克出发。」

===Original===
He left the booth, removing his geek-speaker. “Barney!” he called. “General Mordkovitz! Who’s the ranking officer in direct contact with the Eighteenth Rifles? Major Falkenberg?”

“That’s right.”

“Well, tell him to get as many of his Kragans as he can spare down to the equipment-park.” He turned to Inez Malavez. “You call Jarman; tell him what O’Leary reported, and tell him to get cracking on it. Tell him not to let those geeks get any of that equipment onto contragravity; knock it down as fast as they try to lift out with it. And tell him to see what he can do in the way of troop-carriers or lorries, to get Falkenberg’s Rifles to the equipment-park. … How’s business at the lorry-hangars and maintenance-yard?”

“Kormork’s still working on that,” the girl captain told him. “Nothing definite, yet.”

In one corner of the big room, somebody had thumbtacked a ten-foot-square map of the Company area to the floor. Paula Quinton and Mrs. Jules Keaveney were on their knees beside it, pushing out handfuls of little pink and white pills that somebody had brought in two bottles from the dispensary across the road, each using a billiard-bridge. The girl in the orange sweater had a handful of scribbled notes, and was telling them where to push the pills. There were other objects on the map, too—pistol-cartridges, and cigarettes, and foil-wrapped food-concentrate wafers. Paula, seeing him, straightened.

===Chinese===
他取下土佬语发声器，走出通话间。「巴尼（Barney）！」他喊道，「莫德科维茨将军！和第十八来复枪团直接保持联络的军衔最高的军官是谁？法尔肯贝格（Falkenberg）少校？」

「正是。」

「那就告诉他，把手头能抽出的克拉冈人尽量多派些到设备场去。」他转向伊内丝·马拉维兹。「你给贾曼打电话；把奥利里报告的情况告诉他，叫他立刻动手去办。告诉他绝不能让那帮土佬把任何设备弄上反重力载具；他们一载着东西试图起飞，就立刻把它打下来。再叫他设法弄些运兵车或卡车，好把法尔肯贝格的来复枪团送到设备场去。……卡车机库和维修场那边情况怎么样？」

「科尔莫克还在攻，」女上尉告诉他。「还没有确切消息。」

大房间的一角，有人用图钉把一幅十英尺见方的公司辖区地图钉在了地板上。保拉·昆顿和朱尔斯·基夫尼太太跪在图旁，往外推一把一把粉色和白色的小药丸——那是有人从马路对面的药房里拿来的两瓶药——两人各用一只台球架桥器（billiard-bridge）。穿橙色毛衣的姑娘手里攥着一把字迹潦草的纸条，正指点她们把药丸往哪里推。图上还摆着别的东西——手枪子弹、香烟，还有锡纸包装的浓缩食品块。保拉看见他，直起身来。

===Original===
“The pink are ours, general,” she said. “The white are the geeks.” Von Schlichten suppressed a grin; that was the second time he’d heard her use that word, this evening. “The cigarettes are airjeeps, the cartridges are combat-cars, and the wafers are lorries or troop-carriers.”

“Not exactly regulation map-markers, but I’ve seen stranger things used. … Captain Malavez!”

“Yes, sir?” The girl captain, rushing past, her hands full of teleprint-sheets, stopped in mid-stride.

“What we need,” he told her, “is a big TV-screen, and a pickup mounted on some sort of a contragravity vehicle at about two to five thousand feet directly overhead, to give us an image of the whole area. Can do?”

“Can try, sir. We have an eight-foot circular screen that ought to do all right for two thousand feet. I’ll implement that at once.”

===Chinese===
「粉色的是我们，将军，」她说，「白色的是土佬。」冯·施利希滕忍住没笑；今晚这已是他第二次听见她用这个词了。「香烟是飞行吉普，子弹是战斗车，食品块是卡车或运兵车。」

「算不上制式的地图标示物，不过我见过更古怪的用法。……马拉维兹上尉！」

「什么事，长官？」女上尉正匆匆经过，双手捧满电传纸页，闻声在半步之间收住脚。

「我们需要的，」他对她说，「是一块大电视屏幕，再在一台什么反重力载具上装个摄像机，悬停在我们头顶正上方约两千到五千英尺处，把整个区域的图像传给我们。办得到吗？」

「尽力试试，长官。我们有一块八英尺的圆形屏幕，两千英尺高度的图像应该够用了。我马上去办。」

===Original===
Going into a temporarily idle telecast booth, he called Konkrook. First he spoke to a civilian who chewed a dead cigar, and then he got Themistocles M’zangwe on the screen.

“How is it, now?” he asked.

“Getting a little better,” the Graeco-African replied. “Half an hour ago, we were shooting geeks out the windows, here; now we have them contained between the spaceport and the native-troops and labor barracks, and down the east side of the island to the farms. We have the wire around the farms on the island electrified, and we’re using almost all our combat contragravity to keep the farms on the mainland clear.” He hesitated for a moment. “Did you hear about Eric and Lemoyne?”

Von Schlichten shook his head.

“We just got a call from Rodolfo MacKinnon. He took a couple of prisoners and made them talk. The whole party that were at Orgzild’s palace were massacred. Some of them were lucky enough to get killed fighting. The geeks took Eric and Hendrik alive; rolled them in a puddle of thermoconcentrate fuel and set fire to them. When we can spare the contragravity, we’re going to drop something on the Kee-geek embassy, over in town.”

===Chinese===
他走进一间暂时空闲的通话间，接通孔克鲁克。先和一个嚼着熄灭雪茄的平民说了几句，然后地米斯托克利·姆赞圭出现在屏幕上。

「现在情况如何？」他问。

「稍微好转了些，」这位希腊裔非洲人答道。「半小时前，我们还在这里隔着窗户朝土佬开枪；现在我们已把他们困在太空港与土著部队、劳工营房之间，并沿岛屿东侧一线把他们压到农场。岛上农场四周的铁丝网已通了电，我们几乎把全部战斗反重力载具都用来守住大陆上的农场，不让土佬靠近。」他迟疑了片刻。「埃里克和勒莫因的事，你听说了吗？」

冯·施利希滕摇了摇头。

「我们刚接到罗道夫·麦金农的电话。他抓了两个俘虏，撬开了他们的嘴。在奥格齐尔德王宫里的那一整队人马全遭屠杀。有些人死于战斗，还算走运。土佬活捉了埃里克和亨德里克（Hendrik）；把他们拖到一滩热凝燃料（thermoconcentrate）里滚了几滚，点了火。等我们腾得出反重力载具，就往城里那座基加土佬使馆头上扔点东西。」

===Original===
“Well, that was what I wanted to call you about—contragravity.” He told M’zangwe about King Kankad’s offer. “His crowd ought to be coming in in a couple of hours. What can you scrape up to send to Kankad’s Town to airlift Kragans in?”

“Well, we have three hundred-and-fifty-foot gun-cutters, one 90 mm gun apiece. The *Elmoran*, the *Gaucho*, and the *Bushranger*. But they’re not much as transports, and we need them here pretty badly. Then, we have five fertilizer and charcoal scows, and a lot of heavy transport lorries, and two one-eighty-foot pickup boats.”

“How about the *Piet Joubert*?” von Schlichten asked. “She was due in Konkrook from the east about 13:00 today, wasn’t she?”

M’zangwe swore. “She got in, all right. But the geeks boarded her at the dock, within twenty minutes after things started. They tried to lift out with her, and the Channel Battery shot her down into Konkrook Channel, off the Fifty Sixth Street docks.”

“Well, you couldn’t let the geeks have her, to use against us. What do you hear from the other ships?”

===Chinese===
「嗯，我正是为反重力载具的事给你打电话。」他把坎卡德王的提议告诉了姆赞圭。「他那批人两三个小时内就该到了。你能凑出什么载具派到坎卡德城，把克拉冈人空运过来？」

「是这样，我们有三艘三百五十英尺的炮艇，各装一门90毫米炮：「埃尔莫兰」号、「高乔」号、「丛林游骑兵」号。可当运输船用并不顶事，而且我们这里也很缺它们。另外，还有五艘运肥料和木炭的驳船、一大批重型运输卡车，以及两艘一百八十英尺的接驳艇。」

「「皮特·朱伯特」号（Piet Joubert）怎么样了？」冯·施利希滕问。「她今天13:00前后就该从东边进孔克鲁克港了吧？」

姆赞圭骂了一句。「进是进港了，没错。可事情开始后不到二十分钟，土佬就在码头上占了她的船。他们试图驾着她起飞，海峡炮台把她击落，坠进第五十六街码头外的孔克鲁克海峡。」

「唉，总不能让土佬得着她，掉过头来打我们。其他船只有什么消息？」

===Original===
“*Procyon*’s at Grank; we haven’t had any reports of any kind from there, which doesn’t look so good. The *Northern Lights* is at Grank, too. The *Oom Paul Kruger* should have been at Bwork, in the east, when the gun went off. And the *Jan Smuts* and the *Christiaan De Wett* were both at Keegark; we can assume Orgzild has both of them.”

“All right. I’m sending *Aldebaran* to Kankad’s, to pick up more reinforcements for you.”

“We can use them! And with *Aldebaran*, we ought to be able to take the offensive against the city by this time tomorrow. Anything else?”

“Not at the moment. I’ll see about getting *Aldebaran* sent off, now.”

Leaving the booth, he heard, above the clatter of communications-machines and hubbub of voices, Jules Keaveney arguing contentiously. Evidently Colonel Cheng-Li’s efforts to drag the Resident out of his despondency had been an excessive success.

===Chinese===
「「南河三」号在格兰克（Grank）；我们从那边没收到任何形式的报告，这可不妙。「北极光」号（Northern Lights）也在格兰克。「奥姆·保罗·克鲁格」号（Oom Paul Kruger）事发时应该正在东边的布沃克（Bwork）。「扬·斯穆茨」号和「克里斯蒂安·德韦特」号（Christiaan De Wett）都在基加克；只能假定都落到奥格齐尔德手里了。」

「好。我派「毕宿五」号去坎卡德城，给你再接些增援来。」

「用得上！有了「毕宿五」号，到明天这个时候，我们应该能对城区转入攻势了。还有别的事吗？」

「暂时没有。我这就去安排「毕宿五」号启航。」

走出通话间，他在通讯机器的嗒嗒声和一片人声嘈杂之上，听见朱尔斯·基夫尼在情绪激昂地与人争辩。看来郑立上校把这位驻扎官从沮丧中拽出来的那番努力，成功得过了头。

===Original===
“But it’s crazy! Not just here; everywhere on Uller!” Keaveney was saying. “How did they do it? They have no telecast equipment.”

“You have me stopped, Jules,” Mordkovitz was replying. “I know a lot of rich geeks have receiving sets, but no sending sets.”

The pattern that had been tantalizing von Schlichten took visible shape in his mind. For a moment, he shelved the matter of the *Aldebaran*.

“They didn’t need sending equipment, Barney,” he said. “They used ours.”

“What do you mean?” Keaveney challenged.

===Chinese===
「可这简直是疯了！不光是这里；整个乌勒尔到处都是！」基夫尼正说着。「他们是怎么做到的？他们可没有电视广播设备。」

「这你可把我问住了，朱尔斯，」莫德科维茨答道。「我知道不少有钱的土佬有接收机，可没有发射机。」

那个一直折磨着冯·施利希滕的脉络，在他脑中忽然显出了清晰的形状。「毕宿五」号的事，他暂且搁到一旁。

「他们用不着发射设备，巴尼，」他说。「他们用的是我们的。」

「你这是什么意思？」基夫尼质问道。

===Original===
“Look what happened. Sid Harrington was poisoned in Konkrook. The news, of course, was sent out at once, as the geeks knew it would be, to every residency and trading-station on Uller, and that was the signal they’d agreed upon, probably months in advance. All they had to do was have that geek servant put poison in Harrington’s whiskey, and we did the rest.”

“Well, what was our intelligence doing—sleeping?” Keaveney demanded angrily.

“No, they were writing reports for your civil administration blokes to stuff in the wastebasket, and being called mailed-fist-and-rattling-saber alarmists for their pains.” He turned away from Keaveney. “Barney, where’s Dirk Prinsloo?”

“Aboard his ship. He hitched a ride to the airport with Jarman, when he was here picking up aircrews.”

“Call him. Tell him to take the *Aldebaran* to Kankad’s Town, at once; as soon as he arrives there, which ought to be about 11:00, he’s to pick up all the Kragans he can pack aboard and take them to Konkrook. From then on, he’ll be under Them M’zangwe’s orders.”

===Chinese===
「看看发生的事吧。席德·哈灵顿在孔克鲁克被毒杀。消息当然立刻发了出去——正如土佬们料定的那样——传到乌勒尔每一处驻扎官府和商站，而这正是他们约定好的信号，恐怕几个月前就定下了。他们要做的，无非是让那个土佬仆人往哈灵顿的威士忌里下毒，其余的事都是我们自己做的。」

「那么，我们的情报部门当时在干什么——睡觉吗？」基夫尼怒气冲冲地质问。

「没睡觉，他们在写报告，好让你们文官行政那帮家伙塞进废纸篓，还落得个辛苦一场、反被骂成挥铁拳晃军刀的战争贩子。」他转过身去，不再理基夫尼。「巴尼，德克·普林斯鲁（Dirk Prinsloo）在哪儿？」

「在他船上。他来这儿接机组人员的时候，搭贾曼的便车去了机场。」

「给他打电话。叫他立刻把「毕宿五」号开往坎卡德城；一到那里——大约应该是11:00——就把能塞上船的克拉冈人统统装上，送往孔克鲁克。从那时起，他归地米·姆赞圭指挥。」

===Original===
“To Konkrook?” Keaveney fairly howled. “Are you nuts? Don’t you think we need reinforcements here, too?”

“Yes, I do. I’m going to try to get them,” von Schlichten told him. “Now pipe down and get out of people’s way.”

He crossed the room, to where two Kragans, a male sergeant, and the ubiquitous girl in the orange sweater were struggling to get a big circular TV-screen up, then turned to look at the situation-map. A girl tech-sergeant was keeping Paula Quinton and Mrs. Jules Keaveney informed.

“Start pushing geeks out of the Fifth Zirk Cavalry barracks,” the sergeant was saying. “The one at the north end, and the one next to it; they’re both on fire, now.” She tossed a slip into the wastebasket beside her and glanced at the next slip. “And more pink pills back of the barracks and stables, and move them a little to the northwest; Kragans as skirmishers, to intercept geeks trying to slip away from the cavalry barracks.”

“Though why we want to do that, I don’t know,” Mrs. Keaveney said, pushing out a handful of pink pills with her billiard-bridge. “Let them go, and good riddance!”

“I never did like this bridge-of-silver-for-a-fleeing-enemy idea,” Paula Quinton said, evicting token-mutineers from the two northern barracks. “There’s usually two-way traffic on bridges. Kill them here and we won’t have to worry about keeping them out.”

===Chinese===
「开往孔克鲁克？」基夫尼简直嚎了起来。「你疯了吗？难道你不觉得我们这里也需要增援？」

「觉得。我也会想办法弄增援来，」冯·施利希滕告诉他。「现在闭上嘴，别在这儿碍事。」

他穿过房间，走到两名克拉冈人、一名男军士和那个无处不在的橙毛衣姑娘跟前——他们正费劲地竖起一块大圆形电视屏幕——然后转身去看态势图。一名女技术军士正不断向保拉·昆顿和朱尔斯·基夫尼太太通报情况。

「把白丸从第五齐尔克骑兵团营房那儿挪走吧，」那军士正说着。「北头那座，还有它旁边那座；现在都烧起来了。」她把一张纸条丢进身旁的废纸篓，看了下一张。「再在营房和马厩后面多放些粉丸，稍微往西北挪一点；表示克拉冈人展开散兵线，拦截想从骑兵营房溜走的土佬。」

「不过为什么要那么做，我可不明白，」基夫尼太太说着，用台球架桥器推出一把粉丸。「放他们走才好，眼不见为净！」

「我从来就不喜欢给逃跑的敌人留一座银桥，」保拉·昆顿一面说，一面把象征叛军的白丸从北头那两座营房里清出去。「桥上的往来从来是双向的。在这里杀了他们，就省得再操心怎么把他们挡在外面。」

===Original===
Of course, it was easy to be bloodthirsty about pink pills and white pills. Once, on a three-months’ reaction-drive voyage from Yggdrasill to Loki, he had taught a couple of professors of extraterrestrial zoology to play kriegspiel, and before the end of the trip, he was being horrified by the callous disregard they showed for casualties. But little Paula had the right idea; dead enemies don’t hit back.

A young Kragan with his lower left arm in a sling and a daub of antiseptic plaster over the back of his head came up and gave him a radioprint slip. Guido Karamessinis, the Resident-Agent at Grank, had reported, at last. The city, he said, was quiet, but King Yoorkerk’s troops had seized the Company airport and docks, taken the *Procyon* and the *Northern Lights* and put guards aboard them, and were surrounding the Residency. He wanted to know what to do.

Von Schlichten managed to get him on the screen, after a while.

“It looks as though Yoorkerk’s trying to play both sides at once,” he told the Grank Resident. “If the rebellion’s put down, he’ll come forward as your friend and protector; if we’re wiped out elsewhere, he’ll yell ‘*Znidd suddabit!*’ and swamp you. Don’t antagonize him; we can’t afford to fight this war on any more fronts than we are now. We’ll try to do something to get you unfrozen, before long.”

===Chinese===
当然，对着粉丸白丸喊打喊杀，总是容易的。有一次，在从伊格德拉西尔（Yggdrasill）到洛基（Loki）为期三个月的反作用力驱动航程中，他教会两位地外动物学教授下兵棋（kriegspiel），还没到旅程终点，他们俩对伤亡的那种麻木不仁已经让他心惊肉跳了。不过小保拉的想法是对的；死掉的敌人不会还手。

一个年轻的克拉冈人走上前来，递给他一张无线电打印条；他左下臂吊着悬带，后脑勺贴着一小块消毒纱布。格兰克驻扎代理圭多·卡拉梅西尼斯（Guido Karamessinis）终于来了报告。他说，城里平静，但约克克王（Yoorkerk）的部队占据了公司机场和码头，夺下「南河三」号和「北极光」号并派人看守，还包围了驻扎官府。他请示该怎么办。

过了一阵，冯·施利希滕总算接通了他的屏幕。

「看来约克克是想两头下注，」他对这位格兰克驻扎代理说。「叛乱若是被平定，他会跳出来做你的朋友和保护人；我们若是在别处被消灭，他就会高喊一声「Znidd suddabit！（Znidd suddabit!）」把你一口吞了。别去刺激他；这场仗，我们打不起比现在更多的战线了。用不了多久，我们会想办法给你解围。」

===Original===
He called Krink again. A girl with red-gold hair and a dusting of freckles across her nose answered.

“How are you making out?” he asked.

“So far, fine, general. We’re in complete control of the Company area, and all our native troops, not just the Kragans, are with us. Jonkvank’s pushed the mutineers out of his palace, and we’re keeping open a couple of streets between there and here. We airlifted all our Kragans and half the Sixth N.U.N.I. to the Palace, and we have the Zirks patrolling the streets on ’saurback. Now, we have our lorries and troop-carriers out picking up elements of Jonkvank’s loyal troops outside town.”

“Who’s doing the rioting, then?”

She named three of Jonkvank’s regiments. “And the city hoodlums, and priests from the temples of one sect that followed Rakkeed, and Skilkan fifth columnists. Mr. Shapiro can give you the details. Shall I call him?”

===Chinese===
他再次接通克林克。接电话的是个赤金色头发、鼻梁上洒着一层淡淡雀斑的姑娘。

「你们应付得怎么样？」他问。

「到目前为止很好，将军。公司辖区完全在我们控制之下，我们所有的土著部队——不只是克拉冈人——都和我们站在一起。琼克万克已把叛军赶出了王宫，我们还保持着王宫与这里之间几条街道的畅通。我们把全部克拉冈人和第六北乌勒尔土著步兵团的一半空运到了王宫，齐尔克兵则骑着龙背在街上巡逻。现在，我们的卡车和运兵车正出城接应琼克万克忠诚部队的各支分遣队。」

「那么，是谁在闹暴动？」

她点了琼克万克三个团的名号。「还有城里的流氓，追随拉基德（Rakkeed）的某一教派神庙里的祭司，以及斯基尔克第五纵队分子。详细情况夏皮罗先生可以向您介绍。要我叫他来吗？」

===Original===
“Never mind. He’s probably busy, he’s not as easy on the eyes as you are, and you’re doing all right. … How long do you think it’d take, with the equipment you have, to airlift all of Jonkvank’s loyal troops into the city?”

“Not before this time tomorrow.”

“All right. Are you in radio communication with Jonkvank now?”

“Full telecast, audiovisual,” the girl replied. “Just a minute, general.”

He put in his geek-speaker. The screen exploded into multicolored light, then cleared. Within a few minutes, a saurian Ulleran face was looking out of it at him—a harsh-lined, elderly face, with an old scar, quartz-crusted, along one side.

===Chinese===
「不必了。他多半正忙着，再说他也没你耐看，而你干得很好。……用你们现有的装备，把琼克万克的忠诚部队全部空运进城，你看要多久？」

「快不过明天这个时候。」

「好。你们现在和琼克万克保持着无线电联络吗？」

「全频道电视通讯，声像俱备，」姑娘答道。「请稍等，将军。」

他把土佬语发声器放进口中。屏幕炸开一片彩色光斑，随即清晰起来。不消几分钟，一张蜥蜴般的乌勒尔面孔便从屏幕里望着他——一张线条冷硬的长者面孔，一侧带着一道结满石英硬痂的旧疤。

===Original===
“Your Majesty,” von Schlichten greeted him.

Jonkvank pronounced something intended to correspond to von Schlichten’s name. “We have image-met under sad circumstances, general,” he said.

“Sad for both of us, King Jonkvank; we must help one another. I am told that your soldiers in Krink have risen against you, and that your loyal troops are far from the city.”

“Yes. That was the work of my War Minister, Hurkkurk, who was in the pay of King Firkked of Skilk, may Jeels devour him alive! I have Hurkkurk’s head here somewhere, if you want to see it, but that will not bring my loyal soldiers to Krink any sooner.”

“Dead traitors’ heads do not interest me, King Jonkvank,” von Schlichten replied, in what he estimated that the Krinkan king would interpret as a tone of cold-blooded cruelty. “There are too many traitors’ heads still on traitors’ shoulders. … What regiments are loyal to you, and where are they now?”

===Chinese===
「陛下，」冯·施利希滕向他致意。

琼克万克发出一串自以为对应「冯·施利希滕」之名的音节。「将军，我们在悲痛的情势下隔屏相见，」他说。

「对我们双方都是悲痛，琼克万克王；我们必须互相帮扶。我听说，您在克林克的士兵起兵反叛了您，而您的忠诚部队都远离城池。」

「正是。那是我的军务大臣赫尔库克（Hurkkurk）干的，他拿了斯基尔克王菲尔克德（Firkked）的钱——愿吉尔神（Jeels）活活吞了他！赫尔库克的脑袋，我这儿就收着一颗，您若要看可以给您看，可那并不能让我的忠诚士兵早一刻赶到克林克。」

「死了的叛徒脑袋，我不感兴趣，琼克万克王，」冯·施利希滕答道，用的语气，照他估量，这位克林克王会听成冷酷无情的残忍。「叛徒的脑袋，还有太多仍长在叛徒的肩膀上。……哪些团对您忠诚？他们现在何处？」

===Original===
Jonkvank began naming regiments and locating them, all at minor provincial towns at least a hundred miles from Krink.

“Hurkkurk did his work well; I’m afraid you killed him too mercifully,” von Schlichten said. “Well, I’m sending the *Northern Star* to Krink. She can only bring in one regiment at a trip, the way they’re scattered; which one do you want first?”

Jonkvank’s mouth, until now compressed grimly, parted in a gleaming smile. He made an exclamation of pleasure which sounded rather like a boy running along a picket fence with a stick.

“Good, general! Good!” he cried. “The first should be the regiment Murderers, at Furnk; they all have rifles like your soldiers. Have them brought to the Great Square, at the Palace here. And then, the regiment Fear-Makers, at Jeelznidd, and the regiment Corpse-Reapers, at.…”

“Let that go until the Murderers are in,” von Schlichten advised. “They’re at Furnk, you say? I’ll send the *Northern Star* there, directly.”

===Chinese===
琼克万克开始一一报出团名和驻地，全都在离克林克至少一百英里开外的小镇上。

「赫尔库克活干得不坏；恐怕您杀他杀得太仁慈了，」冯·施利希滕说。「这样吧，我派「北极星」号（Northern Star）去克林克。他们这么分散，她一趟只能运一个团；您想先要哪一个？」

琼克万克一直严峻紧抿的嘴咧开了，闪出一口亮堂堂的笑。他发出一声欢喜的欢呼，听上去活像个男孩拿根棍子沿尖桩栅栏一路划过去。

「好，将军！好！」他叫道。「第一个该运丰克（Furnk）的「杀人团」（regiment Murderers）；他们全都有你们士兵那样的步枪。把他们送到这里王宫前的大广场上。然后是吉尔兹尼德（Jeelznidd）的「造惧团」（regiment Fear-Makers），还有「收尸团」（regiment Corpse-Reapers），在……」

「那些等杀人团到了再说，」冯·施利希滕建议道。「你说他们在丰克？我这就派「北极星」号直接去那里。」

===Original===
