“Oh, good, general! I will not soon forget this! And as soon as the work is finished here, I will send soldiers to help you at Skilk. There shall be a great pile of the heads of those who had part in this wickedness, both here and there!”

“Good. Now, if you will pardon me, I’ll go to give the necessary orders.…”

As he left the booth, he saw Hideyoshi O’Leary in front of the situation-map, and hailed him.

“Harry and Hassan are getting the car re-ammoed; they dropped me off here. Want to come up with us and see the show?”

“No, I want you to go to Krink, as soon as Harry brings the car here again.” He told O’Leary what he intended doing. “You’ll probably have to go around ahead of the *Star* and alert these regiments. And as soon as things stabilize at Krink, prod Jonkvank into airlifting troops here. You’re authorized, in my name, to promise Jonkvank that he can assume political control at Skilk, after we’ve stuffed Firkked’s head in the dustbin.”

===Chinese===
「哦，太好了，将军！这份恩情我不会轻易忘掉！这边事情一了，我就派兵去斯基尔克帮你们。参与这场奸谋的家伙，这边的和那边的，脑袋都要堆成一座大山！」

「好。现在，恕我失陪，我要去下达必要的命令。……」

走出通话间时，他看见秀吉·奥利里（Hideyoshi O’Leary，即希德）站在态势图前，便招呼了他。

「哈里（Harry）和哈桑（Hassan）正在给飞车补充弹药；他们把我搁在这儿了。想跟我们一起上天看热闹吗？」

「不想，我要你去克林克，哈里一把飞车开回来你就动身。」他把自己的打算告诉了奥利里。「你多半得抢在「北极星」号前头先绕过去，通知这几个团。克林克局势一稳定，就催琼克万克往这里空运部队。我授权你以我的名义向琼克万克许诺：等我们把菲尔克德的脑袋塞进垃圾箱，他可以接管斯基尔克的政治管辖。」

===Original===
Jules Keaveney, who always seemed to be where he wasn’t wanted, heard that and fairly screamed.

“General von Schlichten! That is a political decision! You have no authority to make promises like that; that is a matter for the Governor-General, at least!”

“Well, as of now, and until a successor to Sid Harrington can be sent here from Terra, I’m Governor-General,” von Schlichten told him, mentally thanking Keaveney for reminding him of the necessity for such a step. “Captain Malavez! You will send out an all-station telecast, immediately: Military Commander-in-Chief Carlos von Schlichten, being informed of the deaths of both Governor-General Harrington and Lieutenant-Governor Blount, assumes the duties of Governor-General, as of 00:01 today.” He turned to Keaveney. “Does that satisfy you?” he asked.

“No, it doesn’t. You have no authority to assume a civil position of any sort, let alone the very highest position.…”

Von Schlichten unbuttoned his holster and took out his authority, letting Keaveney look into the muzzle of it.

===Chinese===
朱尔斯·基夫尼——此人似乎总能出现在没人想让他待的地方——听到了这话，简直尖叫起来。

「冯·施利希滕将军！这是政治决定！您无权做这样的许诺；这至少是总督的事！」

「哦，从现在起，直到能从地球派席德·哈灵顿的继任者来这里之前，我就是总督，」冯·施利希滕告诉他，心里还感谢基夫尼提醒了他有必要走这一步。「马拉维兹上尉！你立即向全台站发出电视通告：军事总司令卡洛斯·冯·施利希滕（Carlos von Schlichten），兹获悉总督哈灵顿、副总督布朗特（Blount）双双身亡，自今日00:01起接掌总督职权。」他转向基夫尼。「这样你满意了吗？」他问。

「不，不满意。您无权担任任何文职，更遑论最高的职位了。……」

冯·施利希滕解开枪套，掏出了他的「授权」，让基夫尼往枪口里瞧了瞧。

===Original===
“Here it is,” he said. “If you’re wise, don’t make me appeal to it.”

Keaveney shrugged. “I can’t argue with that,” he said. “But I don’t fancy the Uller Company is going to be impressed by it.”

“The Uller Company,” von Schlichten replied, “is six and a half parsecs away. It takes a ship six months to get from here to Terra, and another six months to get back. A radio message takes a little over twenty-one years, each way.” He holstered the pistol again. “You were bitching about how we needed reinforcements, a while ago. Well, here’s where we have to reverse Clausewitz and use politics as an extension by other means of war.”

“That brings up another question, general,” one of Keaveney’s subordinates said. “Can we hold out long enough for help to get here from Terra?”

“By the time help could reach us from Terra,” von Schlichten replied, “we’ll either have this revolt crushed, or there won’t be a live Terran left on Uller.” He felt a brief sadistic pleasure as he watched Keaveney’s face sag in horror. “What do you think we’ll live on, for a year?” he asked. “On this planet, there’s not more than a three months’ supply of any sort of food a human can eat. And the ships that’ll be coming in until word of our plight can get to Terra won’t bring enough to keep us going. We need the farms and livestock and the animal-tissue culture plant at Konkrook, and the farms at Krink and on the plateau back of Skilk, and we need peace and native labor to work them.”

===Chinese===
「这就是授权，」他说。「你若聪明，就别逼我诉诸于它。」

基夫尼耸了耸肩。「这个我没法反驳，」他说，「不过我看乌勒尔公司可不会买它的账。」

「乌勒尔公司，」冯·施利希滕答道，「远在六点五秒差距之外。船从这里去地球要六个月，回程再六个月。无线电报一个来回，单程就得二十一年多。」他把手枪重新插回枪套。「刚才你还在抱怨我们多么需要增援。哼，眼下我们就得把克劳塞维茨（Clausewitz）倒过来，让政治成为战争以其他手段的延续。」

「这就引出另一个问题了，将军，」基夫尼的一名下属说。「我们撑得住，一直撑到地球的援军到来吗？」

「等地球的援军赶到时，」冯·施利希滕答道，「我们要么已粉碎这场叛乱，要么乌勒尔星上已没有一个活着的地球人。」他看着基夫尼吓得垮下来的脸，感到一阵短暂的施虐快意。「你以为这一年我们靠什么过活？」他问。「在这颗行星上，凡是人能吃的东西，没有一样囤得上三个月的量。而在我们的困境传到地球之前陆续进港的船，运来的东西不够我们维持。我们需要孔克鲁克的农场、牲畜和动物组织培养厂，需要克林克和斯基尔克背后高原上的农场，还需要和平，需要土著劳动力来经营这一切。」

===Original===
Nobody seemed to have anything to say after that, for a while. Then Keaveney suggested that the next ship was due in from Niflheim in three months, and that it could be used to evacuate all the Terrans on Uller.

“And I’ll personally shoot any able-bodied Terran who tries to board that ship,” von Schlichten promised. “Get this through your heads, all of you. We are going to break this rebellion, and we are going to hold Uller for the Company and the Terran Federation.” He looked around him. “Now, get back to work, all of you,” he told the group that had formed around him and Keaveney. “Miss Quinton, you just heard me order my adjutant, Colonel O’Leary, on detached duty to Krink. I want you to take over for him. You’ll have rank and authority as colonel for the duration of this war.”

She was thunderstruck. “But I know absolutely nothing about military matters. There must be a hundred people here who are better qualified than I am.…”

“There are, and they all have jobs, and I’d have to find replacements for them, and replacements for the replacements. You won’t leave any vacancy to be filled. And you’ll learn, fast enough.” He went over to the situation-map again, and looked at the arrangement of pink and white pills. “First of all, I want you to call Jarman, at the military airport, and have an airjeep and driver sent around here for me. I’m going up and have a look around. Barney, keep the show going while I’m out, and tell Colonel Quinton what it’s all about.”

===Chinese===
这之后有一阵子，似乎谁也无话可说。后来基夫尼提议，三个月后下一班船将从尼福尔海姆（Niflheim）到来，可以用它把乌勒尔星上所有的地球人撤走。

「谁要是四肢健全还想上那条船，我亲自毙了他，」冯·施利希滕允诺道。「全体都给我记住了。我们要粉碎这场叛乱，我们要为公司、为地球联邦守住乌勒尔。」他环顾四周。「现在，全都回去干活，」他对围在他和基夫尼身边的人群说。「昆顿小姐，你刚听见我命令我的副官奥利里上校离队赴克林克。我要你接替他。这场战争期间，你将享有上校的军衔和职权。」

她惊得呆若木鸡。「可我对军事一窍不通。这里一定有一百个人比我更合格。……」

「是有，可他们个个都有职务，我就得另找人顶替他们，再找人顶替顶替者。你接手，不会留下任何要补的空缺。而且你学得会——很快。」他又走回态势图前，看了看粉丸白丸的布势。「首先，我要你给军用机场的贾曼打电话，让他派一辆飞行吉普和一名驾驶员到我这里来。我要上天去转一圈看看。巴尼，我不在时你把这场戏撑住，把来龙去脉都告诉昆顿上校。」

## VII Bismillah! How Dumb Can We Get? / 第七章 真主啊！我们还能蠢到什么地步？

===Original===
The lights had come on inside the semicircular and now open storm-porch of Company House, but it was still daylight outside. The sky above the mountain to the west was fading from crimson to burnt-orange, and a couple of the brighter stars were winking into visibility. Von Schlichten and the sergeant hurried a hundred yards down the street between low, thick-walled office buildings to the telecast station, next to the Administration Building.

A woman captain met him just inside the door of the big soundproofed room.

“We have a wavelength open to Konkrook, general,” she said. “In booth three.”

He nodded. “Thank you, captain.﻿ ﻿… We’ve all lost a true friend, haven’t we?”

Another girl, a tech-sergeant, was in the booth; on the screen was the image of a third young woman, a lieutenant, at Konkrook station. The sergeant rose and started to leave the booth.

“Stick around, sergeant,” von Schlichten told her. “I’ll want you to take over when I’m through.” He sat down in front of the combination visiscreen and pickup. “Now, lieutenant, just what happened?” he asked. “How did he die?”

===Chinese===
公司大楼（Company House）半圆形的防暴门廊此刻敞开着，廊内灯火已经点亮，外面却还是白昼。西边山岭上空的天色正由绯红褪作焦橙，几颗较亮的星已开始眨着眼显出身来。冯·施利希滕（von Schlichten）和那名军士沿着低矮厚墙的办公楼夹峙的街道疾行了百码，来到行政大楼旁的电视广播站（telecast station）。

一名女上尉就在那间大型隔音室的门内迎住他。

“将军，通孔克鲁克（Konkrook）的波长已经接好，”她说，“在三号演播间。”

他点了点头。“谢谢你，上尉。………我们都失去了一位真正的朋友，对吧？”

演播间里还有一位姑娘，是个技术军士；屏幕上是第三位年轻女子的影像——孔克鲁克站的一名中尉。那军士起身正要退出演播间。

“先别走，军士，”冯·施利希滕对她说，“我讲完之后还要你接手。”他在那台屏幕与摄像机合一的设备前坐下。“那么中尉，究竟出了什么事？”他问。“他是怎么死的？”

===Original===
“We think it was poison, general. General M’zangwe has ordered autopsy and chemical analysis. If you can wait about ten minutes, he’ll be able to talk to you, himself.”

“Call him. In the meantime, give me everything you know.”

“Well, the governor decided to go to bed early; he was going hunting in the morning. I suppose you know his usual routine?”

Von Schlichten nodded. Harrington would have taken a shower, put on his dressing-gown, and then sat down at his desk, lighted his pipe, poured a drink of Terran bourbon, and begun to write his diary.

“Well, at 22:10, give or take a couple of minutes, the Kragan guard-sergeant on that floor heard ten pistol-shots, as fast as they could be fired semi-auto, in the governor’s room. The door was locked, but he shot it off with his own pistol and went in. He found Governor Harrington on the floor, wearing only his gown, holding an empty pistol. He was in convulsions, frothing at the mouth, in horrible pain. Evidently he’d fired his pistol, which he kept on his desk, to call help; all the bullets had gone into the ceiling. The sergeant punched the emergency button, beside the bed, and reported, then tried to help the governor, but it was too late. One of the medics got there in five minutes, just as he was dying. He’d written his diary up to noon of today, and broken off in the middle of a word. There was a bottle and an overturned glass on his desk. The Constabulary got there a few minutes later, and then Brigadier-General M’zangwe took charge. A white rat, given fifteen drops from the whiskey-bottle, died with the same symptoms in about ninety seconds.”

===Chinese===
“我们估计是中毒，将军。姆赞圭将军（General M’zangwe）已经下令尸检和化学分析。您若能等上十分钟左右，他就能亲自同您谈。”

“叫他来。趁这工夫，把你所知道的一切都讲给我。”

“是这样，总督决定早些就寝；他明早要去打猎。他平日的惯例，您想必知道吧？”

冯·施利希滕点了点头。哈灵顿（Harrington）总督此时想必已冲过澡，换上晨衣，坐到书桌前，点着烟斗，斟上一杯地球波本威士忌，开始写他的日记了。

“是这样：22点10分，前后差不了两分钟，那一层值岗的克拉冈（Kragan）卫兵军士听见总督房里连响了十枪，快得跟半自动速射一样。房门锁着，他用自己的手枪打掉锁，闯了进去。他看见哈灵顿总督倒在地板上，身上只披着晨衣，手里攥着一把打空的手枪。他浑身抽搐，口吐白沫，痛楚难当。显然，他是开了那支一向放在书桌上的手枪呼救；子弹全都射进了天花板。军士按响床边的紧急按钮，作了报告，随即设法救护总督，但为时已晚。一名军医五分钟后赶到，正赶上他咽气。他的日记写到今天中午，在一个词的中间戛然断掉。书桌上有一只酒瓶和一只翻倒的酒杯。宪兵队（Constabulary）几分钟后赶到，随后姆赞圭准将（Brigadier-General M’zangwe）接掌了一切。有人从那瓶威士忌里取出十五滴喂给一只白鼠，约莫九十秒工夫，白鼠便带着同样的症状死了。”

===Original===
“Who had access to the whiskey-bottle?”

“A geek servant, who takes care of the room. He was caught, an hour earlier, trying to slip off the island without a pass; they were holding him at the guardhouse when Governor Harrington died. He’s now being questioned by the Kragans.” The girl’s face was bleakly remorseless. “I hope they do plenty to him!”

“I hope they don’t kill him before he talks.”

“Wait a moment, general; we have General M’zangwe, now,” the girl said. “I’ll switch you over.”

The screen broke into a kaleidoscopic jumble of color, then cleared; the chocolate-brown face of Themistocles M’zangwe was looking out of it.

===Chinese===
“那瓶威士忌，谁接触得到？”

“一个照管房间的土佬（geek）仆役。一小时前他想无证溜出岛去，被逮了个正着；哈灵顿总督咽气时，他正被押在警卫室里。现在克拉冈人正在审他。”姑娘的脸冷硬得没有一丝怜悯。“但愿他们狠狠收拾他！”

“我只希望别在他开口之前就把他弄死。”

“将军请稍候；姆赞圭将军来了，”姑娘说，“我这就给您切换过去。”

屏幕碎成万花筒般的一团乱色，随即复归清晰；地米斯托克利·姆赞圭（Themistocles M’zangwe）那张巧克力色的脸正从里面望出来。

===Original===
“I heard what happened, how they found him, and about that geek chamber-valet being arrested,” von Schlichten said. “Did you get anything out of him?”

“He’s admitted putting poison in the bottle, but he claims it was his own idea. But he’s one of Father Keeluk’s parishioners, so.﻿ ﻿…”

“Keeluk! God damn, so that was it!” von Schlichten almost shouted. “Now I know what he wanted with Stalin, and that goat, and those rabbits!”

Five thousand miles away, in Konkrook, Themistocles M’zangwe whistled.

“*Bismillah!* How dumb can we get?” he cried. “Of course they’d need terrestrial animals, to find out what would poison a Terran! Wait a minute; I’ll make a note of that, to spring on this geek, if the Kragans haven’t finished him by now.” Von Schlichten watched M’zangwe pick up a stenophone and whisper into it for a moment. “All right, Carlos, what else?”

===Chinese===
“事情经过、他们如何发现他、还有那个土佬贴身仆役被捕，我都听说了，”冯·施利希滕说。“你们从他嘴里问出点什么没有？”

“他承认往瓶里下了毒，但一口咬定是自己出的主意。可他是基卢克神父（Father Keeluk）教区的教民，所以……”

“基卢克！该死，原来根子在这儿！”冯·施利希滕几乎喊了起来。“现在我明白他要斯大林、那只山羊和那些兔子做什么用了！”

五千英里外的孔克鲁克，地米斯托克利·姆赞圭吹了一声口哨。

“*Bismillah*（真主啊）！我们还能蠢到什么地步？”他叫道。“他们当然需要地球动物，好试出什么东西能毒死地球人（Terran）！慢着，我把这一条记下来，回头好拿去突审这个土佬——要是克拉冈人还没把他结果掉的话。”冯·施利希滕看着姆赞圭拿起一支速记电话（stenophone），对它低语了片刻。“好了，卡洛斯（Carlos），还有什么？”

===Original===
“Has Eric been notified?”

“We called Keegark, but he’s in audience with King Orgzild, and we can’t reach him.”

“Well, who’s in charge at Konkrook, now?”

“Not much of anybody. Laviola, the Fiscal Secretary, and Hans Meyerstein, the Banking Cartel’s lawyer, and Howlett, the Personnel Chief, and Buhrmann, the Commercial Secretary, have made up a sort of quadrumvirate and are trying to run things. I don’t know what would happen if anything came up suddenly.﻿ ﻿…” A blue-gray uniformed arm, with a major’s cuff-braid, came into the screen, handing a slip of paper to M’zangwe; he took it, glanced at it, and swore. Von Schlichten waited until he had read it through.

===Chinese===
“通知埃里克（Eric）了吗？”

“我们往基加克（Keegark）挂了电话，可他正在觐见奥格齐尔德（Orgzild）王，联系不上。”

“那么眼下孔克鲁克由谁主事？”

“谈不上谁主事。财政秘书拉维奥拉（Laviola）、银行卡特尔的律师汉斯·迈耶斯坦（Hans Meyerstein）、人事主任豪利特（Howlett）、商务秘书布尔曼（Buhrmann），凑成了一个四头统治的小班子，勉力维持局面。万一突然冒出什么事，天知道会怎样……”一条蓝灰色制服的手臂伸进画面，袖口缀着少校袖饰，把一张纸条递给姆赞圭；他接过来扫了一眼，骂了句粗话。冯·施利希滕等他把纸条读完。

===Original===
“Well, something has, all right,” the African said. “We just got a call from Jaikark’s Palace﻿—a revolt’s broken out, presumably headed by Gurgurk; Household Guards either mutinied or wiped out by the mutineers, all but those twenty Kragan Rifles we loaned Jaikark. They, and about a dozen of Jaikark’s courtiers and their personal retainers, are holding the approaches to the King’s apartments. The native-lieutenant in charge of the Kragans just radioed in; says the situation is desperate.”

“When a Kragan says that, he means damn near hopeless. Is this being recorded?” When M’zangwe nodded, he continued: “All right. Use the recording for your authority and take charge. I’m declaring martial rule at Konkrook, as of now, 22:53. Tell Eric Blount what’s happened, and what you’ve done, as soon as you can get in touch with him. I’m leaving for Konkrook at once; I ought to get in by 08:00.

“Now, as to the trouble at the Palace. Don’t commit more than one company of Kragans and ten airjeeps and four combat-cars, and tell them to evacuate Jaikark and his followers and our Kragans to Gongonk Island. And alert your whole force. These geek palace revolutions are always synchronized with street-rioting, and this thing seems to have been synchronized with Sid Harrington’s death, too. Get our Kragans out if you can’t save anybody else from the Palace, but sacrificing thirty or forty men to save twenty is no kind of business. And keep sending reports; I can pick them up on my car radio as I come down.” He turned to the girl sergeant. “Keep on this; there’ll be more coming in.”

===Chinese===
“这不，真就冒出事了，”这位非洲人说。“我们刚接到贾伊卡克（Jaikark）王宫打来的电话——宫里起了叛乱，领头的估计是古古尔克（Gurgurk）；王宫卫队不是哗变，就是被叛乱者消灭了，只剩我们借给贾伊卡克的那二十名克拉冈来复枪兵。他们连同贾伊卡克的十来名廷臣及其私人仆从，还守着通往国王寝宫的各处要道。带队的克拉冈土著中尉刚用无线电报来，说局势危殆。”

“克拉冈人说出‘危殆’二字，那就等于近乎绝望了。这些都在录音吗？”等姆赞圭点头后，他接着说：“好。你就以这段录音为凭，接管全局。我现在宣布孔克鲁克实行军事管制，自即刻起，22点53分生效。一俟联系上埃里克·布朗特（Eric Blount），立刻把事情经过和你采取的措施告诉他。我马上动身去孔克鲁克，应当能赶在8点前进城。”

“再说王宫那头的乱子。投入兵力不得超过一个连的克拉冈兵、十辆飞行吉普（airjeep）和四辆战斗车（combat-car），并命令他们把贾伊卡克及其随从连同我们的克拉冈兵一起撤到贡贡克岛（Gongonk Island）去。还有，把你全部人马都动员待命。土佬的宫廷政变向来与街头暴乱同步发动，这一回看来还和席德·哈灵顿之死掐在同一时刻。王宫里若旁人一个也救不出，至少把我们的克拉冈兵撤出来；可为救二十人而搭进三四十人，这种买卖不能做。报告要持续发来，我南下的路上可以用车载电台接收。”他转向那女军士。“盯住这条线路；后面还有的是消息进来。”

===Original===
He rose and left the booth. If we can pull Jaikark’s bacon off the fire, he was thinking, the Company can dictate its own terms to him afterward; if Jaikark’s killed, we’ll have Gurgurk’s head off for it, and then take over Konkrook. In either case, it’ll be a long step toward getting rid of all these geek despots. And with Eric Blount as Governor-General.﻿ ﻿…

The girl captain in charge of the station met him as he came out.

“Poison,” he told her. “A geek servant did the job, on orders from Gurgurk and possibly Rakkeed. Gurgurk’s started a putsch against King Jaikark; I’m going to Konkrook at once. Call the military airport and have my command-car brought to Company House.”

Harry Quong and Hassan Bogdanoff had been at the banquet, too; on a world of lizard-faced silicate-eaters, the social difference between a human general and a human aircar-driver was almost infinitesimal. He’d have to talk to Barney Mordkovitz, too; when word of events at Konkrook got out among the local geeks, as it probably had already.﻿ ﻿…

===Chinese===
他起身出了演播间。要是能把贾伊卡克从火堆里捞出来，他心里盘算着，公司（the Company）事后就能对他自行开条件；要是贾伊卡克被杀，我们就砍下古古尔克的脑袋抵账，然后接管孔克鲁克。无论哪种结局，都是朝铲除这帮土佬暴君迈出的一大步。再让埃里克·布朗特出任总督……

他出来时，主管广播站的那位女上尉迎了上来。

“是毒杀，”他告诉她。“动手的是个土佬仆役，奉古古尔克之命，或许还有拉基德（Rakkeed）的份。古古尔克已经发动了反对贾伊卡克王的政变；我立刻动身去孔克鲁克。打电话给军用机场，叫人把我的指挥车开到公司楼前来。”

哈里·关（Harry Quong）和哈桑·博格丹诺夫（Hassan Bogdanoff）也出席了宴会；在这颗遍地蜥蜴面孔、吞食硅酸盐的居民的星球上，一位人类将军和一位人类飞车司机之间的社会差距，几乎微不足道。他还得找巴尼·莫德科维茨（Barney Mordkovitz）商量；孔克鲁克事变的消息一旦在当地土佬中间传开——只怕早已经传开了……


===Original===
The inner door of the soundproofed telecast-room burst open, three men hurried inside, and it slammed shut behind them. In the brief interval, there had been firing audible from outside. One of the men had a pistol in his right hand, and with his left arm he supported a companion, whose shoulder was mangled and dripped blood. The third man had a burp-gun in his hands. All were in civilian dress-shorts and light jackets. The man with the pistol holstered it and helped his injured companion into a chair. The burp-gunner advanced into the room, looked around, saw von Schlichten, and addressed him.

“General! The geeks turned on us!” he cried. “The Tenth North Uller’s mutinied; they’re running wild all over the place. They’ve taken their barracks and supply-buildings, and the lorry-hangars and the maintenance-yard; they’re headed this way in a mob. Some of the Zirk Cavalry’s joined them.”

“How about the Kragans?”

“The Eighteenth Rifles? They’re with us. I saw a party of them firing into the mob; I saw some of the Tenth N.U.N.I. tossing a dead Kragan on their bayonets.…”

“Have any ammo left for that burp-gun? Come on, then; let’s see what it’s like at Company House,” von Schlichten said. “Captain Malavez, you know what to do about defending this station. Get busy doing it. And have that girl in booth three tell Konkrook what’s happened here, and say that I won’t be coming down, as planned, just yet.”

===Chinese===
隔音电视广播室的内门砰然撞开，三条汉子抢进屋来，门又在他们身后砰地关死。开门的短短一瞬间，外面的枪声传了进来。其中一人右手握着手枪，左臂架着一名同伴，那同伴的肩膀血肉模糊，鲜血直滴。第三人手里端着一支冲锋枪（burp-gun）。三人都穿着便装短裤和薄外套。握手枪的把手枪插回枪套，扶受伤的同伴坐进椅子。端冲锋枪的走进屋内，环顾四周，看见冯·施利希滕，便开口道：

“将军！土佬反了！”他叫道。“第十北乌勒尔土著步兵团（Tenth North Uller Native Infantry）哗变了，正在到处横冲直撞。他们占了营房和补给库，还有载重飞车（lorry）机库和维修场，正成群结队朝这边扑来。一部分齐尔克（Zirk）骑兵也入了伙。”

“克拉冈人呢？”

“第十八来复枪团（Eighteenth Rifles）？他们站在我们一边。我看见他们一队人正朝暴民群里开火；我还看见几个第十北乌勒尔土著步兵团的哗变兵，用刺刀挑着一具克拉冈兵的尸体抛来抛去……”

“那支冲锋枪还有弹药没有？有就跟我来；去看看公司大楼那边是什么光景，”冯·施利希滕说。“马拉维兹上尉（Captain Malavez），这座广播站怎么防御，你知道该做什么，马上动手。再叫三号演播间那位姑娘把这边出的事告诉孔克鲁克，就说我不按原计划立刻南下了。”


===Original===
He opened the door, and the rattle of shots outside became audible again. The civilian with the burp-gun knew better than to let a general go out first; elbowing von Schlichten out of the way, he crouched over his weapon and dashed outside. Drawing his pistol, von Schlichten followed, pulling the door shut after him.

Darkness had fallen, while he had been inside; now the whole Company Reservation was ablaze with electric lights. Somebody at the power-plant—either the regular staff, if they were still holding, or the mutineers, if they had taken it—had thrown on the emergency lights. There was a confused mass of gray-skinned figures in front of Company House, reflected light twinkling on steel over them; from the direction of the native-troops barracks more natives were coming on the run. On the roof of a building across the street, two machine-guns were already firing into the mob. A group of Terrans came running out of a roadway between two buildings, from the direction of the repair-shops; several of them paused to fire behind them with pistols. They started toward Company House, saw what was going on there, and veered, darting into the door of the building from which the auto-weapons were firing. From up the street, a hundred-odd saurian-faced native soldiers were coming at the double, bayonets fixed and rifles at high port; with them ran several Terrans. Motioning his companion to follow, von Schlichten ran to meet them, falling in beside a Terran captain who ran in front.

“What’s the score, captain?” he asked.

“Tenth North Uller and the Fifth Cavalry have mutinied; so have these ragtag Auxiliaries. That mob down there’s part of them.” He was puffing under the double effort of running and talking. “Whole thing blew up in seconds; no chance to communicate with anybody.…”

===Chinese===
他推开门，外面炒豆般的枪声又灌进耳来。那名带冲锋枪的平民深知万万不能让将军先出险；他用胳膊肘把冯·施利希滕拨开，弓身护住枪，一头冲了出去。冯·施利希滕拔出手枪跟了上去，随手把门带拢。

他在屋里的这一阵子，夜幕已经落了下来；此刻整个公司特区（Company Reservation）灯火通明。发电厂那边不知是谁——若是正规员工还在坚守，那是他们；若是已被叛军占领，那是叛军——把应急照明全打开了。公司大楼前挤着一大团乱糟糟的灰皮肤身影，头顶钢铁上反射的灯光明灭闪烁；土著部队营房方向，又有土著正跑步赶来。街对面一座楼房的屋顶上，两挺机枪已经朝暴民群扫射起来。一群地球人从两座楼房之间的一条车道上奔出，方向是修理车间那头；其中几人边跑边回身用手枪射击。他们先是奔向公司大楼，看清那边的光景后立刻拐弯，一头扎进自动武器正喷吐火舌的那座楼的大门。街那头，一百多名蜥蜴面孔的土著士兵正跑步开来，刺刀已上枪，步枪高位端起；与他们并肩奔跑的还有几名地球人。冯·施利希滕招手示意同伴跟上，跑步迎了上去，落到跑在最前的一名地球人上尉身旁。

“上尉，眼下局势如何？”他问。

“第十北乌勒尔土著步兵团和第五骑兵团哗变了；这批杂牌辅助军也反了。下面那群暴民就是他们的一部分。”他边跑边说话，喘得上气不接下气。“整件事几秒钟就炸了锅，根本来不及跟任何人联络……”


===Original===
A Terran woman, in black slacks and an orange sweater, ran across the street in front of them, pursued by a group of enlisted “men” of the Tenth North Uller Native Infantry, all shrieking “*Znidd suddabit!*” The fugitive ran into a doorway across the street; before her pursuers were aware of their danger, the Kragans had swept over them. There was no shooting; the slim, cruel-bladed bayonets did the work. From behind him, as he ran, von Schlichten could hear Kragan voices in a new cry: “*Znidd geek! Znidd geek!*”

The mob were swarming up onto the steps and into the semi-rotunda of the storm-porch. There was shooting, which told him that some of the humans who had been at the banquet were still alive. He wondered, half-sick, how many, and whether they could hold out till he could clear the doorway, and, most of all, he found himself thinking of Paula Quinton. Skidding to a stop within fifty yards of the mob, he flung out his arms crucifix-wise to halt the Kragans. Behind, he could hear the Terrans and native-officers shouting commands to form front.

“Give them one clip, reload, and then give them the bayonet!” he ordered. “Shove them off the steps and then clear the porch!”

===Chinese===
一名地球女人身穿黑色便裤和橙色毛衣，从他们面前的街上横穿而过，身后一群第十北乌勒尔土著步兵团的“男”兵紧追不舍，个个尖声怪叫着“Znidd suddabit!”（乌勒尔土语咒骂）。逃命的女人闪进街对面的一个门洞；追兵还没觉出大祸临头，克拉冈兵已席卷而上。没有人开枪；细窄凶锋的刺刀包办了一切。奔跑间，冯·施利希滕听得身后克拉冈人喊出了新的吼声：“Znidd geek! Znidd geek!”（弄死土佬！）

暴民正蜂拥着冲上台阶，涌进防暴门廊的半圆回廊。里面有枪声传出，可见赴宴的人类当中还有活着的。他心头发紧地想着：还有几个？能不能撑到他清开大门口？而最叫他挂心的，他发现自己满脑子想的竟是保拉·昆顿（Paula Quinton）。他在离暴民不到五十码处急停住脚，双臂十字般平伸，喝止住克拉冈兵。身后传来地球人和土著军官们喝令结成横队的声音。

“先给他们一个弹匣，装弹，然后上刺刀！”他下令。“把他们轰下台阶，再把门廊肃清！”

===Original===
“One clip, fire, and reload, at will!” somebody passed it on in Kragan.

The hundred rifles let go all at once, and for five seconds they poured a deafening two thousand rounds into the mutineers. There was some fire in reply; a Zirk corporal narrowly missed him with a pistol, he saw the captain’s head fly apart when an explosive rifle-bullet hit him, and half a dozen Kragans went down.

“Reload! Set your safeties!” von Schlichten bellowed. “Charge!”

===Chinese===
“一个弹匣，自由射击，然后装弹！”有人用克拉冈语把口令传了下去。

一百支步枪同时打响，五秒钟内向哗变者倾泻了两千发子弹，震耳欲聋。对方有一些还击；一名齐尔克下士的手枪险些打中他，他看见一发爆裂弹头击中那名上尉，上尉的头颅顿时炸裂开来，还有六名克拉冈兵倒了下去。

“装弹！关上保险！”冯·施利希滕大吼。“冲锋！”


===Original===
Under human officers, the North Uller Native Infantry would have stood firm. Even under their native-officers and sergeants, they should not have broken as they did, but the best of these had paid for their loyalty to the Company with their lives, and the rest had destroyed their authority by revolting against the source from which it was derived. At that, the Skilkan peasantry who made up the Tenth Infantry and the Zirk cavalrymen tried briefly to fight as individuals, shrieking “*Znidd suddabit!*” until the Kragans were upon them, stabbing and shooting. They drove the rioters from the steps or killed them there, they wiped out those who had gotten into the semicircle of the storm-porch. The inside doors, von Schlichten saw, were open, but beyond them were Terrans and a dozen or so Kragans. Hideyoshi O’Leary and Barney Mordkovitz seemed to be in command of these.

“We had about thirty seconds’ warning,” Mordkovitz reported, “and the Kragans in the hall bought us another sixty seconds. Of course, we all had our pistols.…”

“Hey! These storm-doors are wedged!” somebody discovered. “Those goddam geek servants…!”

“Yeah, kill any of them you catch,” somebody else advised. “If we could have gotten these doors closed.…”

===Chinese===
倘在地球人军官麾下，北乌勒尔土著步兵本会站稳脚跟。哪怕只由他们的土著军官和军士带队，也不至于崩得这样快；可这些军官里最忠勇的，已经用性命偿清了对公司的忠诚，其余的则因反叛授予他们权威的源头，亲手砸掉了自己的权威。即便如此，构成第十步兵团的斯基尔克（Skilkan）的农夫和齐尔克骑兵仍各自为战了片刻，尖声怪叫着“Znidd suddabit!”，直到克拉冈兵冲至近前，又刺又射。他们把暴乱者赶下台阶，或就地处决，又把闯进防暴门廊半圆回廊里的那些人斩尽杀绝。冯·施利希滕看见里面的门敞开着，门后站着地球人和十来个克拉冈兵。带头的似乎是秀吉·奥利里（Hideyoshi O’Leary）和巴尼·莫德科维茨。

“我们大约提前三十秒得到警报，”莫德科维茨报告道，“门厅里的克拉冈兵又替我们买来了六十秒。当然，我们人人都带着手枪……”

“嘿！这些防暴门全叫人楔死了！”有人发现了。“准是那些天杀的土佬仆役干的……！”

“可不是，逮住几个宰几个，”另一人出主意。“当初这些门要是还关得上……”

===Original===
The mob, driven from the steps, was trying to reform and renew the attack. From up the street, the machine-guns, silent during the bayonet-fight, began hammering again. The mob surged forward to get out of their fire, and were met by a rifle-blast and a hedge of bayonets at the steps; they surged back, and the machine-guns flailed them again. They started to rush the building from whence the automatic-fire came, and there was a fusillade and a shriek of “*Znidd geek!*” from up the street. They turned and fled in the direction from whence they had come, bullets scourging them from three directions at once.

For a moment, von Schlichten and the three Terrans and eighty-odd Kragans who had survived the fight stood on the steps, weapons poised, seeking more enemies. The machine-guns up the street stuttered a few short bursts and were silent. From behind, the beleaguered Terrans and their Kragan guards were emerging. He saw Jules Keaveney and his wife, Commander Prinsloo of the *Aldebaran*, Harry Quong and Bogdanoff. Ah, there she was! He heaved a breath of relief and waved to her.

The Kragans were already setting about their after-battle chores. About twenty of them spread out on guard; the others, by fours, went into the street, one covering with his rifle while the other three checked on their own casualties, used the short, leaf-shaped swords they carried to slash off the heads of enemy wounded, and collected weapons and ammunition. A couple of hundred more Kragans, led by Native-Major Kormork, the co-parent of young with King Kankad, came up at the double and stopped in front of Company House.

===Chinese===
被逐下台阶的暴民正试图重整旗鼓、再度进攻。街那头的机枪在白刃战里一直沉默，这时又嗒嗒地敲响起来。暴民向前涌，想脱出机枪火线，却在台阶前撞上一阵排枪和一堵刺刀的篱墙；他们向后涌回，机枪又把他们抽打了一通。他们转而扑向自动火力射来的那座楼房，街那头立刻响起一阵齐射和“Znidd geek!”的尖啸。他们掉头向来路溃逃，子弹顿时从三个方向同时鞭笞着他们。

有那么一刻，冯·施利希滕和三名地球人，以及战斗后幸存的八十来个克拉冈兵，站在台阶上持枪以待，搜寻着更多敌人。街那头的机枪又断续吐了几个短点射，随即归于沉寂。被围困的地球人和他们的克拉冈卫兵正从里面走出来。他看见朱尔斯·基夫尼（Jules Keaveney）夫妇、“毕宿五”号（Aldebaran）的普林斯鲁海军中校（Commander Prinsloo）、哈里·关和博格丹诺夫。啊，她在那儿呢！他长舒一口气，朝她挥了挥手。

克拉冈兵已经开始料理战后的活计。约二十人散开警戒；其余的每四人一组走进街区，一人持枪掩护，另外三人清点己方伤亡、用随身携带的叶形短剑砍去敌方伤兵的脑袋，并收拾武器弹药。又有两百来个克拉冈兵跑步开到，在公司大楼前停住；带队的是土著少校科尔莫克（Native-Major Kormork）——坎卡德王曾与他共同生育过后代。


===Original===
“We were in quarters, aboard the *Aldebaran* and in the guesthouse at the airport,” Kormork reported. “We were attacked, fifteen minutes ago, by a mob. We took ten minutes beating them off, and five more getting here. I sent Native-Captain Zeerjeek and the rest of the force to retake the supply-depot and the shops and lorry hangars, which had been taken, and relieve the military airport, which is under attack.”

There was still firing from the commercial airport and the smaller military airfield. Once there was a string of heavy explosions that sounded like 80 mm rockets.

“Good enough. I hope you didn’t spread yourself out too thin. What’s the situation at the commercial airport?”

“The two ships, the *Aldebaran* and the freighter *Northern Star*, are both safe,” Kormork replied. “I saw them go on contragravity and rise to about a hundred feet.”

===Chinese===
“我们原在「毕宿五」号上和机场的宾馆里宿营，”科尔莫克报告。“十五分钟前遭到一群暴民攻击。我们用了十分钟把他们打退，又花五分钟赶到这里。我已派土著上尉齐尔吉克（Zeerjeek）率其余兵力去夺回被占的补给站、修理车间和载重飞车机库，并驰援正遭攻击的军用机场。”

商用机场和较小的军用机场那边仍有枪炮声。有一阵传来一连串沉重的爆炸，听上去像80毫米火箭弹。

“很好。但愿你们没把兵力摊得太散。商用机场情况如何？”

“两艘船，「毕宿五」号和货船「北极星」号（Northern Star），都安然无恙，”科尔莫克答道。“我看着它们启动反重力，升到了一百英尺上下。”

===Original===
“Whose crowd is that you have?” he asked the Terran lieutenant who had taken over command of the first force of Kragans.

“Company 6, Eighteenth Rifles, sir. We were on duty at the guardhouse; fighting broke out in the direction of the native barracks. A couple of runners from Captain Retief of Company 4 came in with word that he was being attacked by mutineers from the Tenth N.U.N.I. but that he was holding them back. So Captain Charbonneau, who was killed a few minutes ago, left a Terran lieutenant and a Kragan native-lieutenant and a couple of native-sergeants and thirty Kragans to hold the guardhouse, and brought the rest of us here.”

Von Schlichten nodded. “You’d pass the military airport and the power-plant, wouldn’t you?” he asked.

“Yes, sir. The military airport’s holding out, and I saw the red-and-yellow danger-lights on the fence around the power-plant.”

That meant the power-plant was, for the time, safe; somebody’d turned twenty thousand volts into the fence.

===Chinese===
“你带的这一队是谁的兵？”他问那名接掌了第一队克拉冈兵指挥权的地球人中尉。

“第十八来复枪团第6连，长官。我们原在警卫室值勤；土著营房方向突然打了起来。第4连的雷蒂夫上尉（Captain Retief）派来两名传令兵，说他正遭第十北乌勒尔土著步兵团哗变兵的攻击，但他顶得住。于是几分钟前阵亡的夏博诺上尉（Captain Charbonneau）留下一名地球人中尉、一名克拉冈土著中尉、两名土著军士和三十名克拉冈兵守住警卫室，带着我们其余的人赶到了这里。”

冯·施利希滕点了点头。“你们过来时，路过军用机场和发电厂，对吧？”他问。

“是的，长官。军用机场守住了，我还看见发电厂围栏上红黄相间的危险灯亮着。”

这就是说，发电厂眼下是安全的；有人给围栏通上了两万伏的电。


===Original===
“All right. I’m setting up my command post at the telecast station, where the communication equipment is.” He turned to the crowd that had come out onto the porch from inside. “Where’s Colonel Cheng-Li?”

“Here, general.” The Intelligence and Constabulary officer pushed through the crowd. “I was on the phone, talking to the military airport, the commercial airport, ordnance depot, spaceport, ship-docks and power-plant. All answer. I’m afraid Pop Goode, at the city power-plant, is done for; nobody answers there, but the TV-pickup is still on in the load-dispatcher’s room, and the place is full of geeks. Colonel Jarman’s coming here with a lorry to get combat-car crews; he’s short-handed. Port-Captain Leavitt has all the native labor at the airport and spaceport herded into a repair dock; he’s keeping them covered with the forward 90 mm gun of the *Northern Star*. Lorry-hangars, repair-shops and maintenance-yards don’t answer.”

“That’s what I was going to ask you. Good enough. Harry Quong, Hassan Bogdanoff!”

===Chinese===
“好。我的指挥所就设在电视广播站，通信设备都在那边。”他转向从里面来到门廊上的人群。“郑立上校（Colonel Cheng-Li）在哪儿？”

“在这儿，将军。”那位情报兼宪兵军官从人群中挤出来。“我一直在通话，同军用机场、商用机场、军械库、太空港、船坞和发电厂逐一联络，都有应答。城市发电厂那边的古德老爹（Pop Goode）恐怕是不行了；无人应答，但负荷调度室里的电视摄像机还开着，满屋子都是土佬。贾曼上校（Colonel Jarman）正乘一辆载重飞车来接战斗车的乘员，他人手不够。港务长利维特（Port-Captain Leavitt）已把机场和太空港的全部土著劳工赶进了一座修理坞，正用「北极星」号的舰首90毫米炮看住他们。载重飞车机库、修理车间和维修场均无应答。”

“我正要问你这些。很好。哈里·关！哈桑·博格丹诺夫！”

===Original===
His command-car crew front-and-centered.

“I want you to take Colonel O’Leary up, as soon as my car’s brought here.… Hid, you go up and see what’s going on. Drop flares where there isn’t any light. And take a look at the native-labor camp and the equipment-park, south of the reservation.… Kormork, you take all your gang, and half these soldiers from the Eighteenth, here, and help clear the native-troops barracks. And don’t bother taking any prisoners; we can’t spare personnel to guard them.”

Kormork grinned. The taking of prisoners had always been one of those irrational Terran customs which no Ulleran regarded with favor, or even comprehension.

===Chinese===
他的指挥车乘员应声出列，齐到他跟前站定。

“等我的车一到，你们就载着奥利里上校升空。……希德（Hid），你上去看看那边到底怎么回事。哪里没有灯光，就往哪里投照明弹。顺带察看特区以南的土著劳工营和器材停放场。……科尔莫克，你带上你的全部人马，加上这边第十八团的一半兵力，去协助肃清土著部队营房。抓俘虏的事就免了；我们腾不出人手看押他们。”

科尔莫克咧嘴一笑。抓俘虏向来是地球人那类莫名其妙的习俗之一，没有哪个乌勒尔人对它抱有好感，甚至连理解都谈不上。

## I Commander-in-Chief Front and Center / 第一章 总司令，出列

===Original===
General Carlos von Schlichten threw his cigarette away, flexed his hands in his gloves, and set his monocle more firmly in his eye, stepping forward as the footsteps on the stairway behind him ceased and the other officers emerged from the squat flint keep﻿—Captain Cazabielle, the post C.O.; big, chocolate-brown Brigadier-General Themistocles M’zangwe; little Colonel Hideyoshi O’Leary. Far in front of him, to the left, the horizon was lost in the cloudbank over Takkad Sea; directly in front, and to the right, the brown and gray and black flint mountains sawed into the sky until they vanished in the distance. Unseen below, the old caravan-trail climbed one side of the pass and slid down the other, a sheer five hundred feet below the parapet and the two corner catapult-platforms which now mounted 90 mm guns. On the little hundred-foot-square parade ground in front of the keep, his aircar was parked, and the soldiers were assembled.

Ten or twelve of them were Terrans﻿—a couple of lieutenants, sergeants, gunners, technicians, the sergeant-driver and corporal-gunner of his own car. The other fifty-odd were Ulleran natives. They stood erect on stumpy legs and broad, six-toed feet. They had four arms apiece, one pair from true shoulders and the other connected to a pseudo-pelvis midway down the torso. Their skins were slate-gray and rubbery, speckled with pinhead-sized bits of quartz that had been formed from perspiration, for their body-tissues were silicone instead of carbon-hydrogen. Their narrow heads were unpleasantly saurian; they had small, double-lidded red eyes, and slit-like nostrils, and wide mouths filled with opalescent teeth. Except for their belts and equipment, they were completely naked; the uniform consisted of the emblem of the Chartered Uller Company stencil-painted on chests and backs. Clothing, to them, was unnecessary, either for warmth or modesty. As to the former, they were cold-blooded and could stand a temperature-range of from a hundred and twenty to minus one hundred Centigrade. Von Schlichten had seen them sleeping in the open with their bodies covered with frost or freezing rain; he had also seen them wade through boiling water. As to the second, they had practically no sex-inhibitions; they were all of the same gender, true, functional, hermaphrodites. Any individual among them could bear young, or fertilize the ova of any other individual. Fifteen years ago, when he had come to Uller as a former Terran Federation captain newly commissioned colonel in the army of the Uller Company, it had taken some time before he had become accustomed to the detailing of a noncom and a couple of privates out of each platoon for babysitting duty. At least, though, they didn’t have the squaw-trouble around army posts on Uller that they had on Thor, where he had last been stationed.

===Chinese===
卡洛斯·冯·施利希滕将军（General Carlos von Schlichten）把烟头一扔，在手套里屈伸了几下双手，把单片眼镜往眼窝里按得更牢，随即迈步上前——身后楼梯上的脚步声这时刚刚停歇，其他军官正从低矮的燧石主堡里走出来：要塞指挥官卡扎比埃尔上尉（Captain Cazabielle）；块头魁梧、肤色如巧克力般棕黑的陆军准将地米斯托克利·姆赞圭（Brigadier-General Themistocles M'zangwe）；还有矮小的秀吉·奥利里上校（Colonel Hideyoshi O'Leary）。在他前方极远处靠左，地平线隐没在塔卡德海（Takkad Sea）上空的云堤里；正前方与右侧，褐色、灰色、黑色的燧石群山像锯齿般切入天空，直到远方才渐渐隐没。脚下看不见的地方，古老的商队小径从山隘的一侧攀上来，又顺着另一侧溜下去，落在垛口和两座角台的垂直下方五百英尺处——那两座从前架投石机的角台，如今架的是90毫米炮。主堡前面那块一百英尺见方的小阅兵场上，停着他的飞车（aircar），士兵们已经集合完毕。

其中十来个是地球人（Terran）——两名中尉、几名军士、炮手、技术兵，还有他自己座车的驾驶中士和机枪下士。其余五十多个是乌勒尔土著（Ulleran）。他们挺直站立在粗短的腿和宽大的六趾脚上。个个生着四条手臂，一对出自真正的肩膀，另一对连着躯干中段的一副假骨盆。他们的皮肤呈板岩灰色，质感如橡胶，上面缀满针头大小的石英细粒——那是汗水凝结成的，因为他们的机体组织是硅酮，而非碳氢化合物。狭长的头颅一副蜥蜴相，瞧着叫人不舒服；一双小小的红眼睛有双层眼睑，鼻孔细如裂缝，阔嘴里满是泛着乳光的牙齿。除了腰带和装备，他们浑身赤裸；所谓军服，就是用漏字板漆在胸口和背上的特许乌勒尔公司（Chartered Uller Company）徽记。衣着之于他们毫无必要——无论为御寒还是为遮羞。就御寒而言，他们是冷血种族，受得了从摄氏零上一百二十度到零下一百度的温差；冯·施利希滕见过他们露宿旷野，周身覆着白霜或冻雨，也见过他们蹚过滚沸的开水。至于遮羞，他们几乎没有性禁忌；他们清一色全是同一性别——货真价实、机能完备的雌雄同体。他们中任何一个个体都能生育后代，也都能为任何其他个体的卵授精。十五年前，他以地球联邦（Terran Federation）前上尉、新受任乌勒尔公司陆军上校的身份初到乌勒尔时，曾过了好一阵子才习惯一件事：每个排都要抽一名军士带两名列兵，去执行看娃任务。不过，至少在乌勒尔的兵站周围，没有他上一处驻地托尔（Thor）星上那种土著女人惹出的麻烦。

===Original===
An airjeep, coming in out of the sun, circled the crag-top fort and let down onto the terrace next to von Schlichten’s command-car. It carried a bristle of 15 mm machine-guns, and two of the eight 50 mm rocket-tubes on either side were empty and freshly smoke-stained. The duraglass canopy slid back, and the two-man crew﻿—lieutenant-driver and sergeant-gunner﻿—jumped out. Von Schlichten knew them both.

“Lieutenant Kendall; Sergeant Garcia,” he greeted. “Good afternoon, gentlemen.”

Both saluted, in the informal, hell-with-rank-we’re-all-human manner of Terran soldiers on extraterrestrial duty, and returned the greeting.

“How’s the Jeel situation?” he asked, then nodded toward the fired rocket-tubes. “I see you had some shooting.”

===Chinese===
一架飞行吉普（airjeep）从太阳的方向飞来，绕着岩顶要塞盘旋一圈，降到冯·施利希滕指挥车旁边的平台上。车上密密地架着15毫米机枪，两侧各八具50毫米火箭发射管中有两具已经打空，还带着新鲜的烟熏痕迹。杜拉玻璃舱盖向后滑开，两名乘员——驾驶的中尉和操机枪的中士——跳下车来。冯·施利希滕对他们俩都熟。

“肯德尔（Kendall）中尉；加西亚（Garcia）中士，”他招呼道。“下午好，二位。”

两人行了军礼——那种地球士兵在外星执勤时特有的、满不在乎、“军衔见鬼去，咱们都是人”的做派——并回了问候。

“吉尔（Jeel）人的情况怎么样？”他问，接着朝打空的火箭管点点头。“看得出来，交过火了。”

===Original===
“Yes, sir,” the lieutenant said. “Two bands of them. We sighted the first coming up the eastern side of the mountain about two miles this side of the Blue Springs. We got about half of them with M.G.-fire, and the rest dived into a big rock-crevice. We had to use two rockets on them, and then had to let down and pot a few of them with our pistols. We caught the second band in that little punchbowl place about a mile this side of Zortolk’s Old Fort. There were only six of them; they were bunched together, feeding. Off one of their own gang, I’d say; the way we’ve been keeping them up in the high rocks, they’ve been eating inside the family quite a bit, lately. We let them have two rockets. No survivors. Not many very big pieces, in fact. We let down at Zortolk’s for a beer, after that, and Captain Martinelli told us that one of his jeeps caught what he thinks was the same band that was down off the mountain night-before-last and ate those peasants on Prince Neeldink’s estate.”

“By God, I’m glad to hear that!” There’d been a perfect hell of a flap about that business. Before the Terrans came to Uller, it was a good year when not more than five hundred farm-folk would be killed and eaten by Jeel cannibals. The incident of two nights ago had been the first of its kind in almost six months, but the nobleman whose serfs had been eaten was practically accusing the Company of responsibility for the crime. “I’ll see that Neeldink is informed. The more you do for these damned geeks, the more they expect from you.﻿ ﻿… When you get your vehicle re-ammoed, lieutenant, suppose you buzz back to where you machine-gunned that first gang. If there are any more around, they’ll have moved in for the free meal by now.” This breakdown of the Jeels’ taboo against eating fellow-tribesmen was one of the best things he’d heard from the cannibal-extermination project for some time.

===Chinese===
“是，长官，”中尉说。“有两伙。第一伙是在蓝泉（Blue Springs）这边约两英里处发现的，正沿山的东面向上摸来。我们用机枪火力报销了约莫一半，其余的钻进一道大石缝。我们只好朝石缝用了两枚火箭弹，随后又降下去，用手枪点了几个的名。第二伙是在佐托尔克古堡（Zortolk's Old Fort）这边约一英里的那个小盆地里撞上的，只有六个，正挤作一堆进食。依我看，吃的是他们自己同伙；我们把他们困在高处岩山上，近来他们自家窝里相食的事可真不少。我们赏了他们两枚火箭弹。没有活口。说实话，连大块的残骸都没剩下几块。之后我们降去佐托尔克喝了杯啤酒，马蒂内利（Martinelli）上尉说，他手下的一辆吉普截住了一伙——他认为就是前天夜里下山、吃了尼尔丁克亲王（Prince Neeldink）庄园上那些农夫的那一伙。”

“老天在上，这消息听着真叫人痛快！”那桩事此前闹出过一场好大的风波。在地球人来到乌勒尔以前，一年里被食人的吉尔人杀死吃掉的农夫不超过五百，就算得上好年景了。前夜那件事虽是近六个月来的头一遭，可那位农奴被吃的贵族，几乎是摆明了指责公司得为这桩罪行担责。“我会让人知会尼尔丁克。这些该死的土佬，你替他们做得越多，他们指望你的就越多。……中尉，等你们的车补足了弹药，不妨飞回你们用机枪打第一伙的地方转转。周围要是还有别的吉尔人，这会儿准会凑过来吃这顿白食。”吉尔人那条不吃同族的禁忌竟至崩坏——这是他近来从灭食人项目里听到的最好消息之一。

===Original===
He turned to Themistocles M’zangwe. “In about two weeks, get a little task-force together. Say ten combat-cars, about twenty airjeeps, and a battalion of Kragan Rifles in troop-carriers. Oh, yes, and this good-for-nothing Konkrook Fencibles outfit of Prince Jaizerd’s; they can be used for beaters, and to block escape routes.” He turned back to Lieutenant Kendall and Sergeant Garcia. “Good work, boys. And if the synchro-photos show that any of that first bunch got away, don’t feel too badly about it. These Jeels can hide on the top of a pool-table.”

He climbed into the command-car, followed by Themistocles M’zangwe and Hideyoshi O’Leary. Sergeant Harry Quong and Corporal Hassan Bogdanoff took their places on the front seat; the car lifted, turned to nose into the wind, and rose in a slow spiral. Below, the fort grew smaller, a flat-topped rectangle of masonry overlooking the pass, a gun covering each approach, and two more on the square keep to cover the rocky hogback on which the fort had been built, with the flagpole between them. Once that pole had lifted a banner of ragged black marsh-flopper skin bearing the device of the Kragan riever-chieftain whose family had built the castle; now it carried a neat rectangle of blue bunting emblazoned with the wreathed globe of the Terran Federation and, below that, the blue-gray pennant which bore the vermilion trademark of the Chartered Uller Company.

===Chinese===
他转向地米斯托克利·姆赞圭。“大约两个星期之内，组一支小型特遣队。比如说，十辆战斗车（combat-car），二十来架飞行吉普，再用运兵艇装上一个营的克拉冈来复枪团（Kragan Rifles）。哦对了，还有亚泽尔德亲王（Prince Jaizerd）那支一无所用的孔克鲁克义勇军（Konkrook Fencibles）；可以拿他们充当轰赶猎物的角色，再叫他们堵一堵逃路。”他转回身，对肯德尔中尉和加西亚中士说。“干得好，小伙子们。要是同步航拍照片显示第一伙里有漏网的，也别太往心里去。这些吉尔人，连台球桌面上都藏得住身。”

他登进指挥车，地米斯托克利·姆赞圭和秀吉·奥利里跟了上来。哈里·关（Harry Quong）中士和哈桑·博格丹诺夫（Hassan Bogdanoff）下士在前排就座；飞车升起，转过头迎着风，缓缓盘旋着爬高。下方，要塞渐渐缩小，成了一座俯瞰山隘的平顶矩形石堡，每一条进路都有一门炮扼守，方形主堡上还有两门，封锁着建堡的那道石质山脊，旗杆就竖在两炮之间。从前，这根旗杆升起的是一面破破烂烂的黑色沼跳兽（marsh-flopper）皮横旗，绘着建堡家族那位克拉冈劫掠酋长的纹章；如今挂的，是一幅齐整的蓝色旗布，饰着地球联邦的环冠地球徽记，下面另有一面蓝灰色燕尾旗，旗上是特许乌勒尔公司朱红色的商标。

===Original===
“Where now, sir?” Harry Quong asked.

He looked at his watch. Seventeen-hundred; there wasn’t time for a visit to Zortolk’s Old Fort, ten miles to the north at the next pass.

“Back to Konkrook, to the island.”

The nose of the car swung east by south; the cold-jet rotors began humming and then the hot-jets were cut in. The car turned from the fort and the mountains and shot away over the foothills toward the coastal plain. Below were forests, yellow-green with new foliage of the second growing season of the equatorial year, veined with narrow dirt roads and spotted with occasional clearings. Farther east, the dirty gray woodsmoke of Uller marked the progress of the charcoal-burnings. It took forty years to burn the forests clear back to the flint cliffs; by the time the burners reached the mountains, the new trees at the seaward edge would be ready to cut. Off to the south, he could see the dark green squares, where the hemlocks and Norway spruce had been planted by the Company. With a little chemical fertilizer, they were doing well, and they made better charcoal than the silicate-heavy native wood. That was the only natural fuel on Uller; there was no coal, of course, since fallen timber and even standing dead trees petrified in a matter of a couple of years. There was too much silica on Uller, and not enough of anything else; what would be coal-seams on Terra were strata of silicified wood. And, of course, there was no petroleum. There was less charcoal being burned now than formerly; the Uller Company had been bringing in great quantities of synthetic thermoconcentrate-fuel, and had been setting up nuclear furnaces and nuclear-electric power-plants, wherever they gained a foothold on the planet.

===Chinese===
“现在去哪儿，长官？”哈里·关问。

他看了看表。十七点整；来不及再跑北面十英里外下一道山隘的佐托尔克古堡了。

“回孔克鲁克去，回岛上。”

车头转向东偏南；冷喷旋翼嗡嗡转起，接着热喷引擎也点了火。飞车辞别要塞与群山，掠过丘陵地带，朝海岸平原疾驰而去。下方是森林，顶着赤道年第二个生长季的新叶，泛着黄绿色；窄窄的土路如脉络纵横其间，间或缀着几片林间空地。再往东，乌勒尔木材烧出的灰浊柴烟，标示出烧炭作业推进到了哪里。要把森林一路烧回到燧石峭壁，得花四十年工夫；等烧炭人烧到山边，靠海一侧新长的树又到该伐的时候了。往南望去，能看见一块块墨绿色的方格地，那是公司栽种的铁杉和挪威云杉。稍稍施些化肥，它们长势很好，烧出的炭也比硅质浓重的本地木材更胜一筹。这就是乌勒尔唯一的天然燃料；煤当然没有——倒木乃至站立的枯树，不出几年便会石化。乌勒尔的二氧化硅太多，别样东西都太少；在地球（Terra）上本该是煤层的地方，这里全是硅化木地层。石油自然也没有。如今烧出的木炭比从前少了；乌勒尔公司一直在大量运进合成热凝燃料（thermoconcentrate-fuel），而且在这颗行星上凡是立稳脚跟之处，都在建核炉与核电厂。

===Original===
Beyond the forests came the farmlands. Around the older estates, thick walls of flint and petrified wood had been built, and wide moats dug, to keep out the shellosaurs. But now the moats were dry, and the walls falling into disrepair. Some of the newer farms, land devoted to agriculture with the declining demand for charcoal, had neither moats nor walls. That was the Company, too; the huge shell-armored beasts had become virtually extinct in the Konk Isthmus now, since the introduction of bazookas and recoilless rifles. There seemed to be quite a bit of power-equipment working in the fields, and big contragravity lorries were drifting back and forth, scattering fertilizer, mainly nitrates from Mimir or Yggdrasill. There were still a good number of animal-drawn plows and harrows in use, however.

As planets went, Uller was no bargain, he thought sourly. At times, he wished he had never followed the lure of rapid promotion and fantastically high pay and left the Federation regulars for the army of the Uller Company. If he hadn’t, he’d probably be a colonel, at five thousand sols a year, but maybe it would be better to be a middle-aged colonel on a decent planet﻿—Odin, with its two moons, Hugin and Munin, and its wide grasslands and its evergreen forests that looked and even smelled like the pinewoods of Terra, or Baldur, with snow-capped mountains, and clear, cold lakes, and rocky rivers dashing under great vine-hung trees, or Freya, where the people were human to the last degree and the women were so breathtakingly beautiful﻿—than a Company army general at twenty-five thousand on this combination icebox, furnace, wind-tunnel and stonepile, where the water tasted like soapsuds and left a crackly film when it dried; where the temperature ranged, from pole to pole, between two hundred and fifty and minus a hundred and fifty Fahrenheit and the Beaufort-scale ran up to thirty; where nothing that ran or swam or grew was fit for a human to eat, and where the people.﻿ ﻿…

===Chinese===
森林之外是农田。在较老的庄园四周，曾筑起燧石与硅化木的厚墙，挖出宽阔的壕沟，为的是防壳龙（shellosaur）进来。可如今壕沟干涸，墙垣也日渐倾颓。随着烧炭需求衰落，一些改事农耕的新农场，连壕沟带墙一概全无。这也得归功于公司；自从火箭筒和无后坐力炮传入，那种披着厚重甲壳的巨兽，如今在孔克地峡（Konk Isthmus）一带实际上已经绝迹。田里似乎有不少动力机械在作业，大型反重力（contragravity）货车来回飘行，播撒化肥——主要是从弥米尔（Mimir）或伊格德拉西尔（Yggdrasill）运来的硝酸盐。不过，畜力拉的犁和耙，用着的也还不少。

论行星，乌勒尔实在算不上一笔划算的买卖，他悻悻地想。有几回，他真希望自己当初没有中了快速晋升和天价薪酬的诱惑，离开联邦正规军，投到乌勒尔公司的陆军来。倘若没有，他如今多半是个上校，年薪五千索尔（sol）——可没准儿，在一颗像样的行星上做个中年上校，也强过在这座冰箱、火炉、风洞兼碎石堆的四合一行星上，做年薪两万五的公司陆军将军：像奥丁（Odin），有两轮明月胡金（Hugin）与穆宁（Munin），有辽阔的草原，有看上去、连闻起来都像地球松林的常绿森林；像巴德尔（Baldur），有雪冠群山、清冽寒湖，有在巨树垂藤之下冲过乱石的湍急河流；像弗蕾亚（Freya），那里的人是彻头彻尾的人类，女人美得叫人屏息。而在这颗行星上，水喝起来一股肥皂水味，蒸干了还留下一层脆壳似的薄膜；从这极到那极，气温在华氏二百五十度到零下一百五十度之间摆荡，蒲福风级能直上三十级；凡是会跑、会游、会长的东西，没有一样合于人类入口；还有这里的居民……

===Original===
Of course, there were worse planets than Uller. There was Nidhog, cold and foggy, its equatorial zone a gloomy marsh and the rest of the planet locked in eternal ice. There was Bifrost, which always kept the same face turned to its primary; one side blazingly hot and the other close to absolute zero, with a narrow and barely habitable twilight zone between. There was Mimir, swarming with a race of semi-intelligent quasi-rodents, murderous, treacherous, utterly vicious. Or Niflheim. The Uller Company had the franchise for Niflheim, too; they’d had to take that and agree to exploit the planet’s resources in order to get the franchise for Uller, which furnished a good quick measure of the comparative merits of the two.

Ahead, the city of Konkrook sprawled along the delta of the Konk river and extended itself inland. The river was dry, now. Except in spring, when it was a red-brown torrent, it never ran more than a trickle, and not at all this late in the northern summer. The aircar lost altitude, and the hot-jet stopped firing. They came gliding in over the suburbs and the yellow-green parks, over the low one-story dwellings and shops, the lofty temples and palaces, the fantastically twisted towers, following a street that became increasingly mean and squalid as it neared the industrial district along the waterfront.

===Chinese===
当然，比乌勒尔更糟的行星也有。比如尼德霍格（Nidhog），又冷又雾，赤道带是一片阴郁的沼泽，其余地方尽锁在亘古寒冰里。比如比弗罗斯特（Bifrost），永远以同一面朝着它的主星，一面灼热如焚，另一面逼近绝对零度，中间只夹一条勉强宜居的晨昏带。比如弥米尔，遍地滋生着一族半聪明的类啮齿生物，嗜杀、奸诈、凶恶绝伦。再不然，就是尼福尔海姆（Niflheim）。乌勒尔公司在尼福尔海姆也握有特许经营权；当年为了拿下乌勒尔的特许权，公司不得不连那一颗也一并接手，并允诺开发其资源——两颗行星孰优孰劣，由此一望即知。

前方，孔克鲁克城顺着孔克河（Konk River）的三角洲铺展开来，又向内陆伸展。河如今是干的。除了春天化作一道红褐色洪流之外，它一年到头至多一线细流，到了北方夏季这般晚的时候更是彻底断流。飞车降低高度，热喷引擎停止喷气。他们滑翔着掠过郊区和黄绿色的园林，掠过低矮的单层民居与店铺、高耸的寺庙与宫殿、奇形怪状拧成麻花的高塔，沿一条街道飞去——越靠近滨水的工业区，这条街就越发破败腌臜。

===Original===
Von Schlichten, on the right, glanced idly down, puffing slowly on his cigarette. Then he stiffened, the muscles around his right eye clamping tighter on the monocle. Leaning forward, he punched Harry Quong lightly on the shoulder.

“Circle back, sergeant; let’s have a look at that street again,” he directed. “Something going on, down there; looks like a riot.”

“Yes, sir; I saw it,” the Chinese-Australian driver replied. “Terrans in trouble; bein’ mobbed by geeks. Aircar parked right in the bloody middle of it.”

The car made a twisting, banking loop and came back, more slowly. Colonel Hideyoshi O’Leary was using the binoculars.

“That’s right,” he said. “Terrans being mobbed. Two of them, backed up against a house. I saw one of them firing a pistol.”

===Chinese===
坐在右侧的冯·施利希滕缓缓吐着烟，漫不经心朝下瞟了一眼。随即他浑身一僵，右眼四周的肌肉把单片眼镜夹得更紧。他俯身向前，在哈里·关肩上轻轻捶了一记。

“绕回去，中士；那条街咱们再瞧一遍，”他吩咐道。“下面有动静；看着像暴乱。”

“是，长官；我瞧见了，”这位华裔澳大利亚驾驶员答道。“地球人出事了，正叫土佬围着打。一辆飞车偏偏就停在这乱子的正中央。”

飞车压着坡度兜了个圈，放慢速度折返回来。秀吉·奥利里上校正举着双筒望远镜观察。

“没错，”他说。“地球人遭围攻。两个，背抵着一栋房子。我看见其中一个在用手枪射击。”

===Original===
Von Schlichten had the handset of the car’s radio, and was punching out the combination of the Company guardhouse on Gongonk Island; he held down the signal button until he got an answer.

“Von Schlichten, in car over Konkrook. Riot on Fourth Avenue, just off Seventy-second Street.” No Terran could possibly remember the names of Konkrook’s streets; even native troops recruited from outside found the numbers easier to learn and remember. “Geeks mobbing a couple of Terrans. I’m going down, now, to do what I can to help; send troops in a hurry. Kragan Rifles. And stand by; my driver’ll give it to you as it happens.”

The voice of somebody at the guardhouse, bawling orders, came out of the receiver as he tossed the phone forward over Harry Quong’s shoulder; Quong caught it and began speaking rapidly and urgently into it while he steered with the other hand. Von Schlichten took one of the five-pound spiked riot-maces out of the rack in front of him. Themistocles M’zangwe had already drawn his pistol; he shifted it to his left hand and took a mace in his right. The Nipponese-Irish colonel, looking like a homicidally infuriated pixie, had an automatic in one hand and a long dagger in the other.

===Chinese===
冯·施利希滕抓起车载无线电话筒，拨出贡贡克岛（Gongonk Island）公司卫队室的呼号组合；他按住信号键不放，直到有了应答。

“冯·施利希滕，在孔克鲁克上空座车内。第四大道起暴乱，就在第七十二街口外。”地球人根本不可能记住孔克鲁克各条街的名字；连从外地招募来的土著部队，也觉得用编号学起来、记起来更容易。“一伙土佬围攻两名地球人。我这就下去，尽力救援；火速派兵来，派克拉冈来复枪团。守住频道别挂断；我的驾驶员会随时给你们报实况。”

他把话筒越过哈里·关的肩膀往前一丢，听筒里随即传出卫队室里什么人吼着下令的声音；关一把接住，一手把着方向操纵，一手对着话筒急速而紧迫地讲起来。冯·施利希滕从面前的架子上取下一柄五磅重、带尖刺的防暴狼牙棒。地米斯托克利·姆赞圭早已拔出手枪；他把枪换到左手，右手也操起一柄狼牙棒。那位日本-爱尔兰混血上校，模样活像一只杀性大发、怒不可遏的小妖精，一手握着自动手枪，一手握着长匕首。

===Original===
Harry Quong and Hassan Bogdanoff were old Uller hands; they’d done this sort of work before. Bogdanoff rose into the ball-turret and swung the twin 15 mm’s around, cutting loose. Quong brought the car in fast, at about shoulder-height on the mob. Between them, they left a swath of mangled, killed, wounded, and stunned natives. Then, spinning the car around, Quong set it down hard on a clump of rioters as close as possible to the struggling group around the two Terrans. Von Schlichten threw back the canopy and jumped out of the car, O’Leary and M’zangwe behind him.

There was another aircar, a dark maroon civilian job, at the curb; its native driver was slumped forward over the controls, a short crossbow-bolt sticking out of his neck. Backed against the closed door of a house, a Terran with white hair and a small beard was clubbing futilely with an empty pistol. He was wounded, and blood was streaming over his face. His companion, a young woman in a long fur coat, was laying about her with a native bolo-knife.

Von Schlichten’s mace had a spiked ball-head, and a four-inch spike in front of that. He smashed the ball down on the back of one Ulleran’s head, and jabbed another in the rump with the spike.

===Chinese===
哈里·关和哈桑·博格丹诺夫都是乌勒尔的老人手，这类活计从前干过。博格丹诺夫升进球形炮塔，把双联15毫米机枪甩转过来，随即开火。关驾着飞车疾插而入，高度压得差不多与暴民的肩头齐平。两人协力，在土著中间犁出一条血肉横飞的通路——死的死，伤的伤，昏的昏。紧接着关将车身猛地一旋，朝紧挨着那两个地球人身边混战人群的一簇暴民，硬生生砸落下去。冯·施利希滕掀开舱盖，跳下车去，奥利里和姆赞圭紧随其后。

路缘还停着另一辆飞车，一辆深褐红色的民用车；车上的土著驾驶员伏在操纵器上，颈子里插着一支短短的弩矢。一个白发短须的地球人背抵着一扇紧闭的房门，正抡着打空的手枪徒劳地砸挡。他受了伤，鲜血正顺着脸往下淌。他的同伴是个身穿长皮草大衣的年轻女子，正挥着一柄土著波洛刀向四面乱砍。

冯·施利希滕那柄狼牙棒有个带刺的球形锤头，锤头前端还伸出一根四英寸的尖钉。他抡下球锤，砸在一个乌勒尔人的后脑上，又用尖钉捅了另一个的臀部。

===Original===
“*Zak! Zak!*” he yelled, in pidgin-Ulleran. “*Jik-jik*, you lizard-faced Creator’s blunder!”

The Ulleran whirled, swinging a blade somewhere between a big butcherknife and a small machete. His mouth was open, and there was froth on his lips.

“*Znidd suddabit!*” he screamed.

Von Schlichten parried the cut on the steel shaft of his mace. “*Suddabit* yourself, you geek bastard!” he shouted back, ramming the spike-end into the opal-filled mouth. “And *znidd* you, too,” he added, recovering and slamming the ball-head down on the narrow saurian skull. The Ulleran went down, spurting a yellow fluid about the consistency of gun-oil. Then, without wasting words, he maced another of the things.

===Chinese===
“Zak！Zak！”他用洋泾浜乌勒尔语（pidgin-Ulleran）大吼。“Jik-jik，你这蜥蜴脸的造物主败笔！”

那乌勒尔人猛地旋身，挥来一柄刀，说大不大、说小不小，介于大型屠刀与小型砍刀之间。他嘴大张着，唇上满是白沫。

“Znidd suddabit！”他尖叫道。

冯·施利希滕用狼牙棒的钢柄格开这一刀。“你自己才 Suddabit，土佬杂种！”他回吼一声，趁势把尖钉一头直捣进那张塞满乳光牙的嘴。“连你一块儿 znidd。”他补上一句，收棒回势，球锤正砸在那狭长的蜥蜴头骨上。乌勒尔人栽倒在地，喷出一股黄水，稠得跟枪油相仿。随后他不费半句口舌，又一棒撂翻了这东西里的另一个。

===Original===
Ahead, one of the natives had caught the wounded Terran with both lower hands, and was raising a dagger with his upper right. The girl in the fur coat swung wildly, slashing the knife-arm, then chopped down on the creature’s neck. To one side, a native somewhat better dressed than the others, to the extent of a couple of belts with gold ornaments, drew a Terran automatic. Von Schlichten hurled his mace and drew his pistol, thumbing off the safety as he swung it up, but before he could fire, Hassan Bogdanoff had seen and swung his guns around; the double burst caught the native in the chest and fairly tore him apart.

Another of them closed with the girl, grabbing her right arm with all four hands and biting at her; she screamed and kicked her attacker in the groin, where an Ulleran is, if anything, even more vulnerable than a Terran. The native howled hideously, and von Schlichten, jumping over a couple of corpses, shoved the muzzle of his pistol into the creature’s open mouth and pulled the trigger, blowing its head apart like a rotten pumpkin and splashing both himself and the girl with yellow blood and rancid-looking gray-green brains.

Hideyoshi O’Leary, jumping forward after von Schlichten, stuck his dagger into the neck of a rioter and left it there, then caught the girl around the waist with his free arm. Themistocles M’zangwe dropped his mace and swung the frail-looking man onto his back. Together, they struggled back to the command-car, von Schlichten covering the retreat with his pistol. Another rioter﻿—a Zirk nomad from the North, he guessed﻿—was aiming one of the long-barreled native air-rifles, holding the ten-inch globe of the air-chamber in both lower hands. Von Schlichten shot him, and the Zirk literally blew to pieces.

===Chinese===
前方，一个土著用两只下手攥住了那受伤的地球人，右上臂正举起一柄匕首。皮草大衣的姑娘发狠般挥刀，砍中那条持刀臂，接着一刀劈进那东西的脖颈。一旁有个穿戴比同伙略强些的土著——也不过多系两条缀着金饰的腰带——拔出一支地球人的自动手枪。冯·施利希滕掷出狼牙棒，同时拔枪，枪一扬起拇指便扳开了保险；可他还没来得及开火，哈桑·博格丹诺夫已经瞧见，转过了机枪；两道短点射齐齐咬住那土著的胸口，简直把他撕成了两半。

又一个土著缠上那姑娘，四只手一齐抓住她的右臂，张口便咬；她尖叫一声，一脚踹中袭击者的裆部——乌勒尔人那个部位若说与地球人有什么不同，就是更加不禁打。那土著发出一声凄厉骇人的嚎叫，冯·施利希滕跃过两具尸体，把枪口捅进那东西张开的大嘴里扣动扳机，把它的脑袋轰得像只烂南瓜般四分五裂，黄血和馊臭的灰绿色脑浆溅了他和姑娘一身。

秀吉·奥利里紧跟着冯·施利希滕抢上前去，把匕首捅进一个暴民的脖颈便弃之不顾，腾出的那条手臂一把搂住姑娘的腰。地米斯托克利·姆赞圭撂下狼牙棒，把那个看上去孱弱的男人甩上自己的背。两人合力，一路挣扎着退回指挥车，冯·施利希滕用手枪掩护后撤。又有一个暴民——他猜是个北方来的齐尔克（Zirk）游牧民——正端着一支土著的长筒气步枪瞄准，两只下手捧着那只十英寸的球形贮气罐。冯·施利希滕一枪打去，那齐尔克人当场炸了个粉碎。

===Original===
For an instant, he wondered how the small bursting-charge of a 10 mm explosive pistol-bullet could accomplish such havoc, and assumed that the native had been carrying a bomb in his belt. Then another explosion tossed fragmentary corpses nearby, and another and another. Glancing quickly over his shoulder, he saw four combat-cars coming in, firing with 40 mm auto-cannon and 15 mm machine-guns. They swept between the hovels on one side and the warehouses on the other, strafing the mob, darted up to a thousand feet, looped, and came swooping back, and this time there were three long blue-gray troop-carriers behind them.

These landed in the hastily cleared street and began disgorging native Company soldiers﻿—Kragan mercenaries, he noted with satisfaction. They carried a modified version of the regular Terran Federation infantry rifle, stocked and sighted to conform to their physical peculiarities, with long, thorn-like, triangular bayonets. One platoon ran forward, dropped to one knee, and began firing rapidly into what was left of the mob. Four-handed soldiers can deliver a simply astonishing volume of fire, particularly when armed with auto-rifles having twenty-shot dropout magazines which can be changed with the lower hands without lowering the weapon.

There was a clatter of shod hoofs, and a company of the King of Konkrook’s cavalry came trotting up on their six-legged, lizard-headed, quartz-speckled mounts. Some of these charged into side alleys, joyfully lancing and cutting down fleeing rioters, while others dismounted, three tossing their reins to a fourth, and went to work with their crossbows. Von Schlichten, who ordinarily entertained a dim opinion of the King of Konkrook’s soldiery, admitted, grudgingly, that it was smart work; four hands were a big help in using a crossbow, too.

===Chinese===
有一瞬他直纳闷，一枚10毫米爆炸枪弹里那点小小的炸药，怎么造成得出这般浩劫，还只当那土佬腰带里揣着炸弹。紧接着，又一记爆炸把残碎的尸体抛上近旁半空，跟着又是一记、又一记。他飞快回头一瞥：四辆战斗车正破空而来，40毫米自动炮和15毫米机枪一齐开火。它们贴着一侧的棚户区和另一侧的货栈横扫暴民，一掠而上蹿升到一千英尺，翻个圈又俯冲回来——这一回，它们身后还跟着三艘长长的蓝灰色运兵艇。

运兵艇降落在仓促清出的街面上，开始吐出公司的土著士兵——克拉冈雇佣兵，他满意地看在眼里。他们扛着地球联邦制式步兵步枪的改型，枪托和瞄具都依他们的身体特点改造过，配着长长的、荆棘似的三角刺刀。一个排跑步上前，单膝跪地，朝暴民的残余部分快速开火。四手士兵倾泻出的火力着实惊人，尤其当他们装备的自动步枪配着二十发直落式弹匣、下面的两只手不必放低枪身就能换弹匣的时候。

一阵钉蹄铁的嘚嘚声，孔克鲁克王的一队骑兵骑着六条腿、蜥蜴头、缀满石英斑点的坐骑小跑而来。一部分人马冲进小巷，兴高采烈地挺矛捅翻、挥刀砍翻四散奔逃的暴民；另一部分下马，三个把缰绳丢给第四个，随即操起弩弓上阵。冯·施利希滕平日对孔克鲁克王的兵卒评价甚低，此刻也不得不勉为其难地承认：干得漂亮；用弩的时候，四条手臂同样帮了大忙。

===Original===
A Terran captain of native infantry came over, saluting.

“Are you and your people all right, general?” he asked.

Von Schlichten glanced at the front seat of his car, where Harry Quong, a pistol in his right hand, was still talking into the radiophone, and Hassan Bogdanoff was putting fresh belts into his guns. Then he saw that the Graeco-African brigadier and the Irish-Japanese colonel had gotten the wounded man into the car. The girl, having dropped her bolo, was leaning against the side of the car, one foot heedlessly in what was left of an Ulleran who had gotten smashed under it, weak with nervous reaction.

“We seem to be, Captain Pedolsky. Very smart work; you must have those vehicles of yours on hyperspace-drive.﻿ ﻿… How is he, colonel?”

“We’d better get him to the hospital, right away,” O’Leary replied. “I think he has a concussion.”

“Harry, call the hospital. Tell them what the score is, and tell them we’re bringing the casualty in to their top landing stage.﻿ ﻿… Why, we’ll make out very nicely, captain. You’d better stay around with your Kragans and make sure that these geeks of King Jaikark’s don’t let the riot flare up again and get away from them. And don’t let them get the impression that they can maintain order around here without our help; the Company would like to see that attitude discouraged.”

===Chinese===
一名统领土著步兵的地球人上尉走了过来，行了个礼。

“您和您的人都还好吧，将军？”他问。

冯·施利希滕朝车子的前排座瞥了一眼：哈里·关右手握着手枪，还在对着无线电话筒讲话；哈桑·博格丹诺夫正往机枪里换新弹链。接着他看见，那位希腊-非洲裔准将和爱尔兰-日本混血上校已经把伤员弄进了车里。那姑娘撂下了波洛刀，正倚着车身，一只脚浑不在意地踩在一个被飞车砸烂在下面的乌勒尔人残躯里，神经反应发作，浑身发软。

“我们都算平安，佩多尔斯基（Pedolsky）上尉。干得真利落；你们那些载具准是装了超空间引擎。……他怎么样，上校？”

“我们最好马上送他去医院，”奥利里答道。“我看他是脑震荡。”

“哈里，给医院打电话。把情况报清楚，告诉他们我们把伤员送到他们的顶层降落台。……啊，我们会安排得很妥当，上尉。你最好带着你的克拉冈兵守在这一带，盯住贾伊卡克王（King Jaikark）手下的这些土佬，别让暴乱死灰复燃、反脱出他们的掌控。还有，别让他们生出念头，以为没有我们帮忙，他们也能在这一带维持秩序；公司乐见这种苗头被扼杀掉。”

===Original===
“Yes, sir, I understand.” Captain Pedolsky opened the pouch on his belt and took out the false palate and tongue-clicker without which no Terran could do more than mouth a crude and barely comprehensible pidgin-Ulleran. Stuffing the gadget into his mouth, he turned and began jabbering orders.

Von Schlichten helped the girl into the car, placing her on his right. The wounded civilian was propped up in the left corner of the seat, and Colonel O’Leary and Brigadier-General M’zangwe took the jump-seats. The driver put on the contragravity-field, and the car lifted up.

“Them, see if there’s a flask and a drinking-cup in the door pocket next to you,” he said. “I think Miss Quinton could use a drink.”

The girl turned. Even in her present disheveled condition, she was beautiful﻿—a trifle on the petite side, with black hair and black eyes that quirked up oddly at the outer corners. Her nails were black-lacquered and spotted with little gold stars, evidently a new feminine fad from Terra.

===Chinese===
“是，长官，明白。”佩多尔斯基上尉打开腰带上的皮囊，取出假腭和击舌器——少了这两样，任何地球人至多只能咕哝出一嘴粗劣得勉强可辨的洋泾浜乌勒尔语。他把那小玩意儿塞进口中，转身叽里呱啦地发号施令起来。

冯·施利希滕扶那姑娘上车，让她坐在自己右边。受伤的平民斜支在座位的左角，奥利里上校和姆赞圭陆军准将坐进折叠座椅。驾驶员打开反重力场，飞车升了起来。

“地米，看看你身旁的车门袋里有没有小酒瓶和杯子，”他说。“我想昆顿（Quinton）小姐需要喝上一口。”

姑娘转过脸来。即便眼下这副蓬乱狼狈的模样，她依然是美的——身量略嫌娇小，黑发黑眼，眼梢向外奇异地微微上翘。她的指甲漆成黑色，缀着点点小金星，显然是地球上传来的新式女子风尚。

===Original===
“I certainly could, general.﻿ ﻿… How did you know my name?”

“You’ve been on Uller for the last three months; ever since the *City of Canberra* got in from Niflheim. On Uller, there aren’t enough of us that everybody doesn’t know all about everybody else. You’re Dr. Paula Quinton; you’re an extraterrestrial sociographer, and you’re a field-agent for the Extraterrestrials’ Rights Association, like Mohammed Ferriera, here.” He took the cup and flask from Themistocles M’zangwe and poured her a drink. “Take this easy, now; Baldur honey-rum, a hundred and fifty proof.”

He watched her sip the stuff cautiously, cough over the first mouthful, and then get the rest of it down.

“More?” When she shook her head, he stoppered the flask and relieved her of the cup. “What were you doing in that district, anyhow?” he wanted to know. “I’d have thought Mohammed Ferriera would have had more sense than to take you there, or go there, himself, for that matter.”

===Chinese===
“我确实需要，将军。……您怎么会知道我的名字？”

“你来乌勒尔已经三个月了；打「堪培拉城」号（City of Canberra）从尼福尔海姆抵港那时起。在乌勒尔，我们人少得很——谁的底细谁都一清二楚。你是保拉·昆顿（Paula Quinton）博士，地外社会志学者（extraterrestrial sociographer），还是地外种族权利协会（Extraterrestrials' Rights Association）的实地干事，就跟这儿的穆罕默德·费雷拉（Mohammed Ferriera）一样。”他从地米斯托克利·姆赞圭手里接过杯子和酒瓶，给她斟了一杯。“慢着点喝；这是巴德尔蜜糖朗姆酒，一百五十标准度。”

他看着她小心地呷了一口，头一口呛得咳嗽起来，随后还是把余下的咽了下去。

“再来一点？”见她摇头，他便塞好瓶塞，把杯子从她手里接了过去。“说起来，你们到底到那个街区去做什么？”他问道。“我原以为穆罕默德·费雷拉会更有分寸，不至于带你去那种地方——就说他自己，也不该去。”

===Original===
“We went to visit a friend of his, a native named Keeluk, who seems to be a sort of combination clergyman and labor leader,” she replied. “I’m going to observe labor conditions at the North Pole mines in a short while, and Mr. Keeluk was going to give me letters of introduction to friends of his at Skilk.”

With the aid of his monocle, von Schlichten managed to keep a straight face. Neither M’zangwe nor O’Leary had any such aid; the African rolled his eyes and the Japanese-Irishman grimaced.

“We talked with Mr. Keeluk for a while,” the girl said, “and when we came out, we found that our driver had been killed and a mob had gathered. Of course, we were carrying pistols; they’re part of this survival-kit you make everybody carry, along with the emergency-rations and the water-desilicator. Mr. Ferriera’s wasn’t loaded, but mine was. When they rushed us, I shot a couple of them, and then picked up that big knife.﻿ ﻿…”

“That’s why you’re still alive,” von Schlichten commented.

“We wouldn’t be if you hadn’t come along,” she told him. “I never in my life saw anything as beautiful as you coming through that mob swinging that war-club!”

“Well, I never saw anything much more beautiful than those 40 mm’s beginning to land in the mob,” von Schlichten replied.

===Chinese===
“我们去拜访他的一位朋友，一个名叫基卢克（Keeluk）的土著，看模样是个神职人员掺着劳工领袖的混合体，”她答道。“过不了多久，我要去北极矿区考察劳工状况，基卢克先生打算给我写几封介绍信，写给他斯基尔克（Skilk）的朋友。”

亏得有单片眼镜遮掩，冯·施利希滕总算绷住了一张正经脸。姆赞圭和奥利里可没有这等凭借；非洲裔的那位直翻白眼，日本-爱尔兰混血的那位挤出了一脸怪相。

“我们和基卢克先生谈了一阵，”姑娘说，“出来的时候才发现，我们的驾驶员被杀了，还聚起来了一伙暴民。当然，我们随身带着手枪；那是你们强制人人携带的生存包里的一部分，跟应急口粮和脱硅净水器放在一起。费雷拉先生的那支没装子弹，我的装了。他们朝我们猛扑的时候，我打翻了两三个，然后就抄起了那把大刀。……”

“正因如此，你现在还活着，”冯·施利希滕评了一句。

“可要不是你们赶到，我们早没命了，”她对他说。“我这辈子再没见过比那更美的场面——你抡着那柄战棒，从暴民堆里杀开一条路过来！”

“哦，我也再没见过比那美到哪里去的场面——那些40毫米炮弹开始落进暴民堆里的时候，”冯·施利希滕答道。

===Original===
The aircar swung out over Konkrook Channel and headed toward the blue-gray Company buildings on Gongonk Island, and the Company airport, swarming with lorries and airboats, where the ten thousand-ton *Oom Paul Kruger* had just come in from Keegark, and the Company’s one real warship, the cruiser *Procyon*, was lifting out for Grank, in the North. Down at the southern tip of the island, the three-thousand-foot globe of the spaceship *City of Pretoria*, from Niflheim, was loading with cargo for Terra.

“Just what happened, while you and Mr. Ferriera were in Keeluk’s house. Miss Quinton?” Hideyoshi O’Leary asked, trying not to sound official. “Was Keeluk with you all the time? Or did he go out for a while, say fifteen or twenty minutes before you left?”

“Why, yes, he did.” Paula Quinton looked surprised. “How did you guess it? You see, a dog started barking, behind the house, and he excused himself and.﻿ ﻿…”

“A dog?” von Schlichten almost shouted. The other officers echoed him, and on the front seat, Harry Quong said, “Coo-bli’me!”

“Why, yes.﻿ ﻿…” Paula Quinton’s eyes widened. “But there are no dogs on Uller, except a few owned by Terrans. And wasn’t there something about﻿ ﻿… ?”

===Chinese===
飞车兜了个弯，掠出孔克鲁克水道（Konkrook Channel），朝贡贡克岛上蓝灰色的公司楼群和公司机场飞去。机场上货车与飞艇（airboat）穿梭如蚁，万吨级的「奥姆·保罗·克鲁格」号刚从基加克（Keegark）抵港，公司唯一一艘真正的军舰——「南河三」号巡洋舰——正拔升北去，飞往格兰克（Grank）。在岛屿南端，那艘从尼福尔海姆来的三千英尺球形飞船「比勒陀利亚城」号（City of Pretoria），正在装运发往地球的货物。

“你和费雷拉先生在基卢克家里的时候，究竟都发生了些什么，昆顿小姐？”秀吉·奥利里问，竭力不让口气听着像公事公办。“基卢克一直陪着你们吗？还是中间出去过一阵——比如说，在你们动身前十五二十分钟？”

“咦，是出去过。”保拉·昆顿面露讶色。“您怎么猜着的？是这样的，屋后有条狗叫了起来，他说声失陪就……”

“狗？”冯·施利希滕几乎喊出声来。其余军官异口同声，前排座位上，哈里·关冒出一句：“乖乖，我的老天！”

“咦，是狗啊……”保拉·昆顿睁大了眼睛。“可是乌勒尔没有狗啊，除了地球人养的那几条。还有，之前不是闹过一阵……？”

===Original===
Von Schlichten had the radiophone and was calling the command car at the scene of the riot. The sergeant-driver answered.

“Von Schlichten here; my compliments to Captain Pedolsky, and tell him he’s to make immediate and thorough search of the house in front of which the incident occurred, and adjoining houses. For his information, that’s Keeluk’s house. Tell him to look for traces of Governor-General Harrington’s collie, or any of the other terrestrial animals that have been disappearing﻿—that goat, for instance, or those rabbits. And I want Keeluk brought in, alive and in condition to be interrogated. I’ll send more troops, or Constabulary, to help you.” He handed the phone to M’zangwe. “You take care of that end of it, Them; you know who can be spared.”

“But, what﻿ ﻿… ?” the girl began.

“That’s why you were attacked,” he told her. “Keeluk was afraid to let you get away from there alive to report hearing that dog, so he went out and had a gang of thugs rounded up to kill you.”

“But he was only gone five minutes.”

“In five minutes, I can put all the troops in Konkrook into action. Keeluk doesn’t have radio or TV﻿—we hope﻿—but he has his forces concentrated, and he has a pretty good staff.”

===Chinese===
冯·施利希滕抓起无线电话，呼叫暴乱现场的那辆指挥车。接电话的是驾驶中士。

“我是冯·施利希滕；向佩多尔斯基上尉致意，告诉他立即彻底搜查事发现场正前方那栋房子和两侧相邻的宅子。供他参考：那是基卢克的房子。让他查找哈灵顿总督（Governor-General Harrington）那条牧羊犬的踪迹，或者其他任何一直失踪的地球动物的踪迹——比方说那只山羊，或者那几只兔子。还有，我要基卢克押解归案，要活的，要还能受审的。我会再派些部队，或者治安队，过去帮你们。”他把话筒递给姆赞圭。“这一头就交给你了，地米；哪些人抽得开身，你清楚。”

“可是，这到底……？”姑娘刚要开口。

“这正是你们遇袭的原因，”他告诉她。“基卢克怕你们活着离开那里，把听到那条狗的事报出去，所以出去纠集了一帮打手来杀你们。”

“可他前后只走了五分钟。”

“五分钟，足够我把孔克鲁克的全部部队都发动起来。基卢克没有无线电，也没有电视——但愿如此——可他的兵力是集中起来的，手底下还有一批相当像样的班底。”

===Original===
“But Mr. Keeluk’s a friend of ours. He knows what our Association is trying to do for his people.﻿ ﻿…”

“So he shows his appreciation by setting that mob on you. Look, he has a lot of influence in that section. When you were attacked, why wasn’t he out trying to quiet the mob?”

“When they jumped you, you tried to get back into the house,” M’zangwe put in. “And you found the door barred against you.”

“Yes, but.﻿ ﻿…” The girl looked troubled; M’zangwe had guessed right. “But what’s all the excitement about the dog? What is it, the sacred totem-animal of the Uller Company?”

“It’s just a big brown collie, named Stalin, like half the dogs on Terra. Somebody stole it, and Keeluk was keeping it, and we want to know why. We don’t like geek mysteries; not when they lead to murderous attacks on Terrans, at least.”

===Chinese===
“可基卢克先生是我们的朋友呀。他清楚我们协会正努力为他的族人做些什么……”

“于是他就拿煽动暴民围攻你们来报答这份情。你想，他在那个街区颇有势力。你们遇袭的时候，他怎么没出来设法平息暴民？”

“他们扑向你们的时候，你们想退回那栋房子里去，”姆赞圭插话道。“结果发现门从里头闩死了。”

“是的，可是……”姑娘神色惶惑；姆赞圭猜了个正着。“可一条狗有什么好这样兴师动众的？它是什么，特许乌勒尔公司的神圣图腾兽吗？”

“不过是一条棕色的大牧羊犬，名字叫斯大林，跟地球上的一半狗重名。有人把它偷了出来，基卢克一直养着它，我们想知道为什么。我们不喜欢土佬打哑谜；至少，当哑谜引出对地球人的凶杀围攻时，我们不喜欢。”

===Original===
The aircar let down on the hospital landing stage. A stretcher was waiting, with a Terran interne and two Ulleran orderlies. They got the still-unconscious Mohammed Ferriera out of the car.

“You’d better go with them, yourself, Miss Quinton,” von Schlichten advised. “You have a couple of nasty-looking bruises and bumps. A couple of abrasions, too, where those geeks grabbed you; they have hides like sandpaper. And better have that coat cleaned, before that goo on it hardens, or it’ll be ruined.”

“Yes. You have a lot of it on your uniform, too.”

He glanced down at the blue-gray jacket. “So I have. And another thing. Those letters Keeluk was going to give you, the ones to his friends in Skilk. Did you get them?”

She felt in the pocket of her coat. “Yes. I still have them.”

“I wish you’d let Colonel O’Leary have a look at them. There may be more to them than you think.﻿ ﻿… Hid, will you go with Miss Quinton?”

===Chinese===
飞车降落在医院的降落台上。一副担架早已候着，旁边是一名地球人实习医生和两名乌勒尔护理员。他们把仍昏迷不醒的穆罕默德·费雷拉从车里抬了出来。

“你自己最好也跟他们去，昆顿小姐，”冯·施利希滕劝道。“你身上有几处瘀伤肿块，瞧着不轻。还有几处擦伤，是那些土佬抓的；它们的皮跟砂纸一样。还有，那件大衣得赶紧送去洗，趁上面的黏糊玩意儿还没结硬，不然就毁了。”

“是呢。您制服上也沾了不少。”

他低头看了看蓝灰色的军上衣。“可不是嘛。还有一桩事。基卢克原本要给你的那几封信，给他斯基尔克的朋友们的那些。你拿到手了吗？”

她在皮草大衣的口袋里摸了摸。“拿到了。还带在我身上。”

“我希望你把信交给奥利里上校过目一下。那里的名堂，也许比你想的多。……希德（Hid），你陪昆顿小姐去好吗？”

## IX Don’t Push Them Anywhere Put Them Back in the Bottle / 第九章 别把它们推到任何地方，把它们装回瓶子里

===Original===
He looked at his watch, and stood for a moment, pumping the stale air and tobacco-smoke of the telecast station out of his lungs, as the light airjeep let down into the street. Oh-one-fifteen﻿—two hours and a half since the mutiny at the native-troops barracks had broken out. The Company reservation was still ablaze with lights, and over the roof of the hospital and dispensary and test-lab he could see the glare of the burning barracks. There was more fire-glare to the south, in the direction of the mine-equipment park and the mine-labor camp, and from that direction the bulk of the firing was to be heard.

The driver, a young lieutenant who seemed to be of predominantly Malayan and Polynesian blood, slid back the duraglass canopy for him to climb in, then snapped it into place when he had strapped himself into his seat.

“Can you handle the armament, sir?” he asked.

Von Schlichten nodded approvingly. Not a very flattering question, but the boy was right to make sure, before they started out.

“I’ve done it, once or twice,” he understated. “Let’s go; I want a look at what’s going on down at the equipment-park and the labor-camp, first.”

===Chinese===
他看了看表，趁着轻巧的飞行吉普（airjeep）降入街上的当口，站了片刻，把电视广播站里那股浑浊空气连同烟草烟气从肺里排出去。凌晨一时十五分——距土著部队兵营哗变爆发，已经过去两个半小时了。公司特区（Company Reservation）依旧灯火通明；越过医院、诊疗所和试验室的屋顶，他能望见燃烧的兵营映出的火光。南面还有更多的火光，在采矿设备场和劳工营的方向，枪声也大半从那边传来。

驾驶员是个年轻中尉，看相貌，血统以马来和波利尼西亚成分为主。他滑开杜拉玻璃（duraglass）舱盖，请冯·施利希滕（von Schlichten）登机，待他在座椅上系好安全带，又啪的一声把舱盖扣严。

「您能操纵机载武器吗，长官？」他问。

冯·施利希滕赞许地点点头。这问题算不得恭维，可出发前先问个明白，这小伙子做得对。

「干过一两回。」他说得轻描淡写。「走吧；先去设备场和劳工营那边，看看那边的情形。」

===Original===
They lifted up, the driver turning the nose of the airjeep in the direction of the flames and explosions and magnesium-lights to the south and tapping his booster-button gently. The vehicle shot forward and came floating in over the scene of the fighting. The situation-map at the improvised headquarters had shown a mixture of pink and white pills in the mine-equipment park; something was going to have to be done about the lag in correcting it, for the area was entirely in the hands of loyal Company troops, and the mob of laborers and mutinous soldiers had been pushed back into the temporary camp where the workers had been gathered to await transportation to the Arctic. As he feared, the rioting workers, many of whom were trained to handle contragravity equipment, had managed to lift up a number of dump-trucks and powershovels and bulldozers, intending to use them as improvised airtanks, but Jarman’s combat-cars had gotten on the job promptly and all of these had been shot down and were lying in wreckage, mostly among the rows of parked mining-equipment.

From the labor-camp, a surprising volume of fire was being directed against the attack which had already started from the retaken equipment-park. This was just another evidence of the failure of Intelligence and the Constabulary﻿—and consequently of himself﻿—to anticipate the brewing storm. There was, of course, practically no chance of keeping Ullerans from having native weapons, swords, knives, even bows and air-rifles, and a certain number of Volund-made trade-quality automatic pistols could be expected, but most of the fire was coming from military rifles, and now and then he could see the furnace-like backflash of a recoilless rifle or a bazooka, or the steady flicker of a machine-gun. Even if a few of these weapons had been brought from the barracks by retreating Tenth Infantry or Fifth Cavalry mutineers, there were still too many.

Hovering above the fighting, aloof from it, he saw six long troop-carriers land and disgorge Kragan Rifles who had been released by the liquidation of resistance at the native-troops barracks. A little later, two airtanks floated in, and then two more, going off contragravity and lumbering on treads to fire their 90 mm rifles. At the same time, combat-cars swooped in, banging away with their lighter auto-cannon and launching rockets. The titanium prefab-huts, set up to house the laborers and intended to be taken north with them for their stay on the polar desert, were simply wiped away. Among the wreckage, resistance was being blown out like the lights of a candelabrum. Push the white pills out, girls, he thought. Don’t push them anywhere; put them back in the bottle. This year, there wouldn’t be any mining done at the North Pole; next year, the stockholders’ll be bitching about their dividend-checks. And a lot of new machine operators are going to have to be trained for next year’s mining. If there is any mining, next year.

===Chinese===
他们腾空而起，驾驶员把飞行吉普的机头对准南面的火光、爆炸和镁光照明，轻轻点了点助推按钮。飞行器向前窜出，飘到战地上空。临时指挥部里的态势图上，采矿设备场一带还标着粉、白两色混杂的丸标；地图更新竟如此滞后，得想法子解决了，因为该地区其实已完全在忠于公司的部队手里，那群劳工和哗变士兵也已被推回临时营地——工人们本是聚在那里，等候运往北极的。正如他所担心的，闹事的工人中有许多人受过操纵反重力（contragravity）设备的训练，果然设法弄飞了若干辆自卸卡车、电铲和推土机，打算拿它们充当应急的空中坦克（airtank），好在贾曼（Jarman）上校的战斗车（combat-car）及时赶到，把这些家伙尽数击落，残骸大多倒在成排停放的采矿设备之间。

劳工营方向射出的火力，量大得惊人，压向已从收复的设备场发起的进攻。这不过是又一个证据，证明情报部门和宪兵队（Constabulary）——因而也是他本人——没能预见这场酝酿中的风暴。当然，想让乌勒尔人（Ulleran）手里连土产武器都没有——长刀、短刀，甚至弓和气步枪——那是根本办不到的，市面上再混进若干沃隆德（Volund）产的贸易级自动手枪，也属意料之中；可眼下大部分火力出自军用步枪，还不时可见无后坐力炮或巴祖卡火箭筒那熔炉般的口焰回闪，或是机枪连绵不断的闪动火舌。退一万步说，就算这些武器有一部分是第十步兵团或第五骑兵团的哗变兵撤出兵营时带出来的，数量也还是太多了。

他悬停在战斗上空、置身事外，只见六架长长的运兵艇降落，吐出克拉冈来复枪团（Kragan Rifles）的士兵——土著部队兵营的抵抗一经肃清，他们便腾出了手。不多时，两辆空中坦克飘了进来，接着又是两辆，它们关掉反重力，靠履带隆隆碾进，以 90 毫米炮开火。与此同时，战斗车俯冲而至，用较轻的自动机关炮砰砰猛轰，并发射火箭。那些为安置劳工而搭起、原打算随他们北上、供在极地荒原驻留之用的钛制预制棚屋，就这样被整个抹掉了。残骸之间，抵抗之火如枝形烛台上的盏盏灯火，被逐一吹灭。把白丸往外推呀，姑娘们，他心想。别把它们推到任何地方，把它们装回瓶子里去。今年，北极那边是采不成矿了；明年，股东们该对着红利支票骂娘了。而且，为着明年的采矿，还得训练一大批新的机械操作手——如果明年还有采矿这回事的话。

===Original===
He took up the hand-phone and called H.Q.

“Von Schlichten, what’s the wavelength of the officer in command at the equipment-park?”

A voice at the telecast station furnished it; he punched it out.

“Von Schlichten, right overhead. That you, Major Falkenberg? Nice going, major, how are your casualties?”

“Not too bad. Twenty or thirty Kragans and loyal Skilkans, and eight Terrans killed, about as many wounded.”

“Pretty good, considering what you’re running into. Get many of your Kragans mounted on those hipposaurs?”

===Chinese===
他拿起手持话机，接通司令部。

「我是冯·施利希滕，设备场指挥官用的波长是多少？」

电视广播站里的一个声音报了出来；他按键调好。

「冯·施利希滕，就在你们头顶上。是法尔肯贝格（Falkenberg）少校吗？打得漂亮，少校，你们伤亡如何？」

「不算太糟。克拉冈人和忠于我们的斯基尔克人折了二三十个，地球人（Terran）阵亡八名，负伤的也差不多这个数。」

「考虑到你们撞上的是什么局面，这算好的了。你的克拉冈人有多少骑上河马龙（hipposaur）了？」

===Original===
“About a hundred, a lot of ’saurs got shot, while we were leading them out from the stables.”

“Well, I can see geeks streaming away from the labor-camp, out the south end, going in the direction of the river. Use what cavalry you have on them, and what contragravity you can spare. I’ll drop a few flares to show their position and direction.”

Anticipating him, the driver turned the airjeep and started toward the dry Hoork River. Von Schlichten nodded approval and told him to release flares when over the fugitives.

“Right,” Falkenberg replied. “I’ll get on it at once, general.”

“And start moving that mine-equipment up into the Company area. Some of it we can put into the air; the rest we can use to build barricades. None of it do we want the geeks getting hold of, and the equipment-park’s outside our practical perimeter. I’ll send people to help you move it.”

“No need to do that, sir; I have about a hundred and fifty loyal North Ullerans﻿—foremen, technicians, overseers﻿—who can handle it.”

===Chinese===
「差不多一百个。把它们从棚圈里往外牵的时候，不少龙被打死了。」

「是这样，我看见土佬（geek）正一股股地撤出劳工营，从南端往外涌，朝河的方向去了。手头有多少骑兵，就朝他们用多少；反重力载具能匀出多少，就出多少。我会投几枚照明弹，标示他们的位置和去向。」

驾驶员早料到了这一着，已调转飞行吉普，朝干涸的霍尔克河（Hoork River）飞去。冯·施利希滕赞许地点点头，吩咐他飞到逃亡者上空时投放照明弹。

「明白，」法尔肯贝格答道，「我马上就办，将军。」

「还有，把那些采矿设备往公司区里搬。一部分可以弄上天去；剩下的可以用来筑街垒。这些东西一件也不能让土佬拿到手，而设备场在我们的实际防线之外。我会派人帮你搬。」

「不必了，长官；我手下约有一百五十名忠于我们的北乌勒尔人——工头、技术员、监工——他们搬得动。」

===Original===
“All right. Use your own judgment. Put the stuff back of the native-troops barracks, and between the power-plant and the Company office-buildings, and anywhere else you can.” The lieutenant nudged him and pushed a couple of buttons on the dashboard.

“Here go the flares, now.”
