“行，这套话我还可以拿去试试迈耶斯坦。为了银行卡特尔在这场买卖里的损失，他正急得六神无主。……好，一有新情况我再给你去电话。”

===Original===
By 03:30, it was daylight; the attacks against the northwest corner of the perimeter stopped entirely. Wallingsby had the three-hundred-odd Skilkan laborers at work; he had gathered up all the tarpaulin he could find, and had the two sewing-machines in the tentmaker’s shop running on sandbags. Jules Keaveney, to von Schlichten’s agreeable surprise, had taken hold of his A.R.P. assignment, and was doing an efficient job in organizing for firefighting, damage-control and first aid. Colonel Jarman had his airjeeps and combat-cars working in ever-widening circles over the countryside, shooting up everything in sight that even looked like contragravity equipment. Some of these patrols had to be recalled, around 10:30, when sporadic nuisance-sniping began from the side of the mountain to the west. And, along with everything else, Paula Quinton managed, along with her other work, to get a complete digest prepared of the situation elsewhere in the Terran-occupied parts of the planet.

The situation at Konkrook was brightening steadily. The second wave of Kankad’s improvised airlift, reinforced by contragravity from Konkrook, had come in; there were now close to two thousand fresh Kragans on Gongonk Island and the mainland farms, Kankad himself with them. The *Aldebaran* had reached Kankad’s Town, and was loading another thousand Kragans.﻿ ﻿… There was nothing more from Keegark. A message from Colonel MacKinnon had come in at dawn, to the effect that the geeks had penetrated his last defenses and that he was about to blow up the Residency; thereafter Keegark went off the air.﻿ ﻿… By 07:30, the *Northern Star* had landed the regiment Murderers, armed with first-quality Terran infantry-rifles and a few machine-guns and bazookas, at the Palace at Krink, and by 08:45 she had returned with another regiment, the Jeel-Feeders. The three-lane street connecting the Palace and the Residency had been widened to six, and then to eight.﻿ ﻿… Guido Karamessinis, at Grank, was still at uneasy peace with King Yoorkerk, who was still undecided whether the rebels or the Company were going to be the eventual victors, and afraid to take any irrevocable step in either direction.﻿ ﻿… Eight men and four women, the survivors of a trading-station on the eastern shore of Takkad Sea, reached Konkrook in a lorry; another trading station, on the south shore, reported by telecast that the natives there had refused to rise against them, and had crucified five of Rakkeed’s disciples who had come among them preaching *znidd suddabit*.

===Chinese===
到03时30分，天已大亮；对防线西北角的攻击完全停止了。沃林斯比把三百来名斯基尔克劳工发动了起来；他搜罗了能找到的全部篷布，让帐篷铺里的两台缝纫机赶制沙袋。令冯·施利希滕又惊又喜的是，朱尔斯·基夫尼也接手了他那份防空差事，把消防、损管和急救的组织工作做得井井有条。贾曼上校的飞行吉普和战斗车在四郊一圈圈越兜越远地巡逻，见到任何看着像反重力装备的东西就扫射。大约10时30分，西面山坡上开始响起零零星星的骚扰冷枪，有一部分巡逻队只得召回。而在百忙之中，保拉·昆顿还见缝插针，让人备好了一份完整的简报，汇总这颗行星上地球占领区其他各地的情况。

孔克鲁克的局势正稳步好转。坎卡德那支临时拼凑的空运机队的第二波，加上孔克鲁克方面反重力载具的加强，已经抵达；贡贡克岛和大陆农场上如今已有将近两千名生力克拉冈人，坎卡德本人在其中。「毕宿五」号已抵达坎卡德城，正在装载又一千名克拉冈人。……基加克方面再无消息。拂晓时收到麦金农上校发来的电文，大意是土佬已突破他最后的防线，他将炸毁总督府；此后基加克便音讯断绝。……到07时30分，「北极星」号已将杀人团（Murderers）运抵克林克王宫登陆——该团装备着一色的上好地球步枪，外加几挺机枪和几具火箭筒——到08时45分，它又运来了另一个团，食吉尔团（Jeel-Feeders）。连接王宫与总督府的三车道大街已拓宽成六车道，继而又拓宽成八车道。……格兰克的圭多·卡拉梅西尼斯仍与约克克王维持着不安的和平；这位国王仍在盘算，起义者和公司到底谁是最后的赢家，不敢朝任何一个方向迈出不可挽回的一步。……八男四女——塔卡德海东岸一处商站的幸存者——乘一辆卡车抵达孔克鲁克；南岸另一处商站通过电视广播报告，当地土著拒绝起来攻打他们，还把混进去传扬 znidd suddabit 的拉基德门徒中的五个钉上了十字架。

===Original===
At 11:00, Paula Quinton and Barney Mordkovitz virtually ordered him to get some sleep. He went to his quarters at Company House, downed a spaceship-captain’s-size drink of honey-rum, and slept until 16:00. As he dressed and shaved, he could hear, through the open window, the slow sputter of small-arms’ fire, punctuated by the occasional *whump-whump-whump* of 40 mm auto-cannon or the hammering of a machine-gun.

Returning to his command-post at the telecast station, the terrain-board showed that the perimeter of defense had been pushed out in a bulge at the northwest corner; the TV-screen pictured a crude breastwork of petrified tree-trunks, sandbags, mining machinery, packing-cases and odds-and-ends, upon which Wallingsby’s native laborers were working under guard while a skirmish-line of Kragans had been thrown out another four or five hundred yards and were exchanging potshots with Skilkans on the gullied hillside.

“Where’s Colonel Quinton?” he asked. “She ought to be taking a turn in the sack, now.”

===Chinese===
11时整，保拉·昆顿和巴尼·莫德科维茨几乎是勒令他去睡觉。他回到公司大楼的住处，灌下一杯飞船船长分量的蜜糖朗姆酒，一觉睡到16时。穿衣刮脸的时候，他能听见开着的窗外轻武器时断时续的噼啪声，间或夹着40毫米机关炮咚咚咚的轰响，或是机枪的哒哒声。

回到电视广播站的指挥所，地形板显示防御圈在西北角向外推成了一块凸出部；电视屏幕上是一道粗糙的胸墙，用石化树干、沙袋、采矿机械、包装箱和杂七杂八的东西堆成，沃林斯比的土著劳工在警戒下继续修筑，一列克拉冈人散兵线又前出四五百码，正与冲沟纵横的山坡上的斯基尔克人对射冷枪。

“昆顿上校在哪儿？”他问。“这会儿该轮到她上床睡一觉了。”

===Original===
“She’s taking one,” Major Falkenberg, who had commanded the action at the native-troops barracks and the labor-camp, the night before, told him. “General Mordkovitz chased her off to bed a couple of hours ago, called me in to take her place, and then went out to replace me. Colonel Guilliford’s in the hospital; got hit about thirteen hundred. They’re afraid he’s going to lose a leg.”

“That’s a bloody shame!” He pointed to the northwest corner of the perimeter on the screen. “Whose idea was that?” he asked. “It’s a good one; I ought to have thought of it, myself.”

“Your new adjutant,” Falkenberg grinned. “She asked somebody what those big domes, up there, were. When they told her there were ten thousand tons of thermoconcentrate, five thousand tons of blasting-explosives, and five tons of plutonium, under them, she damned near fainted, and then she ordered that, right away.”

===Chinese===
“她正睡着呢。”前一夜指挥土著部队营房和劳工营战斗的法尔肯贝格少校告诉他。“两个钟头前，莫德科维茨将军把她撵去睡觉，把我叫来顶她的班，然后他自己又出去顶我的班。吉利福德上校进了医院；大约十三点挂的彩。他们担心他那条腿保不住。”

“真惨！”他指着屏幕上防线的西北角。“这是谁的主意？”他问。“好主意；我自己本该想到的。”

“您那位新副官。”法尔肯贝格咧嘴一笑。“她问人家，那边那几个大圆顶是什么。人家告诉她，底下是一万吨热凝燃料、五千吨爆破炸药，外加五吨钚，她差点儿当场晕过去，然后立刻下了那道命令。”

===Original===
More reports came in. The entire garrison of the small Residency at Kwurk, the most northern of the eastern shore Free Cities, had arrived at Kankad’s Town in two hundred-foot contragravity scows and five aircars. Two of the aircars arrived half an hour behind the rest of the refugee flotilla, having turned off at Keegark to pay their respects to King Orgzild. They reported the Keegark Residency in ruins, its central buildings vanished in a huge crater; the *Jan Smuts* and the *Christiaan De Wett* were still in the Company docks, both apparently damaged by the blast which had destroyed the Residency. One of the aircars had rocketed and machine-gunned some Keegarkans who appeared to be trying to repair them; the other blew up King Orgzild’s nitroglycerine plant. Von Schlichten called Konkrook and ordered a bombing-mission against Keegark organized, to make sure the two ships stayed out of service.

The *Northern Star* was still bringing loyal troops into Krink. King Jonkvank, whom von Schlichten called, was highly elated.

“We are killing traitors wherever we find them!” he exulted. “The city is yellow with their blood; their heads are piled everywhere! How is it with you at Skilk?”

===Chinese===
新的报告接连送来。库尔克——东岸自由城邦中最靠北的那座——小型总督府的全体驻军，已乘两艘百英尺长的反重力驳船和五辆飞车抵达坎卡德城。其中两辆飞车比难民船队的其余船只晚了半个钟头才到——它们中途拐到基加克，向奥格齐尔德王致了致意。它们报告说：基加克总督府已成废墟，中央建筑群消失在一个巨大的弹坑里；「扬·斯穆茨」号和「克里斯蒂安·德韦特」号仍停在公司船坞，看来都被摧毁总督府的那场爆炸所伤。其中一辆飞车用火箭和机枪收拾了一伙看样子正想修理这两艘船的基加克人；另一辆则炸飞了奥格齐尔德王的硝化甘油厂。冯·施利希滕接通孔克鲁克，下令组织一次对基加克的轰炸，务使那两艘船彻底趴窝。

「北极星」号仍在往克林克运送效忠的部队。冯·施利希滕致电琼克万克王，对方兴高采烈。

“我们正四处诛杀叛贼，见一个杀一个！”他欢欣鼓舞。“全城都被他们的血染黄了；他们的脑袋堆得到处都是！你们在斯基尔克战况如何？”

===Original===
“We have killed many, also,” von Schlichten boasted. “And tonight, we will kill more; we are preparing bombs of great destruction, which we will rain down upon Skilk until there is not one stone left upon another, or one infant of a day’s age left alive!”

Jonkvank reacted as he was intended to. “Oh, no, general, don’t do all that!” he exclaimed. “You promised me that I should have Skilk, on the word of a Terran. Are you going to give me a city of ruins and corpses? Ruins are no good to anybody, and I am not a Jeel, to eat corpses.”

Von Schlichten shrugged. “When you are strong, you can flog your enemies with a whip; when you are weak, all you can do is kill them. If I had five thousand more troops, here.﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
“我们也杀了许多。”冯·施利希滕吹嘘道。“今夜我们还要杀更多；我们正在酿造大威力的炸弹，要让它们如雨点般落在斯基尔克头上，直到没有一块石头还叠在另一块上，没有一个出生才一天的婴孩还活着！”

琼克万克的反应正如他所料。“哦，别，将军，千万别那样！”他叫起来。“你以地球人的信义答应过我，斯基尔克归我。你要给我的难道是一座废墟和尸体的城？废墟对谁都没用，我又不是吉尔人，不吃尸体。”

冯·施利希滕耸耸肩。“你强大的时候，可以拿鞭子抽你的敌人；你弱小的时候，就只剩下杀了。要是我这儿再多五千兵……”

===Original===
“Oh, I will send troops, as soon as I can,” Jonkvank hastened to promise. “All my best regiments: the Murderers, the Jeel-Feeders, the Corpse-Reapers, the Devastators, the Fear-Makers. But, now that we have stopped this sinful rebellion, here, I can’t take chances that it will break out again as soon as I strip the city of troops.”

Von Schlichten nodded. Jonkvank’s argument made sense; he would have taken a similar position, himself.

“Well, get as many as you can over here, as soon as possible,” he said. “We’ll try to do as little damage to Skilk as we can, but﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
“哦，我一定派兵，尽快派。”琼克万克忙不迭地许诺。“把我最好的团全派来：杀人团、食吉尔团、收尸团（Corpse-Reapers）、蹂躏团（Devastators）、造惧团（Fear-Makers）。只是这桩罪孽的叛乱刚刚在此平定，我总不能冒险——一从城里抽走部队，它马上再闹起来。”

冯·施利希滕点点头。琼克万克的理由站得住；换了他自己，也会是同样的立场。

“那么，能调多少就尽量多调些过来，越快越好。”他说。“我们会尽量少毁斯基尔克，不过……”

===Original===
At 18:30, Paula joined him for her breakfast, while he sat in front of the big screen, eating his dinner. There had been light ground-action along the southern end of the perimeter﻿—King Firkked’s regulars, reinforced by Zirk tribesmen and levies of townspeople, all of whom seemed to have firearms, were filtering in through the ruins of the labor-camp and the wreckage of the equipment-park﻿—and there was renewed sniping from the mountainside. The long afternoon of the northern autumn dragged on; finally, at 22:00, the sun set, and it was not fully dark for another hour. For some time, there was an ominous quiet, and then, at 00:30, the enemy began attacking in force, driving herds of livestock﻿—lumbering six-legged brutes bred by the North Ullerans for food﻿—to test the defenses for electrified wire and land-mines. Most of these were shot down or blown up, but a few got as far as the wire, which, by now, had been strung and electrified completely around the perimeter.

Behind them came parties of Skilkan regulars with long-handled insulated cutters; a couple of cuts were made in the wire, and a section of it went dead. The line, at this point, had been rather thinly held; the defenders immediately called for air-support, and Jarman ordered fifteen of his remaining twenty airjeeps and five combat-cars into the fight. No sooner were they committed than the radar on the commercial airport control-tower picked up air vehicles approaching from the north, and the air-raid sirens began howling and the searchlights went on.

===Chinese===
18时30分，保拉来陪他吃她的早餐，他则坐在大屏幕前吃晚餐。防线南端有过一阵小规模地面交火——菲尔克德王的正规军得到齐尔克部民和城市民丁的补充，人手似乎都有火器，正从劳工营的废墟和装备场的残骸间渗进来——山坡上的冷枪也重新响起。北国之秋漫长的下午磨磨蹭蹭地挨过去；到22时，太阳终于落山，又一个钟头之后天才全黑。有一阵子，是一种不祥的寂静，然后，00时30分，敌人大举进攻，驱赶着一群群牲畜——北乌勒尔人饲养作肉用的、六条腿的笨重巨兽——去试探防线有没有电网和地雷。这些牲畜大多被打死或炸飞，但有几头一直拱到了铁丝网跟前；此时，环整个防线一圈，铁丝网已经架设完毕并通了电。

牲畜后面跟着一队队斯基尔克正规军，扛着长柄绝缘剪；铁丝网被剪开两道口子，有一段断了电。这一段的守线兵力本来就比较单薄；守军立即呼叫空中支援，贾曼便把手上剩下的二十辆飞行吉普中的十五辆和五辆战斗车投入战斗。它们刚一投入，民用机场指挥塔上的雷达就发现飞行器从北面逼近，空袭警报器随即嚎叫起来，探照灯也亮了。

===Original===
As a protection from the sudden fury of the summer and winter gales, the buildings were all low, thick-walled, and provided with steel doors and window-shutters which were electrically operated and centrally controlled. These slammed shut in every occupied building. The contragravity which had been sent to support the ground-defense at the south side of the Reservation turned to meet this new threat, and everything else available, including the four heavy airtanks, lifted up. Meanwhile, guns began firing from the ground and from rooftops.

There had been four aircars, ordinary passenger vehicles equipped with machine-guns on improvised mounts, and ten big lorries converted into bombers, in the attack. All the lorries, and all but one of the makeshift fighter-escort, were shot down, but not before explosive and thermoconcentrate bombs were dumped all over the place. One lorry emptied its load of thermoconcentrate-bombs on the control-building at the airport, starting a raging fire and putting the radar out of commission. A repair-shop at the ordnance-depot was set on fire, and a quantity of small-arms and machine-gun ammunition piled outside for transportation to the outer defenses blew up. An explosive bomb landed on the roof of the building between Company House and the telecast station, blowing a hole in the roof and demolishing the upper floor. And another load of thermoconcentrate, missing the power-plant, set fire to the dry grass between it and the ruins of the native-troops barracks.

===Chinese===
为了抵御夏冬两季狂风的骤然肆虐，这里的房屋一律低矮、厚墙，装有电动集中控制的钢门和钢窗板。各个有人建筑的门窗轰然关死。原本派去支援特区南侧地面防御的反重力部队掉头迎击这一新威胁，其余一切能升空的，包括那四辆重型空中坦克，全都升了空。与此同时，地面和房顶上的火炮开了火。

参加这次进攻的，有四辆飞车——普通客车装上临时枪架、架起机枪——和十辆改装成轰炸机的大卡车。所有卡车，连同那支临时拼凑的战斗机护航队里除一架之外的全部，都被打了下来，但在那之前，爆炸弹和热凝燃料弹已经扔得满处都是。一辆卡车把整车的热凝燃料弹倾泻在机场指挥楼上，燃起熊熊大火，雷达就此瘫痪。军械库的一间修理车间起了火，堆在外面准备运往外围防线的一批轻武器和机枪弹药炸上了天。一发爆炸弹落在公司大楼与电视广播站之间的那幢建筑顶上，把屋顶炸开一个大洞，上层楼面整个掀塌。还有一车热凝燃料没能命中发电厂，却点燃了发电厂与土著部队营房废墟之间的枯草。

===Original===
Before the air-attack had been broken up, the soldiers of King Firkked and their irregular supporters were swarming through the dead section of wire. They had four or five big farm-tractors, nuclear-powered but unequipped with contragravity-generators, which they were using like ground-tanks of the First Century. This attack penetrated to the middle of the Reservation before it was stopped and the attackers either killed or driven out; for the first time since daybreak, the red-and-yellow lights came on around the power-plant.

As soon as the combined air and ground attack was beaten off, von Schlichten ordered all his available contragravity up, flying patrols around the Reservation and retaliatory bombing missions against Skilk, and began bombarding the city with his 90 mm guns. A number of fires broke out, and at about 02:00 a huge expanding globe of orange-red flame soared up from the city.

“There goes Firkked’s thermoconcentrate stock,” he said to Paula, who was standing beside him in front of the screen.

===Chinese===
空袭尚未被打散，菲尔克德王的士兵和他们的非正规后备队已经从断电的那段铁丝网蜂拥而入。他们弄来四五台大型农用拖拉机——核动力，却没装反重力发生器——把它们当第一世纪的地面坦克使。这一路攻击一直打到特区中部才被遏止，来犯者或死或被逐出；破晓以来头一回，发电厂四周亮起了红黄警示灯。

空地联合攻势一被打退，冯·施利希滕便下令所有可用的反重力载具升空，绕特区飞行巡逻，并出动报复性轰炸斯基尔克，同时开始用90毫米炮轰击城区。多处起火，大约02时，一团不断膨胀的橙红色火焰巨球从城里腾空而起。

“菲尔克德的热凝燃料库存报销了。”他对站在身边、同看屏幕的保拉说。

===Original===
Half an hour later, he discovered that he had been overly optimistic. Much of the enemy’s supply of Terran thermoconcentrate had been destroyed, but enough remained to pelt the Reservation and the Company buildings with incendiaries, when a second and more severe air-attack developed, consisting of forty or fifty makeshift lorry-bombers and fifteen aircars. The previous attack von Schlichten had viewed in the screen at the telecast station; it was his questionable good fortune to observe the second one directly, having been out inspecting the defenses around the ordnance-depot at the time.

Like the first, the second air-attack was beaten off, or, more exactly, down. Most of the enemy contragravity was destroyed; at least two dozen vehicles crashed inside the Reservation. As in the first instance, there was a simultaneous ground attack from the southern side, with a demonstration-attack at the north end. For a while, von Schlichten found himself fighting hand-to-hand, first with his pistol and then, when his ammunition was gone, with a picked-up rifle and bayonet. It was full daylight before the last of the attackers was either killed or driven out.

Five minutes later, while he was reloading his pistol-clips with salvaged cartridges, the *Northern Star* came bulking over the mountains from the west.

===Chinese===
半个钟头后，他发现自己过于乐观了。敌人的地球产热凝燃料虽已被毁掉大半，但剩下的仍足够用来向特区和公司建筑倾泻燃烧弹——第二波更猛烈的空袭来了，由四五十辆改装卡车轰炸机和十五辆飞车组成。上一波空袭，冯·施利希滕是在电视广播站的屏幕里看着度过的；这一波他则有幸——或者说不幸——亲临其境，因为当时他正出去视察军械库周围的防线。

与第一波一样，第二波空袭也被打退了，或者更确切地说，被打了下来。敌方反重力载具大半被毁；至少二十几架坠毁在特区之内。与第一波如出一辙，南面同时发起地面进攻，北端伴以佯攻。有一阵子，冯·施利希滕发现自己陷入了肉搏，先用手枪，弹药打光后，又抓起一支步枪上刺刀干。直到天色大亮，最后一批来犯者才或死或逃。

五分钟后，他正往手枪弹匣里装拣来的子弹，「北极星」号黑压压地翻过西面的群山，压了过来。

## XIV The Reviewers Panned Hell Out of It / XIV 评论家们把它骂了个狗血淋头

===Original===
He woke with a guilty start and looked up at the clock on the ceiling; it was 09:45. Kicking himself free of the covers, he slid his feet to the floor and sprinted for the bathroom. While he was fussing to get the shower adjusted to the right temperature, he bludgeoned his conscience by telling himself that a wide-awake general is more good than a half-asleep general, that there was nothing he could do but hope that Hargreaves’s patrols would keep the bomb away from Konkrook until Pickering’s brain-trust came up with one of their own, and that the fact that the commander-in-chief was making sack-time would be much better for morale than the spectacle of him running around in circles. He shaved carefully; a stubble of beard on his chin might betray the fact that he was worried. Then he dressed, put his monocle in his eye, and called the headquarters that had been set up in Sid Harrington’s﻿—now his﻿—office. A girl at the switchboard appeared on his screen, and gave place to Paula Quinton, who had been up for the past two hours.

“The *Northern Lights* got in about three hours ago, general,” she told him. “She had four of King Yoorkerk’s infantry regiments aboard﻿—the Seventh, Glorious-and-Terrible, the Fourth, Firm-in-Adversity, the Second, Strength-of-the-Throne, and the Twelfth, Forever-Admirable. They’re the sorriest-looking rabble I ever saw, but Hideyoshi says they’re the best Yoorkerk has, and they all have Terran-style rifles. General M’zangwe broke them into battalions, and put a battalion in with each of the Kragan regiments. I think they’re more afraid of the Kragans than they are of the rebels.”

He nodded. That was probably the best way to employ them, within the existing situation. The trouble was, Them M’zangwe was incurably tactical-minded. Put those geeks of Yoorkerk’s in with the Kragans and they’d be most useful in conquering Konkrook, but the trouble was that, after associating with Kragans, they might develop into reasonably good troops themselves, to the undesired improvement of King Yoorkerk’s army. On the other hand maybe not. Keep them in Company service long enough, and they might want to forget about Yoorkerk and stay there.

===Chinese===
他带着愧疚猛然惊醒，抬头看天花板上的钟：09:45。他一脚蹬开被子，双脚落地，直奔浴室。他一边手忙脚乱地把淋浴调到合适的温度，一边揉搓着自己的良心，对自己说：清醒的总司令比半睡半醒的总司令更有用；他此刻无能为力，只能指望哈格里夫斯的巡逻部队能把那颗炸弹挡在孔克鲁克之外，直到皮克林的智囊团也造出一颗来；而且，总司令安心睡觉，总比看着他团团乱转更提振士气。他仔细地刮了脸；下巴上冒出的胡茬，也许会泄露他心绪不宁。然后他穿好衣服，把单片眼镜扣进眼眶，呼叫设在席德·哈灵顿的——如今是他的——办公室里的司令部。屏幕上先出现接线台的一个姑娘，随即让位给保拉·昆顿——她已经起来两个钟头了。

「『北极光』号大约三小时前到了，将军。」她告诉他，「船上载着约克克王的四个步兵团——第七团『荣威可畏』（Glorious-and-Terrible）、第四团『坚忍不移』（Firm-in-Adversity）、第二团『王权砥柱』（Strength-of-the-Throne），还有第十二团『永受赞颂』（Forever-Admirable）。他们是我见过最寒碜的一群乌合之众，不过秀吉说，这已经是约克克拿得出手的精锐了，而且他们全装备着地球式步枪。姆赞圭将军把他们拆编成营，每个克拉冈团里掺进一个营。我看，他们与其说怕叛军，不如说更怕克拉冈人。」

他点了点头。在当前局势下，这大概是使用他们的最好办法。麻烦在于，地米·姆赞圭是个无可救药的战术脑瓜。把约克克的那帮土佬放进克拉冈人中间，攻打孔克鲁克时他们最能派上用场；但问题在于，跟克拉冈人混久了，他们自己说不定也能练成相当像样的部队，让约克克王的军队得到不该有的长进。不过，也可能不会。让他们在公司手下干得够久，他们或许就把约克克忘到脑后，赖着不走了。

===Original===
“How’s the situation over in town?” he asked.

“Well, it’s slowing up, since we began pulling contragravity out,” she told him, “but the geeks are breaking up rapidly.﻿ ﻿… Oh, there was something funny about that hassle, last evening, when the *Procyon* came in. Two contragravity vehicles, an aircar and an air-lorry, that went out to meet the ship, are unaccounted for.”

“You mean two of our vehicles are missing?”

She shook her head, frowning in perplexity. “Well, no. All the vehicles that answered that unidentified-aircraft alert returned, but there were these two that went out that we haven’t any record of. Colonel Grinell is investigating, but he can’t find out anything.﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「城里的局势怎么样？」他问。

「这个，自从我们开始把反重力艇撤出来，进度就慢下来了。」她告诉他，「不过土佬们垮得很快。……哦，昨天傍晚『南河三』号抵达时那场乱子里，有件事挺蹊跷。有两辆反重力载具——一辆飞车、一辆空中卡车——出去接船，下落不明。」

「你是说，我们有两辆载具失踪了？」

她摇头，困惑地皱着眉。「倒也不是。应不明飞行器警报出动的载具全都回来了，偏偏这两辆出去过的，我们却没有任何记录。格里内尔上校正在查，可什么都查不出来。……」

===Original===
“Tell him not to waste any more time,” he said. “Those two were probably geeks from Konkrook. You know, that’s how the von Schlichten family got out of Germany, in the Year Three﻿—flew a bomber to Spain. The Konkrook war-criminals are getting out before the Army of Occupation moves in.”

“Well, the posts at the old Kragan castles report some contragravity, and parties riding ’saurs, moving west from the city,” she told him. “There are a lot of refugees on the roads. And combat reports from Konkrook agree that resistance is getting weaker every hour.﻿ ﻿… And the supra-atmosphere observation-craft﻿—they’re beginning to call her the *Sky-Spy*﻿—is up a hundred and fifty miles over Keegark. We have radar and vision screens and telemetered radiation and other detectors here, tuned to her. They’re installing a similar set on the *Northern Lights* at the shipyard. By the way, Air-Commodore Hargreaves wants to know if he can take a pair of 155 mm rifles from the Channel Battery and mount them on the *Lights*.”

“Yes, of course, he can have anything he wants, as long as it isn’t urgently needed for the bomb project.”

===Chinese===
「告诉他别再浪费时间了。」他说，「那两辆八成是孔克鲁克的土佬。知道吗，当年冯·施利希滕家就是这么逃出德国的——在『第三年』（Year Three），开着一架轰炸机飞去了西班牙。孔克鲁克的战争罪犯们，这是赶在占领军进城之前溜之大吉呢。」

「这个，旧克拉冈城堡的各驻点报告，有一些反重力载具，还有骑河马龙的队伍，正从城里往西去。」她告诉他，「路上难民成群。孔克鲁克方面的战斗报告一致认为，抵抗每小时都在减弱。……还有，那艘超大气层观测艇——大家开始管它叫『天谍』（Sky-Spy）号——正悬在基加克上空一百五十英里处。我们这里有雷达屏、可视屏，还有遥测辐射和其他探测设备，全都对准了它。船厂正在给『北极光』号装一套类似的设备。对了，哈格里夫斯空军准将想知道，能不能从海峡炮台调两门 155 毫米炮，装到『北极光』号上。」

「当然可以，他想要什么都行，只要不是炸弹工程急着要用的。」

===Original===
“*Sky-Spy* reports normal contragravity traffic between Keegark and the farming-villages around﻿—aircars, lorries, a few scows﻿—but nothing suspicious. No trace of either of the Boer-class ships. Kankad’s people are building receiving sets to install on the *Procyon* and the *Aldebaran*, and another set for Kankad’s Town. Pickering and his people are still working, but they all look pretty frustrated. They have Major Thornton, at the ammunition plant, doing experimental work on chemical-explosive charges to bring the subcritical masses together and hold them together till an explosion can be produced; they’re using most of the skilled electrical and electronics people to work up a detonating device. That’s why Kankad’s people are doing most of the detection-device work. Hargreaves is fitting a lot of small craft﻿—combat-cars and civilian aircars﻿—with radar sets, to use for patrolling.”

“That sounds good,” von Schlichten said. “I’ll be around and see how things are, after I’ve had some breakfast.”

He had breakfast at the main cafeteria, four floors down; there wasn’t as much laughing and talking as usual, but the crowd there seemed in good spirits. He spent some time at headquarters, watching Keegark by TV and radar. So far, nothing had been done about direct reconnaissance over Keegark with radiation-detectors, but Hargreaves reported that a couple of privately owned aircars were being fitted for the job.

===Chinese===
「『天谍』号报告，基加克与其周围农业村落之间的反重力交通一切正常——飞车、卡车，还有几艘驳船——没有可疑迹象。两艘布尔级船都毫无踪影。坎卡德的人正在制造接收设备，准备装到『南河三』号和『毕宿五』号上，另外还要给坎卡德城装一套。皮克林和他的人还在干，但个个都显得挺泄气。他们让弹药厂的桑顿少校做化学炸药实验，想办法把亚临界质量块压到一起、箍到一起，直到能引发爆炸；大部分熟练的电气和电子技术人员都调去研制起爆装置了。所以探测设备的活儿，大半都由坎卡德的人在干。哈格里夫斯正在给一大批小型艇——战斗车和民用飞车——装雷达，用来巡逻。」

「听着不错。」冯·施利希滕说，「我吃过早饭，就到处转转看看情况。」

他在楼下四层的主食堂吃了早饭；谈笑不如往常那么多，但那里的人们情绪似乎不错。他在司令部待了一阵，用电视和雷达监视基加克。到目前为止，携辐射探测器直接飞临基加克上空侦察的事还没有着落，不过哈格里夫斯报告说，正在给两辆私人飞车加装设备，准备承担这项任务。

===Original===
He made a flying inspection trip around the island, and visited the farms south of the city, on the mainland, and, finally, made a sweep in the command-car over the city itself. Reconnaissance in person was an archaic and unprogressive procedure, and it was a good way to get generals killed, but one could see a lot of things that would be missed on TV. He let down several times in areas that had already been taken, and talked to company and platoon officers. For one thing, King Yoorkerk’s flamboyantly named regiments weren’t quite as bad as Paula had thought. She’d been spoiled by the Kragans in her appreciation of other native troops. They had good, standard-quality, Volund-made arms; they were brave and capable; and they had been just enough insulted by being integrated into Kragan regiments to try to make a good showing.

By noon, resistance in the city was beginning to cave in. Surrender flags were appearing on one after another of the Konkrookan rebel strong-points, and at 14:30, after he had returned to the Island, a delegation, headed by the Konkrookan equivalent of Lord Mayor and composed largely of prominent merchants, came across the channel under a flag of truce to surrender the city’s Spear of State, with abject apologies for not having Gurgurk’s head on the point of it. Gurgurk, they reported, had fled to Keegark by air the night before, which explained the incident of the unaccountable aircar and lorry. The Channel Battery stopped firing, and, with the exception of an occasional spatter of small-arms fire, the city fell silent.

At 16:00, von Schlichten visited the headquarters Pickering had set up in the office building at the power-plant. As he stepped off the lift on the third floor, a girl, running down the hall with her arms full of papers in folders, collided with him; the load of papers flew in all directions. He stooped to help her pick them up.

===Chinese===
他乘飞艇绕岛做了一圈飞行视察，去了大陆上城南的农场，最后又坐指挥车在城池上空兜了一圈。亲自侦察是套陈旧而不合时宜的老程序，也是让将军们送命的好办法，可有些东西，电视上是看不出来的。他在已攻占的地段降落了几次，同连排军官们交谈。至少有一点：约克克王那些名号花哨的团，并不像保拉想的那么糟。她是被克拉冈人惯坏了眼光，才这么低估其他土著部队。他们装备着优质标准的沃隆德造武器；他们勇敢干练；而且，被编进克拉冈团这件事恰好刺痛了他们，人人憋着劲要露一手。

到中午，城里的抵抗开始崩塌。孔克鲁克叛军的一个个据点上相继升起了投降旗。14:30，他回到岛上之后，一支代表团打着白旗渡过水道而来——为首的是孔克鲁克城里地位相当于市长大人的角色，成员多为商界名流——前来交出城市的国矛，并为矛尖上没有挑着古古尔克的人头连连赔罪。据他们报告，古古尔克前一夜已乘飞行器逃往基加克，这就解开了那辆飞车和卡车下落不明之谜。海峡炮台停止了射击；除了偶尔一阵轻武器零响，全城归于沉寂。

16:00，冯·施利希滕视察了皮克林在发电厂办公楼里设立的总部。他刚走出三楼的电梯，一个怀抱文件夹的姑娘从走廊里奔跑过来，一头撞在他身上；那摞纸片四散飞舞。他弯腰帮她拾捡。

===Original===
“Oh, general! Isn’t it wonderful?” she cried. “I just can’t believe it!”

“Isn’t what wonderful?” he asked.

“Oh, don’t you know? They’ve got it!”

“Huh? They have?” He gathered up the last of the big envelopes and gave them to her. “When?”

===Chinese===
「哦，将军！这难道不棒吗？」她叫道，「我简直不敢相信！」

「什么棒不棒的？」他问。

「哦，您还不知道？他们搞成了！」

「嗯？搞成了？」他拾起最后几个大信封递给她。「什么时候？」

===Original===
“Just half an hour ago. And to think, those books were around here all the time, and.﻿ ﻿… Oh, I’ve got to run!” She disappeared into the lift.

Inside the office, one of Pickering’s engineers was sitting on the middle of his spinal column, a stenograph-phone in one hand and a book in the other. Once in a while, he would say something into the mouthpiece of the phone. Two other nuclear engineers had similar books spread out on a desk in front of them; they were making notes and looking up references in the *Nuclear Engineers’ Handbook*, and making calculations with their sliderules. There was a huddle around the drafting-boards, where two more such books were in use.

“Well, what’s happened?” he demanded, catching Pickering by the arm as he rushed from one group to another.

===Chinese===
「就在半个钟头前。想想看，那些书一直就在这儿摆着，而且……哦，我得赶紧走了！」她一头钻进电梯，不见了。

办公室里，皮克林手下的一名工程师正坐在自己的尾椎骨上，一手握着速记电话，一手拿着书，不时对着话筒说上几句。另外两名核工程师面前摊着同样的书；他们一边做笔记，一边翻查《核工程师手册》，用计算尺演算。绘图板旁也围着一群人，那里还有两本同样的书在使用中。

「喂，出什么事了？」他一把抓住正从一群人奔向另一群人的皮克林，问道。
===Original===
“Ha! We have it!” Pickering cried. “Everything we need! Look!”

He had another of the books under his arm. He held it out to von Schlichten, and von Schlichten suddenly felt sicker than he had ever felt since, at the age of fourteen, he had gotten drunk for the first time. He had seen men crack up under intolerable strain before, but this was the first time he had seen a whole roomful of men blow their tops in the same manner.

The book was a novel﻿—a jumbo-size historical novel, of some seven or eight hundred pages. Its dust-jacket bore a slightly-more-than-bust-length picture of a young lady with crimson hair and green eyes and jade earrings and a plunging﻿—not to say power-diving﻿—neckline that left her affiliation with the class of Mammalia in no doubt whatever. In the background, a mushroom-topped smoke-column rose, and away from it something intended to be a four-motor propeller-driven bomber of the First Century was racing madly. The title, he saw, was *Dire Dawn*, and the author was one Hildegarde Hernandez.

===Chinese===
「哈！我们有了！」皮克林叫道，「我们要的东西全都有了！瞧！」

他腋下还夹着一本同样的书。他把它递给冯·施利希滕，冯·施利希滕顿时感到一阵反胃，其程度之烈，自他十四岁头一回喝醉以来未有过。崩溃于不堪重负之下的人，他见过；可满满一屋子人集体失态发疯，这还是头一遭。

这本书是部小说——一部大开本的历史小说，约有七八百页。护封上印着一位年轻女士及胸稍上的画像：绯红的头发，碧绿的眼睛，翡翠耳环，还有一道深不见底——简直称得上俯冲轰炸式——的领口，让人对她的哺乳纲出身毫无疑义。背景里，一根蘑菇状烟柱冉冉升起，一驾意在表现第一世纪四引擎螺旋桨轰炸机的东西正没命地逃离烟柱。他看清了书名——《凶险的黎明》（Dire Dawn），作者是某位希尔德加德·埃尔南德斯（Hildegarde Hernandez）。

===Original===
“Well, it has a picture of an A-bomb explosion on it,” he agreed.

“It has more than that; it has the whole business. Case specifications, tampers, charge design, detonating device, everything. Why, the end-papers even have diagrams, copies of the original Nagasaki-bomb drawings. Look.”

Von Schlichten looked. He had no more than the average intelligent layman’s knowledge of nuclear physics﻿—enough to recharge or repair a conversion-unit﻿—but the drawings looked authentic enough. They seemed to be copies of ancient blueprints, lettered in First Century English, with Lingua Terra translations added, and marked **Top Secret** and **U.S. Army Corps of Engineers** and **Manhattan Engineering Distric**.

===Chinese===
「这个，封面上确实画着原子弹爆炸。」他承认。

「何止是画了张图；它把全套家伙都写齐了。弹壳规格、反射层、装药设计、起爆装置，应有尽有。喏，衬页上甚至还有图——长崎原子弹原始图纸的副本。瞧。」

冯·施利希滕看了看。他的核物理知识，不比一般聪明的门外汉多多少——够给能量转换装置充充电、修一修罢了——可这些图纸看上去相当地道。它们像是古老蓝图的副本，标注用的是第一世纪的英文，附有地球语译文，还印着「绝密」（Top Secret）、「美国陆军工程兵部队」（U.S. Army Corps of Engineers）和「曼哈顿工程区」（Manhattan Engineering Distric）的字样。

===Original===
“And look at this!” Pickering opened at a marked page and showed it to him. “And this!” He opened where another slip of paper had been inserted. “Everything we want to know, practically.”

“I don’t get this.” He wasn’t sick, anymore, just bewildered. “I read some reviews of this thing. All the reviewers panned hell out of it﻿—‘World War II Through a Bedroom Keyhole’; ‘Henty in Black Lace Panties’﻿—that sort of thing.”

“Yeh, yeh, sure,” Pickering agreed. “But this Hernandez had illusions of being a great serious historical novelist, see. She won’t try to write a book till she’s put in years of research﻿—actually, about six months’ research by a herd of librarians and college-juniors and other such literary coolies﻿—and she boasts that she never yet has been caught in an error of historical background detail.

===Chinese===
「再瞧这个！」皮克林翻到夹了记号的一页给他看。「还有这个！」他又翻到另一处夹着纸条的地方。「我们想知道的，差不多全有了。」

「这我可弄不明白了。」他不再恶心，只剩下一头雾水。「这东西的书评我读过几篇，评论家们把它骂了个狗血淋头——『从卧室钥匙孔里看第二次世界大战』啦、『穿黑蕾丝内裤的亨蒂』啦——全是这一路货色。」

「是啊，是啊，没错。」皮克林表示同意，「可是这位埃尔南德斯抱着当伟大严肃历史小说家的宏愿，明白吗。不先做上几年研究，她绝不动笔——实际上，是让一群图书馆管理员、大学三年级生之类的文学苦力替她研究了大约六个月——而且她自夸，从未在历史背景细节上被抓到过岔子。

===Original===
“Well, this opus is about the old Manhattan Project. The heroine is a sort of super-Mata-Hari, who is, alternately and sometimes simultaneously, in the pay of the Nazis, the Soviets, the Vatican, Chiang Kai-Shek, the Japanese Emperor, and the Jewish International Bankers, and she sleeps with everybody but Joe Stalin and Mao Tse-tung, and of course, she is in on every step of the A-bomb project. She even manages to stow away on the *Enola Gay*, with the help of a general she’s spent fifty incandescent pages seducing.

“In order to tool up for this production-job, La Hernandez did her researching just where Lourenço Gomes probably did his﻿—University of Montevideo Library. She even had access to the photostats of the old U.S. data that General Lanningham brought to South America after the debacle in the United States in AE 114. Those end-papers are part of the Lanningham stuff. As far as we’ve been able to check mathematically, everything is strictly authentic and practical. We’ll have to run a few more tests on the chemical-explosive charges﻿—we don’t have any data on the exact strength of the explosives they used then﻿—and the tampers and detonating device will need to be tested a little. But in about half an hour, we ought to be able to start drawing plans for the case, and as soon as they’re finished, we’ll rush them to the shipyard foundries for casting.”

Von Schlichten handed the book back to Pickering, and sighed deeply. “And I thought everybody here had gone off his rocker,” he said. “We will erect, on the ruins of Keegark, a hundred-foot statue of Señorita Hildegarde Hernandez.﻿ ﻿… How did you get onto this?”

===Chinese===
「这部大作写的是当年的曼哈顿计划。女主人公是位超级玛塔·哈丽，她轮番、有时同时领取纳粹、苏联、梵蒂冈、蒋介石、日本天皇和犹太国际银行家们的津贴；她跟什么人都上过床，唯独漏掉了乔·斯大林和毛泽东；当然，原子弹工程的每一步她都躬逢其盛。她甚至还设法混上了『艾诺拉·盖伊』号——多亏一位将军帮忙，为了勾引他，她足足烧了五十页炽烈欲燃的篇幅。

「为了给这部大制作备料，这位埃尔南德斯女士做研究的地点，多半正是洛伦索·戈麦斯当年做研究的地方——蒙得维的亚大学图书馆。她甚至能接触到兰宁厄姆将军的旧美国资料影印件——那是原子纪元 114 年美国大崩盘之后，兰宁厄姆将军带到南美来的。那些衬页就是兰宁厄姆资料的一部分。就我们已经用数学验算过的部分而言，一切全都货真价实、切实可用。化学炸药还得再试几轮——他们当年所用炸药的准确强度，我们没有数据——反射层和起爆装置也需要稍加测试。不过大约半个钟头后，我们就该能动手画弹壳图纸了，图一画完，立刻送船厂铸造车间浇铸。」

冯·施利希滕把书还给皮克林，深深叹了口气。「我还当这儿的人都疯了呢。」他说，「我们要在基加克的废墟上，为希尔德加德·埃尔南德斯小姐立一座一百英尺高的雕像。……你们是怎么摸到这条线索的？」

===Original===
Pickering pointed to a young man with dull brick colored hair, who was punching out some kind of a problem on a small computing machine.

“Piet van Reenen, over there, he has a girlfriend whose taste runs to this sort of literary bubble-gum. She told him it was all in a book she’d just read, and showed him. We descended in force on the bookshop and grabbed every copy in stock. We are now running a sort of gaseous-diffusion process, to separate the nuclear physics from the pornography. I must say, Hildegarde has her biological data very well in hand, too.”

“I’ll bet she’d have fun writing a novel about these geeks,” von Schlichten said. “Well, how soon do you think you can have a bomb ready for us?”

===Chinese===
皮克林指了指一个头发呈暗砖红色的年轻人，他正在一架小型计算机上演算什么题目。

「那边那位，皮特·范雷嫩，他有个女朋友，口味正对这种文学口香糖。她告诉他，全都在她刚读完的一本书里，还翻给他看了。于是我们大举杀向书店，把库存扫荡一空。现在我们正在搞一种气体扩散法，把核物理从色情描写里分离出来。我得说，希尔德加德的生物学数据也掌握得非常到家。」

「我敢打赌，让她拿这些土佬写部小说，一定写得津津有味。」冯·施利希滕说，「好了，你们觉得多久能给我们造出一颗炸弹来？」

===Original===
“Casting the cases is going to slow us down the most,” Pickering said. “But, even with that, we ought to have one ready in three days, at the most. By two weeks, we’ll be turning them out on an assembly-line.”

“I hope we don’t need more than one. But you’d better produce at least half a dozen. And have some practice-bombs made up, out of concrete or anything, as long as they’re the right weight and airfoil and have some way of releasing smoke. Get them done as soon as you have your case designed. We want to be able to make a couple of practice drops.”

There was no use, he thought, of raising hopes which might prove premature. He told Paula Quinton, of course, and Themistocles M’zangwe, and, by telecast on sealed beam, King Kankad and Air-Commodore Hargreaves. Beyond that, there was nothing to do but wait, and hope that Hargreaves could keep Orgzild’s bombers away from Gongonk Island and Kankad’s Town and that Hildegarde Hernandez had been playing fair with her public. He visited the city, where a few pockets of diehard resistance were being liquidated, and where everybody who had not been too deeply and publicly involved in the *znidd suddabit* conspiracy was now coming forward and claiming to have been a lifelong friend of the Terrans and the Company. Von Schlichten returned to Gongonk Island, debating with himself whether to declare a general amnesty or to set up a dozen guillotines in the city and run them around the clock for a week. There were cogent arguments for and against either procedure.

===Chinese===
「浇铸弹壳是最拖工夫的一道。」皮克林说，「不过即便如此，最多三天，我们也该能备好一颗。两个星期后，我们就能上流水线成批生产了。」

「我希望我们用不着第二颗。但你们最好至少造出半打来。再让人做几颗练习弹，混凝土或者随便什么材料都行，只要重量和气动外形对，再有办法施放烟幕就行。弹壳设计一完成就把它们做出来。我们得先试投几回。」

他想，点燃或许为时过早的希望，没有什么用处。他当然通知了保拉·昆顿和地米斯托克利·姆赞圭，又通过封闭波束电视告知了坎卡德王和哈格里夫斯空军准将。除此之外，无事可做，只有等待，只有指望哈格里夫斯能把奥格齐尔德的轰炸机挡在贡贡克岛和坎卡德城之外，指望希尔德加德·埃尔南德斯没有糊弄她的读者。他去城里转了转：几股死硬抵抗正被肃清；凡是没在 znidd suddabit 阴谋中陷得太深、太招摇的人，此刻都纷纷上前，自称是地球人和公司的终身挚友。冯·施利希滕回到贡贡克岛，心里盘算着：是宣布大赦好，还是在城里立起一打断头台、昼夜不歇地开上一个星期好。两下里都有言之成理的赞成与反对。

===Original===
By 21:00, the last organized resistance had been wiped out, and curfew had been imposed, and peace of a sort restored. There was still the threat from Keegark, but it was looking less ominous now than it had the evening before. Von Schlichten and Paula were having dinner in the Broadway Room, confident that there was nothing left to do that they could do anything about, when the extension phone that had been plugged in at their table rang.

“Colonel Quinton here,” Paula identified herself into it, and listened for a moment. “There has? When?﻿ ﻿… Well, where did it come from?﻿ ﻿… I see. And the direction?﻿ ﻿… Anything else?”

Apparently there was nothing else. She hung up, and turned to von Schlichten.

===Chinese===
到 21:00，最后一股有组织的抵抗已被消灭，宵禁已经颁布，某种太平算是恢复了。基加克的威胁仍然悬着，但比起前一天傍晚，凶相已经淡了几分。冯·施利希滕和保拉正在「百老汇厅」用晚餐，自信凡是人力可为的事都已做完——这时，插在他们餐桌上的分机电话响了。

「我是昆顿上校。」保拉对着话筒报了身份，听了一会儿。「有了？什么时候？……那么，从哪儿来的？……明白了。航向呢？……还有别的吗？」

显然没有别的了。她挂上电话，转向冯·施利希滕。
===Original===
“The *Sky-Spy* just detected a ship lifting out from Keegark, presumed one of the Boer-class freighters, either the *Jan Smuts* or the *Oom Paul Kruger*. It was first picked up on contragravity at about a hundred feet, rising vertically from near the Palace. The supposition is the geeks had her camouflaged since the time Commander Prinsloo first bombarded Keegark with the *Aldebaran*. That was about twenty minutes ago; at last report, she’s fifty miles north of Keegark, headed up the Hoork River.”

Von Schlichten started thinking aloud: “That could be a feint, to draw our ships north after her, and leave the approach to Konkrook or Kankad’s open, but that would be presuming that they know about the *Sky-Spy*, and I doubt that, though not enough to take chances on. They know we have ground and ship-radar, and they may think they can slip down the Konk Valley either undetected or mistaken for one of our ships from North Uller.”

He picked up the phone. “Get me through on telecast to Air-Commodore Hargreaves, aboard the *Procyon*,” he said. “I’ll take it in the office; I’ll be up directly.” He rose. “Finish your dinner, and have the rest of mine sent up,” he told Paula.

===Chinese===
「『天谍』号刚刚探测到一艘船从基加克升空，推测是布尔级货船之一，不是『扬·斯穆茨』号就是『奥姆·保罗·克鲁格』号。它最初是在约一百英尺高度上被反重力场探测到的，正从王宫附近垂直爬升。据推测，自普林斯鲁中校率『毕宿五』号首次炮击基加克起，土佬们就一直把它伪装隐藏着。那是大约二十分钟前的事；据最新报告，它已在基加克以北五十英里，正沿霍尔克河上溯。」

冯·施利希滕开始出声盘算：「这可能是个佯动，想引我们的船追着它北上，把通往孔克鲁克或坎卡德城的航路空出来。可那就得假定他们知道『天谍』号的存在——这一点我很怀疑，不过还没怀疑到肯去冒险的地步。他们知道我们有地面雷达和舰载雷达，也许以为能顺着孔克河谷溜下来，要么不被发觉，要么被误认成我们北乌勒尔来的船。」

他拿起电话。「给我接通『南河三』号上哈格里夫斯空军准将的电视电话。」他说，「我在办公室接；我马上上去。」他站起身。「把你的晚饭吃完，我剩下的那份让人送上去。」他对保拉说。

===Original===
Leaving the elevator, he rushed into the big headquarters room just as contact was established with the *Procyon*, on station over the northwestern corner of Takkad Sea, between Kankad’s Town and Keegark. The *Aldebaran*, he knew, was west of Keegark; the *Northern Lights*, now fitted with a pair of 155 mm guns, in addition to her 90’s, had just arrived at Kankad’s. He had the *Aldebaran* sent north along the crest of the mountain-range between the Hoork and Konk river-valleys, where she could cover both with her own radar and other detection-devices and exchange information with the *Sky-Spy*, and the *Gaucho* sent in what looked like the right course to intercept the Boer-class freighter from Keegark. The *Northern Lights*, also with screens tuned to the *Sky-Spy*, was sent to take over the *Aldebaran*’s regular station. Finally, he called Skilk and had the *Northern Star* sent south down the Hoork Valley.

After that, there was nothing to do but wait, and watch the screens. Paula Quinton put in an appearance shortly after he had finished calling Skilk, pushing a cocktail-wagon on which their interrupted dinners had been placed. They finished eating, and drank coffee, and smoked. Most of the rest of his staff who were not busy on the bomb-project or at the shipyards or with the occupation of Konkrook drifted in; they all sat and stared from one to another of the screens, which told, in radar-patterns and direct vision and telescopic vision and heat and radiation detection, the story of what was going on to the northeast of them.

Keegark was dark, on the vision-screen; evidently King Orgzild had invented the blackout, too. Not that it did him any good; the radar-screen showed the city clearly, and it was just as clear on the radiation and heat-screens. The Keegarkan ship was completely blacked out, but the radiations from her engines and the distinctive radiation-pattern of her contragravity-field showed clearly, and there was a speck that marked her position on the radar-screen. The same position was marked with a pinpoint of light on the vision-screen﻿—some device on the *Sky-Spy*, synchronized with the detectors, kept it focused there. The Company ships and contragravity vehicles all were carrying topside lights, visible only from above, which flashed alternate red and blue to identify them.

===Chinese===
他一出电梯就冲进大司令部房间，恰逢与『南河三』号的通讯接通——该舰正泊在塔卡德海西北角上空，位于坎卡德城与基加克之间。他知道『毕宿五』号在基加克以西；『北极光』号加装了两门 155 毫米炮——原有的 90 毫米炮之外——刚刚抵达坎卡德城。他命令『毕宿五』号沿霍尔克河与孔克河两河谷之间的山脉山脊北进，在那里用自身的雷达和其他探测设备同时盯住两条河谷，并与『天谍』号互通情报；又派『高乔』号沿着看似正确的航线出发，去拦截那艘从基加克来的布尔级货船。『北极光』号——屏幕同样调谐到『天谍』号——则受命接管『毕宿五』号的例行阵位。最后，他呼叫斯基尔克，命『北极星』号沿霍尔克河谷南下。

此后无事可做，只有等待，只有盯着屏幕。他跟斯基尔克通话刚毕，保拉·昆顿就到了，推着一辆鸡尾酒车，他们被打断的晚餐就摆在上面。两人吃完饭，喝着咖啡，抽着烟。参谋班子里其余没在炸弹工程、船厂或占领孔克鲁克事务中忙碌的人，陆陆续续都聚了过来；大家坐着，目光从一块屏幕移到另一块屏幕——雷达图像、直视画面、望远画面、热探测与辐射探测，正讲述着他们东北方正在发生的一切。

直视屏幕上，基加克一片漆黑；显然，奥格齐尔德王连灯火管制也发明出来了。只是这对他毫无益处；雷达屏上城市历历在目，在辐射屏和热屏上同样一清二楚。那艘基加克船实行了彻底的灯火管制，但它的引擎辐射和反重力场特有的辐射波形清晰可辨，雷达屏上还有一个光点标着它的位置。直视屏上，同一位置亮着一粒针尖大的光点——『天谍』号上某种与探测器联动的装置，让镜头始终对准那里。公司的舰船和反重力载具全都开着顶灯——只有从上方才能看见——红蓝交替闪烁，以资识别。

===Original===
Time crawled slowly around the clock-face on the wall, the sixty-five-second minutes of Uller dragging like hours. The spots that marked the enemy ship and her hunters crawled, too; seen from the hundred-and-fifty-mile altitude of the *Sky-Spy*, even the six-hundred-mile speed of the *Gaucho* was barely visible. They drank coffee till the stuff revolted them; they smoked until their throats and mouths were dry, they watched the screens until they thought that they would see them in their dreams forever. Then the *Gaucho* reported radar-contact with the Keegarkan ship, which had begun to turn in a hairpin-shaped course and was coming south down the Konk Valley.

After that, the *Gaucho* began reporting directly, and her topside identification-light went out.

“… doused our lights; we’re down in the valley, altitude about a thousand feet. We’re trying to get a glimpse of her against the sky,” a voice came in. “We’re cutting in our forward TV-pickup.” The voice repeated, several times, the wavelength, and somebody got an auxiliary screen tuned in. There was nothing visible on it but the darkness of the valley, the star-jeweled sky, and the loom of the East Konk Mountains. “We still can’t see her, but we ought to, any moment; radar shows her well above the mountains. Ah, there she is; she just obscured Beta Hydrae V; she’s moving toward that big constellation to the east of it, the one they call Finnegan’s Goat. Now she’ll be right in the center of the screen; we’re going straight for her. We’re going to try to slow her down till the *Aldebaran* can get here.﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
时间绕着墙上的钟面缓慢爬行，乌勒尔每分钟六十五秒的长分，拖得像一个个钟头。标示敌船及其追猎者的光点也在爬行；从『天谍』号一百五十英里的高度看下去，就连『高乔』号六百英里的时速也几乎看不出来。他们喝咖啡喝到反胃，抽烟抽到喉咙口发干，盯屏幕盯到觉得这辈子做梦都会梦见这些屏幕。这时，『高乔』号报告与基加克船雷达接触——敌船已开始作发卡形转弯，正沿孔克河谷向南而来。

此后，『高乔』号开始直接通话报告，它的顶空识别灯熄灭了。

「……灯已熄灭；我们在河谷低空，高度约一千英尺。我们正试着借天光瞥见它。」一个声音传进来。「我们正接通前视电视摄像机。」那声音把波长重复了几遍，有人把一块辅助屏幕调谐到位。屏幕上什么都看不见，只有河谷的黑暗、缀满星斗的夜空，和东孔克山脉的巍巍黑影。「我们还是看不见它，但随时都应该能看见；雷达显示它已高过山脊。啊，在那儿；它刚遮住水蛇座β-V（Beta Hydrae V），正朝它东边那片大星座移动——就是他们叫作『芬尼根的山羊』（Finnegan's Goat）的那个。现在它该正好处在屏幕中央；我们径直朝它去了。我们要设法拖住它，等『毕宿五』号赶到。……」

===Original===
The enemy ship was vaguely visible, now, becoming clearer in the starlight. She was a Boer-class freighter, all right. Probably the *Jan Smuts*; the *Oom Paul Kruger* had last been reported at Bwork, and there was little chance that she had slipped into Keegark since the uprising had started. For all anybody knew, she could have been destroyed in the fighting before the Bwork Residency fell.

“All right, we have her spotted; we’re going to open up on her,” the voice from the *Gaucho* announced. “She has two 90’s to our one; we’ll try to disable them, first.” The vision-screen lit with the indirect glare of the gun-flash, and the image in it jiggled violently as the ship shook to the recoil, then steadied again, with the enemy ship visible in the middle of it, growing larger and larger as the *Gaucho* rushed toward her. The gun fired again and again, flooding the screen with momentary yellow light and disturbing the image as the recoil shook the gun-cutter. The enemy ship began firing in reply, the shots were all wide misses. Apparently the geek guncrew didn’t know how to synchronize the radar sights, and were ignorant of the correct setting for the proximity-fuses. The *Gaucho*’s searchlights came on, bathing her quarry in light. It was the *Jan Smuts*; the name and the figurehead-bust of the old soldier-philosopher were plainly visible. Her forward gun had been knocked out, and she was trying to swing about to get a field of fire for her stern-gun.

“We’re going to give her a rocket-salvo,” the voice said. “Watch this, now!”

===Chinese===
敌船现在隐约可见了，在星光下渐渐清晰。确实是艘布尔级货船。多半是『扬·斯穆茨』号；『奥姆·保罗·克鲁格』号最后据报在布沃克，起义爆发以来它溜进基加克的可能性微乎其微。天晓得，它也许早在布沃克总督府陷落之前的战斗里就被打毁了。

「好了，我们咬住它了；我们准备开火。」『高乔』号上的声音宣布。「它有两门 90 毫米炮，我们只有一门；我们先设法敲掉它们。」直视屏被炮口闪光映得一亮，后坐震得舰身猛晃，画面随之剧烈抖动，随即又稳定下来——敌船居于画面中央，随着『高乔』号猛扑过去而越来越大。那门炮一发射击再射击，阵阵黄光瞬时刷满屏幕，后坐震动炮艇，画面也随之扰动。敌船开始还击，炮弹全都偏得老远。显然，土佬炮组不会同步校准雷达瞄准具，也不懂近炸引信的正确装定。『高乔』号的探照灯亮起，把猎物罩在光柱里。正是『扬·斯穆茨』号；船名和船首那尊老军人哲学家的半身雕像清晰可见。它的前炮已被打哑，正在试图转向，好让尾炮获得射界。

「我们要给它来一顿火箭齐射。」那声音说，「都瞧好了！」

===Original===
The rockets leaped forward, from the topside racks, four and four and four and four, at half-second intervals. The first four hit the *Smuts* amidships and low, exploding with a flare that grew before it could die away as the second four landed. Nobody ever saw the third and fourth four land. The *Jan Smuts* vanished in a blaze of light that blinded everybody in the room; when they could see again, after some thirty seconds, the screen was dark.

In the direct-vision screen from the *Sky-Spy*, the whole countryside of the Konk Valley, five hundred miles north of Konkrook, was lighted. The heat and radiation detectors were going insane. And in the shifting confusion on the radar-screen, there was no trace either of the *Jan Smuts* or the *Gaucho*.

“Well, the geeks did have an A-bomb,” Themistocles M’zangwe said, at length. “I’d been trying to kid myself that we were just preparing against a million-to-one chance. I wonder how many more they have.”

===Chinese===
火箭从顶部发射架上跃出，四枚、四枚、再四枚、又四枚，间隔半秒。头四枚打在『斯穆茨』号中段偏下处，爆炸的火光尚未及熄灭，第二组四枚又已落下，光焰愈燃愈旺。第三组、第四组落下的情形，谁也没有看见。『扬·斯穆茨』号在一团烈焰中消失了，强光晃得满屋人人目眩；约莫三十秒后他们重见光明时，屏幕已是一片漆黑。

在『天谍』号传回的直视屏幕上，孔克鲁克以北五百英里的孔克河谷全境都被照亮了。热探测和辐射探测器都疯了似的。而在雷达屏上变幻的混乱图像里，『扬·斯穆茨』号和『高乔』号双双没了踪影。

「唉，土佬们到底还是有一颗原子弹。」地米斯托克利·姆赞圭终于开口，「我一直哄自己说，我们不过是在防备那百万分之一的万一。真不知道他们还有几颗。」

===Original===
“Paula, find out who was in command of the *Gaucho*; he’d be a junior-grade lieutenant. Fix up orders promoting him to navy captain, as of now. It’s probably the only thing we can do for him, anymore. And promotions of the same order for everybody else aboard that cutter. Authority Carlos von Schlichten, acting Governor-General.” He picked up a phone. “Get me Commander Prinsloo, on *Aldebaran*.﻿ ﻿…”

He ordered Prinsloo to launch airboats and make a search; cautioned him to be careful of radiation, but to take no chances on any of the *Gaucho*’s complement being still alive and in need of help. While that was going on, the *Sky-Spy* reported another ship coming over her horizon to the east, from the direction of Bwork. That would be the *Oom Paul Kruger*. Hargreaves had already learned of the advent of the second freighter. He was unwilling to take the *Procyon* off her station until the *Aldebaran* returned from the Konk Valley. In this, von Schlichten concurred.

Somebody suggested that a drink would be in order. They had just watched the all-but-certain death of three Terran officers, fifteen Terran airmen, and ten Kragans, but they had all been living in too close companionship with death in the past three days﻿—or was it three centuries﻿—to be too deeply affected. And they had also watched, at least for a day or so, the removal of the threat that had hung over their heads. And they had seen proof that they had a defense against King Orgzild’s bombs.

===Chinese===
「保拉，查一查『高乔』号的指挥官是谁；该是个海军中尉。马上起草晋升令，晋升他为海军上校，即日生效。这大概是我们唯一还能为他做的事了。艇上其余所有人，一律同等晋升。签令人：代理总督卡洛斯·冯·施利希滕。」他拿起电话。「给我接『毕宿五』号上的普林斯鲁中校。……」

他命令普林斯鲁放出飞艇进行搜索；告诫他当心辐射，但若『高乔』号艇员中还有生者待援，切不可存侥幸之心。搜索进行期间，『天谍』号又报告东方地平线上出现一艘船，来自布沃克方向。那该是『奥姆·保罗·克鲁格』号了。哈格里夫斯已获悉第二艘货船的到来。在『毕宿五』号从孔克河谷返回之前，他不愿让『南河三』号离开阵位。在这一点上，冯·施利希滕深表同意。

有人提议，这时候该喝上一杯。他们刚刚目睹了三名地球军官、十五名地球航空兵和十名克拉冈人几乎必死的结局；可过去这三天——抑或是三个世纪？——他们与死亡厮守得太紧，已不至于为此过分伤感。而且他们也刚刚眼见，至少在一天左右的时间里，悬在他们头顶的威胁烟消云散。而且他们也看到了实证：对付奥格齐尔德王的炸弹，他们手里已有反制之物。

===Original===
They were still mixing cocktails when Pickering phoned in.

“Some good news, general, from Operation ‘Hildegarde.’ We ought to have at least one bomb ready to drop by 15:00 tomorrow, four or five more by next midnight,” he said. “We don’t need to have cases cast. We got our dimensions decided, and we find that there are a lot of big empty liquid-oxygen flasks, or tanks, rather, at the spaceport, that’ll accommodate everything﻿—fissionables, explosive-charges, tampers, detonator, and all.”

“Well, go ahead with it. Make up a few of them; as many as you can between now and 24:00 Sunday.” He thought for a moment. “Don’t waste time on those practice bombs I mentioned. We’ll make a practice drop with a live bomb. And don’t throw away the design for the cast case. We may need that, later on.”

===Chinese===
鸡尾酒还没调好，皮克林的电话就进来了。

「将军，『希尔德加德』行动有好消息。到明天 15:00，我们至少该有一颗可以投掷的炸弹，下一个午夜再有四五颗。」他说，「弹壳不用浇铸了。尺寸已经定下来，我们发现航天港有一大批空的大液氧瓶——该说是液氧罐——装下全部家什绰绰有余：核裂变材料、炸药、反射层、起爆器，一应俱全。」

「好，放手去干。多造几颗；从现在到星期天 24:00，能造多少造多少。」他想了想。「我提过的练习弹就别费工夫了。我们用实弹试投。还有，浇铸弹壳的设计图也别扔。往后说不定用得上。」

## XII The Shadow of Niflheim / 第十二章 尼福尔海姆的阴影

===Original===
The sun slid lower and lower toward the horizon behind them as the aircar bulleted south along the broad valley and dry bed of the Hoork River, nearing the zone of equal day and night. Hassan Bogdanoff drove while Harry Quong finished his lunch, then changed places to begin his own. Von Schlichten got two bottles of beer from the refrigerated section of the lunch-hamper and opened one for Paula Quinton and one for himself.

“What are we going to do with these geeks,”﻿—she was using the nasty and derogatory word unconsciously and by custom, now﻿—“after this is all over? We can’t just tell them, ‘Jolly well played, nice game, wasn’t it?’ and go back to where we were Wednesday evening.”

“No, we can’t. There’s going to have to be a Terran seizure of political power in every part of this planet that we occupy, and as soon as we’re consolidated around and north of Takkad Sea, we’re going to have to move in elsewhere,” he replied. “Keegark, Konkrook, and the Free Cities, of course, will be relatively easy. They’re in arms against us now, and we can take them over by force. We had to make that deal with Jonkvank, or, rather, I did, so that will be a slower process, but we’ll get it done in time. If I know that pair as well as I think I do, Jonkvank and Yoorkerk will give us plenty of pretexts, before long. Then, we can start giving them government by law instead of by royal decree, and real courts of justice; put an end to the head-payment system, and to these arbitrary mass arrests and tax-delinquency imprisonments that are nothing but slave-raids by the geek princes on their own people. And, gradually, abolish serfdom. In a couple of centuries, this planet will be fit to admit to the Federation, like Odin and Freya.”

===Chinese===
飞车沿着霍尔克河宽阔的河谷与干涸的河床像子弹般向南疾驰，太阳在他们身后越沉越低，直向地平线坠去，车已渐近昼夜等长的地带。哈里·关吃午饭的当口由哈桑·博格丹诺夫驾驶，随后两人换班，轮到哈桑吃自己那份。冯·施利希滕从午餐篮的冷藏格里取出两瓶啤酒，替保拉·昆顿开了一瓶，又给自己开了一瓶。

「等这一切了结之后，我们打算拿这些土佬怎么办？」——这个恶毒的蔑称，她如今说出口已是全然无心、习以为常了——「总不能对他们来一句『打得漂亮，这局精彩，是不是？』，就退回星期三晚上的老样子去。」

「不能，退不回去了。凡是我们占领的这颗行星上的每一处，都得由地球人强行接管政权；等我们在塔卡德海周边及其以北站稳脚跟，就得向其他地方进军，」他答道。「基加克、孔克鲁克，还有那些自由城邦，办起来当然相对容易。它们眼下正持械与我们作对，我们可以用武力接管。跟琼克万克那笔交易当时不得不做——说得再准些，是我不得不做——所以那边的过程会慢一些，但我们来得及办成。要是我对那两位的了解没有走眼，琼克万克和约克克要不了一多久，就会给我们送来大把现成的借口。到那时，我们就可以着手给他们以法治代替王命的治理，建立像样的司法法庭；终结人头贡赋制度，终结那些名为大抓捕、欠税监禁，实则纯属土佬王公对自家族人猎奴的勾当。然后，逐步废除农奴制。再过两百年，这颗行星就够格加入联邦了，像奥丁和弗蕾亚一样。」

===Original===
“Well, won’t that depend a lot on whom the Company sends here to take Harrington’s place?”

“Unless I’m much mistaken, the Company will confirm me,” he replied. “Administration on Uller is going to be a military matter for a long time to come, and even the Banking Cartel and the mercantile interests in the Company are going to realize that, and see the necessity for taking political control. The Federation Government owns a bigger interest in the Company than the public realizes, too; they’ve always favored it. And just to make sure, I’m sending Hid O’Leary to Terra on the next ship, to make a full report on the situation.”

“You think it’ll be cleared up by then? The *City of Montevideo* is due in from Niflheim in a little under three months.”

===Chinese===
「可是，这不很大程度上要看公司派谁来接哈灵顿的班吗？」

「除非我大错特错，公司会正式确认我，」他答道。「今后很长一段时间，乌勒尔的治理都是军务，连银行卡特尔和公司里的商业利益集团也会认识到这一点，明白接管政治的必要。联邦政府在公司里占的股份也比公众以为的大；他们向来赞成这么做。为保险起见，我要派希德·奥利里搭下一班船回地球，呈上一份当地局面的全盘报告。」

「您以为到那时事情就都了结了？『蒙得维的亚城』号不出三个月就要从尼福尔海姆抵达了。」

===Original===
“It’ll have to be cleared up by then. We can’t keep this war going more than a month, at the present rate. Police-action, and mopping-up, yes, full-scale war, no.”

“Ammunition?” she asked.

He looked at her in pleased surprise. “Your education has been progressing, at that,” he said. “You know, a lot of professional officers, even up to field rank in the combat branches, seem to think that ammo comes down miraculously from Heaven, in contragravity lorries, every time they pray into a radio for it. It doesn’t; it has to be produced as fast as it’s expended, and we haven’t been doing that. So we’ll have to lick these geeks before it runs out, because we can’t lick them with gunbutts and bayonets.”

===Chinese===
「到那时非了结不可。照目前的消耗速度，这场仗我们撑不过一个月。治安行动、清剿残敌，可以；全面战争，不行。」

「弹药？」她问。

他又是欢喜又是惊讶地望着她。「看来你这门功课还真有长进，」他说。「您知道吗，许多职业军官，甚至战斗兵种里熬到校官的，似乎都以为只要对着无线电祷告一声，弹药就会由反重力卡车装着，从天上神迹般地送下来。才不是那么回事；弹药消耗多快，就得生产多快，而我们一直没做到。所以我们得趁弹药没耗光之前摆平这些土佬，因为靠枪托和刺刀，是摆不平他们的。」

===Original===
“Well, how about nuclear weapons?” Paula asked. “I hate to suggest it﻿—I know what they did on Mimir, and Fenris, and Midgard, and what they did on Terra, during the First Century. But it may be our only chance.”

He finished his beer and shoved the bottle into the waste-receiver, then got out his cigarettes.

“I’d hate to have to make a decision like that, Paula,” he told her. “The military use of nuclear energy is the last﻿—well, the next-to-last﻿—thing I’d want to see on Uller. Fortunately, or unfortunately, it’s a decision I won’t have to make. There isn’t a single nuclear bomb on the planet. The Company’s always refused to allow them to be manufactured or stockpiled here.”

===Chinese===
「那么，核武器怎么样？」保拉问。「我不愿意提这个——弥米尔星、芬里斯星、米德加德（Midgard）星上出过什么事，第一世纪里地球本土又出过什么事，我都知道。可这也许是咱们唯一的机会。」

他喝完啤酒，把瓶子塞进废物收纳口，然后掏出烟来。

「保拉，这样的决断，我真不愿去做，」他对她说。「核能的军事用途，是我最不想——嗯，倒数第二不想——在乌勒尔见到的东西。所幸——抑或不幸——这个决断轮不到我来做。这颗行星上一颗核弹都没有。公司从来拒绝许可在这里制造或贮存核弹。」

===Original===
“I don’t think there’d be any criticism of your making them, now, general. And there’s certainly plenty of plutonium. You could make A-bombs, at least.”

“There isn’t anybody here who even knows how to make one. Most of our nuclear engineers could work one up, in about three months, when we’d either not need one or not be alive.”

“Dr. Gomes, who came in on the *Pretoria*, two weeks ago, can make them,” she contradicted. “He built at least a dozen of them on Niflheim, to use in activating volcanoes and bringing ore-bearing lava to the surface.”

===Chinese===
「我想事到如今，您造这东西不会招来什么非议了，将军。钚反正有的是。至少，原子弹您是造得出来的。」

「这里根本没有一个人知道怎么造。我们的核工程师大多数倒是能在大约三个月里捣鼓出一颗来——到那时候，我们要么用不着它，要么已经没命了。」

「两周前搭『比勒陀利亚』号来的那位戈麦斯博士就会造，」她反驳道。「他在尼福尔海姆至少造过一打，用来激活火山，把含矿的熔岩引上地表。」

===Original===
Von Schlichten’s hand, bringing his lighter to the tip of his cigarette, paused for a second. Then he completed the operation, snapped it shut, and put it away.

“When did all this happen?”

She took time out for mental arithmetic; even a spaceship officer had to do that, when a question of interstellar time-relations arose.

===Chinese===
冯·施利希滕正把打火机凑向烟头的手停顿了一秒。随后他做完这套动作，「啪」地合上打火机，收了起来。

「这些都是什么时候的事？」

她停下来心算了一会儿；碰上星际时间换算的问题，就连飞船军官也得来这一套。

===Original===
“About three-fifty days ago, Galactic Standard. They’d put off the first shot, six bombs, before I got in from Terra. I saw the second shot a day or so before I left Niflheim on the *Canberra*. Dr. Gomes had to stay over till the *Pretoria* to put off the third shot. Why?”

“Did you run into a geek named Gorkrink, while you were on Nif?” he asked her. “And what sort of work was he doing?”

“Gorkrink? I don’t seem to remember.﻿ ﻿… Oh, yes! He was helping Dr. Murillo, the seismologist. His year was up after the second shot; he came to Uller on the *Canberra*. Dr. Murillo was sorry to lose him. He understood Lingua Terra perfectly; Dr. Murillo could talk to him, the way you do with Kankad, without using a geek-speaker.”

===Chinese===
「按银河标准历，大约三百五十天前。我还没从地球抵达的时候，他们就已经放完了第一轮，六颗。我乘『堪培拉』号离开尼福尔海姆的前一天左右，亲眼见了第二轮。戈麦斯博士只好留下等『比勒陀利亚』号，好放第三轮。怎么？」

「你在尼夫的时候，碰没碰上一个叫戈克林克的土佬？」他问她。「他干的是哪一路活计？」

「戈克林克？我好像想不起来了……哦，对了！他在给地震学家穆里略博士打下手。第二轮起爆之后，他的一年工期就满了；他是搭『堪培拉』号来乌勒尔的。穆里略博士舍不得放他走。他的地球语听得透熟；穆里略博士跟他讲话，就像您跟坎卡德讲话一样，用不着土佬语发声器。」

===Original===
“Well, but what sort of work﻿ ﻿… ?”

“Helping set and fire the A-bombs.﻿ ﻿… *Oh! Good Lord!*”

“You can say that again, and deal in Allah, Shiva, and Kali,” von Schlichten told her. “Especially Kali.﻿ ﻿… Harry! See if you can get some more speed out of this can. I want to get to Konkrook while it’s still there!”

===Chinese===
「那么，他干的到底是什么活计……？」

「帮忙布设、起爆那些原子弹……哦！天哪！」

「这话你尽管再说一遍，把安拉、湿婆和迦梨（Kali）全都请出来赌咒，」冯·施利希滕对她说。「尤其是迦梨……哈里！看看能不能再从这铁罐子里榨出点速度来。我要趁孔克鲁克还在的时候赶回去！」

---

===Original===
It was full dark when Konkrook came in view beyond the East Konk Mountains, a lurid smear on the underside of the clouds, and, at Gongonk Island and at the Company farms to the south, a couple of bunches of searchlights fingering about in the sky. When von Schlichten turned on the outside sound-pickup, he could hear the distant tom-tomming of heavy guns, and the crash of shells and bombs. Keeping the car high enough to be above the trajectories of incoming shells, Harry Quong circled over the city while Hassan Bogdanoff talked to Gongonk Island on the radio.

The city was in a bad way. There were seventy-five to a hundred big fires going, and a new one started in a rising ball of thermoconcentrate flame while they watched. The three gun-cutters, *Elmoran*, *Gaucho*, and *Bushranger*, and about fifty big freight lorries converted to bombers, were shuttling back and forth between the island and the city. The Royal Palace was on fire from end to end, and the entire waterfront and industrial district were in flames. Combat-cars and airjeeps were diving in to shell and rocket and machine-gun streets and buildings. He saw six big bomber-lorries move in dignified procession to unload, one after the other, on a row of buildings along what the Terrans called South Tenth Street, and on the roofs of buildings a block away, red and blue flares were burning, and he could see figures, both human and Ulleran, setting up mortars and machine-guns.

Landing on the top stage of Company House, on the island, they were met by a Terran whom von Schlichten had seen, a few days ago, bossing native-labor at the spaceport, but who was now wearing a major’s insignia. He greeted von Schlichten with a salute which he must have learned from some movie about the ancient French Foreign Legion. Von Schlichten seriously returned it in kind.

===Chinese===
孔克鲁克映入眼帘时，天已全黑；越过东孔克山脉望去，那只是涂抹在云层底面的一道刺眼血光，而在贡贡克岛和南面的公司农场，几簇探照灯光柱正在天空中摸索。冯·施利希滕打开外界拾音器，听得见重炮遥远的咚咚轰响，以及炮弹炸弹的爆裂声。哈里·关让飞车保持在来袭炮弹弹道之上，绕着城市盘旋，哈桑·博格丹诺夫则用无线电与贡贡克岛通话。

城里情况不妙。七十五处到一百处大火正烧着，就在他们注视之下，又有一团热凝燃料的火球腾腾升起，烧成一片新的火场。三艘炮艇——「埃尔莫兰」号、「高乔」号、「丛林游骑兵」号——加上大约五十辆改装成轰炸机的大型货运车，在岛与城之间穿梭往返。王宫从头到尾烧成了一条火龙，整片滨水区和工业区都陷在火海里。战斗车和飞行吉普一次次俯冲下来，对着街道和建筑开炮、放火箭、用机枪扫射。他看见六辆大型轰炸车排着不慌不忙的队形依次进入，对地球人称为南十街沿线的一排建筑挨个倾泻弹药；隔着好几个街区，一片屋顶上红蓝两色的信号弹烧得正旺，他看得出上面有人影——地球人和乌勒尔人都有——正在架设迫击炮和机枪。

他们在岛上公司大楼的顶层平台降落，一名地球人迎上前来——几天前冯·施利希滕还见过此人在航天港监工土著劳工，如今他身上却挂起了少校军衔。他向冯·施利希滕行了个军礼，那一手必定是从哪部讲古时法国外籍军团的老电影里学来的。冯·施利希滕一本正经地照原样回了礼。

===Original===
“Everybody’s down in the Governor-General’s office, sir,” he said. “Your office, that is. King Kankad’s here with us, too.”

He accompanied them to the elevator, then turned to a telephone; when von Schlichten and Paula reached the office, everybody was crowded at the door to greet them: Themistocles M’zangwe, his arm in a sling; Hans Meyerstein, the Johannesburg lawyer, who seemed to have even more Bantu blood than the brigadier-general; Morton Buhrmann, the Commercial Superintendent; Laviola, the Fiscal Secretary; a dozen or so other officers and civil administrators. There was a hubbub of greetings, and he was pleased to detect as much real warmth from the civil administration crowd as from the officers.

“Well, I’m glad to be back with you,” he replied, generally. “And let me present Colonel Paula Quinton, my new adjutant; Hid O’Leary’s on duty in the north.﻿ ﻿… Them, this was a perfectly splendid piece of work here; you can take this not only as a personal congratulation, but as a sort of unit citation for the whole crowd. You’ve all behaved simply above praise.” He turned to King Kankad, who was wearing a pair of automatics in shoulder-holsters for his upper hands and another pair in cross-body belt holsters for his lower. “And what I’ve said for anybody else goes double for you, Kankad,” he added, clapping the Kragan on the shoulder.

===Chinese===
「各位都在下面的总督办公室里，长官，」他说。「也就是您的办公室。坎卡德王也同我们在一起。」

他陪他们走到电梯口，随即转身去接电话；等冯·施利希滕和保拉到了办公室，众人都挤在门口迎候：吊着手臂的地米斯托克利·姆赞圭；约翰内斯堡律师汉斯·迈耶斯坦，他看着比那位准将的班图血统还要更浓些；商务总监莫顿·布尔曼；财政秘书拉维奥拉；另有十来位军官和民政官员。寒暄声响成一片；他高兴地察觉，文官们流露的真心热络，丝毫不比军官们少。

「好，我很高兴回到大家中间，」他向众人答道。「容我介绍，保拉·昆顿上校，我的新任副官；希德·奥利里正在北边当值……地米，这里这一手干得漂亮极了；你们不妨把我这话不只当作我个人的道贺，也当作给全体的集体嘉奖令。你们大家的表现，简直无可赞誉。」他转向坎卡德王——只见对方上手的肩挎枪套里插着一对自动手枪，下手的斜挎腰带枪套里又插着一对。「我对旁人说过的那些话，搁在你身上要翻倍，坎卡德，」他补上一句，拍了拍这位克拉冈人的肩膀。

===Original===
“All he did was save the lot of us!” M’zangwe said. “We were hanging on by our fingernails here till his people started coming in. And then, after you sent the *Aldebaran*.﻿ ﻿…”

“Where is the *Aldebaran*, by the way? I didn’t see her when I came in.”

“Based on Kankad’s, flying bombardment against Keegark, and keeping an eye out for those ships. Prinsloo caught the *De Wett* in the docks there and smashed her, but the *Jan Smuts* got away, and we haven’t been able to locate the *Oom Paul Kruger*, either. They’re probably both on the Eastern Shore, gathering up reinforcements for Orgzild,” M’zangwe said.

===Chinese===
「他干的那点事，不过是把我们这一整伙人全救了下来！」姆赞圭说。「他的人开始开进之前，我们在这儿全凭指甲缝里那点劲吊着命。后来，等您把『毕宿五』号派来……」

「对了，『毕宿五』号眼下在哪儿？我进来的时候没瞧见她。」

「驻在坎卡德城，对基加克实施轰炸，顺便盯紧那几条船。普林斯鲁在基加克的船坞里堵住了『克里斯蒂安·德韦特』号，把她砸了个稀烂；可『扬·斯穆茨』号跑掉了，『奥姆·保罗·克鲁格』号我们也一直没能找到。那两条船八成都在东岸，替奥格齐尔德收拢兵力，」姆赞圭说。

===Original===
“Our ability to move troops rapidly is what’s kept us on top this long, and Orgzild’s had plenty of time to realize it,” von Schlichten said. “When we get *Procyon* down here, I’m going to send her out, with a screen of light scout-vehicles, to find those ships and get rid of them.﻿ ﻿… How’s Hid been making out, at Grank, by the way? I didn’t have my car-radio on, coming down.”

That touched off another hubbub: “Haven’t you heard, general?”﻿ ﻿… “Oh, my God, this is simply out of this continuum!”﻿ ﻿… “Well, tell him, somebody!”﻿ ﻿… “No, get Hid on the screen; it’s his story!”

Somebody busied himself at the switchboard. The rest of them sat down at the long conference-table. Laviola and Meyerstein and Buhrmann were especially obsequious in seating von Schlichten in Sid Harrington’s old chair, and in getting a chair for Paula Quinton. After a while, the jumbled colors on the big screen resolved themselves into an image of Hideyoshi O’Leary, grinning like a pussycat beside an empty goldfish-bowl.

===Chinese===
「我们能快速调兵，这才压得住局面这么久，而奥格齐尔德有的是时间认清这一点，」冯·施利希滕说。「等『南河三』号调下来，我就派她出去，带一队轻型侦察艇护航，找到那几条船，除掉它们……对了，希德在格兰克那边干得怎么样？下来这一路我没开车载电台。」

这话又点燃一片喧哗：「您还没听说吗，将军？」……「哦，我的天哪，这事儿简直不在这个时空连续统里！」……「那还等什么，谁来跟他讲！」……「别，把希德调上屏幕；这故事得他本人讲！」

有人到交换台前忙活起来。其余的人在长会议桌旁落座。拉维奥拉、迈耶斯坦和布尔曼格外殷勤，把冯·施利希滕让到席德·哈灵顿的旧椅子上，又给保拉·昆顿张罗座位。过了一会儿，大屏幕上纷乱的色块凝成了秀吉·奥利里的影像——他咧着嘴笑，活像蹲在空金鱼碗旁边的那只猫。
===Original===
“Well, what happened?” von Schlichten asked, after they had exchanged greetings. “How did Yoorkerk like the movies? And did you get the *Procyon* and the *Northern Lights* loose?”

“Yoorkerk was deeply impressed,” O’Leary replied. “His story is that he is and always was the true and ever-loving friend of the Company; he acted to prevent quote certain disloyal elements unquote from harming the people and property of the Company. *Procyon*’s on the way to Konkrook. I’m holding *Northern Lights* here and *Northern Star* at Skilk; where do you want them sent?”

“Leave *Northern Star* at Skilk, for the time being. Tell the Company’s great and good friend King Yoorkerk that the Company expects him to contribute some soldiers for the campaign here and against Keegark, when that starts; be sure you get the best-armed and best-trained regiments he has, and get them down here as soon as possible. Don’t send any of your Kragans or Karamessinis’ troops here, though; hold them in Grank till we make sure of the quality of Yoorkerk’s friendship.”

===Chinese===
寒暄过后，冯·施利希滕发问：「到底出了什么事？约克克看那几部电影看得尽兴吗？还有，你把『南河三』号和『北极光』号脱身的事办成了吗？」

「约克克深受触动，」奥利里答道。「他的说法是：他如今是、从来也是公司真诚不渝的朋友；他出兵是为了防止——照他的原话——『某些不忠分子』危害公司的人员与财产。『南河三』号已在来孔克鲁克的路上。『北极光』号我叫她留在这儿，『北极星』号留在斯基尔克；您要把她们派去哪儿？」

「『北极星』号暂时就留在斯基尔克。你去对公司那位「伟大而忠实的朋友」约克克王说：公司指望他为这里的战事、以及日后攻打基加克的战事凑一些兵；务必挑他手下装备最好、训练最精的团队，尽快派下来。不过，你自己的克拉冈人和卡拉梅西尼斯的部队一个也不要往这儿派；先把他们攥在格兰克，等我们验一验约克克这份友谊的成色再说。」

===Original===
“Well, general, I think we can be pretty sure, now. You see, he turned Rakkeed the Prophet over to me.﻿ ﻿…”

“*What?*” Von Schlichten felt his monocle starting to slip and took a firmer grip on it. “Who?”

“Pay me, Them; he didn’t drop it,” Hideyoshi O’Leary said. “Why, Rakkeed the Prophet. Yoorkerk was holding our ships and our people in case we lost; he was also holding Rakkeed at the Palace in case we won. Of course, Rakkeed thought he was an honored guest, right up till Yoorkerk’s guards dragged him in and turned him over to us.﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「咳，将军，我看这份成色眼下可以放心了。您瞧，他把先知拉基德捆起来，移交给了我……」

「什么？」冯·施利希滕觉出单片眼镜开始下滑，赶紧捏紧了些。「谁？」

「地米，掏钱吧，他可没把眼镜惊掉，」秀吉·奥利里说。「没错，先知拉基德。约克克扣着我们的船、我们的人，以防我们打输；同时也把拉基德软禁在王宫里，以防我们打赢。当然啦，拉基德一直当自己是备受礼遇的贵客——直到约克克的卫兵把他拖进来，移交到我们手上……」

===Original===
“That geek,” von Schlichten said, “is too smart for his own good. Some of these days he’s going to play both ends against the middle and both ends’ll fold in on him and smash him.” A suspicion occurred to him. “You sure this is Rakkeed? It would be just like Yoorkerk to try to sell us a ringer.”

O’Leary shook his head solemnly. “I thought of that, right away. This is the real article; Karamessinis’ Constabulary and Intelligence officers certified him for me. What do you want me to do, send him down to Konkrook?”

Von Schlichten shook his head. “Get the priests of the locally venerated gods to put him on trial for blasphemy, heresy, impersonating a prophet, practicing witchcraft without a license, or any other ecclesiastical crimes you or they can think of. Then, after he’s been given a scrupulously fair trial, have the soldiers of King Yoorkerk behead him, and stick his head up over a big sign, in all native languages, ‘Rakkeed the False Prophet.’ And have audiovisuals made of the whole business, trial and execution, and be sure that the priests and Yoorkerk’s officers are in the foreground and our people stay out of the pictures.”

===Chinese===
「那个土佬，」冯·施利希滕说，「精明得过了头，早晚坑了自己。迟早有一天，他两头下注、左右通吃，结果两头一齐朝中间合拢，把他夹个粉碎。」他忽然起了个疑心。「你拿得准这是拉基德本人？拿个冒牌货来诓我们，正是约克克干得出来的事。」

奥利里郑重其事地摇头。「这一点我马上就想到了。是如假包换的正主儿；卡拉梅西尼斯的宪兵队和情报军官替我验明了正身。您要我拿他怎么办，押来孔克鲁克？」

冯·施利希滕摇了摇头。「让当地所奉诸神的祭司们审判他——罪名嘛，渎神、异端、假冒先知、无照行巫，或者你跟他们能想得出的任何教法罪名。然后，等他受完一场一丝不苟的公正审判，就叫约克克王的士兵砍他的头，把头颅高高挂起，底下竖一块大牌子，用各种土著文字写上：『假先知拉基德』。全过程——审判加处决——都给我录成影像，务必让祭司们和约克克的军官们站在镜头前排，我们的人一个都不许进画面。」

===Original===
“Soap and towels, for General Pontius von Pilate!” Paula Quinton called out.

“That’s an idea; I was wondering what to give Yoorkerk as a testimonial present,” Hideyoshi O’Leary said. “A nice thirty-piece silver set!”

“Quite appropriate,” von Schlichten approved. “Well, you did a first-class job. I want you back with us as soon as possible﻿—incidentally, you’re now a brigadier-general﻿—but not till the situation at Grank-Krink-Skilk is stabilized. And, eventually, you’ll probably have to set up permanent headquarters in the north.”

===Chinese===
「打肥皂、递毛巾，伺候本丢·冯·彼拉多将军洗手喽！」保拉·昆顿高声叫道。

「这倒是个主意；我正发愁送约克克什么纪念礼呢，」秀吉·奥利里说。「一套精美的三十件头银餐具！」

「再合适不过，」冯·施利希滕嘉许道。「好，你这趟差办得一流。我要你尽快回到我们这边来——顺带说一句，你现在已经是一名准将了——但得等格兰克—克林克—斯基尔克一线的局势稳下来再说。而且往后，你多半得在北方设一个常驻指挥部。」

===Original===
After Hideyoshi O’Leary had thanked him and signed off, and the screen was dark again, he turned to the others.

“Well, gentlemen, I don’t think we need worry too much about the north, for the next few days. How long do you estimate this operation against Konkrook’s going to take, to complete pacification, Them?”

“How complete is complete pacification, general?” Themistocles M’zangwe wanted to know. “If you mean to the end of organized resistance by larger than squad-size groups, I’d say three days, give or take twelve hours. Of course, there’ll be small groups holding out for a couple of weeks, particularly in the farming country and back in the forest.﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
秀吉·奥利里道了谢，断了通讯，屏幕重归漆黑，他转向其余众人。

「好了，诸位，今后几天，北边的事我看不必太操心。地米，你对孔克鲁克这场行动的估计是多久——要打到完全绥靖，得用多长时间？」

「完全绥靖有多完全，将军？」地米斯托克利·姆赞圭要问个分明。「如果您指的是超过一个班规模的有组织抵抗全部终结，我估摸三天，前后差不出十二个钟头。当然，还会有小股残敌再顽抗上两三个星期，尤其是在农垦区和森林深处……」

===Original===
“We can forget them; that’s minor-tactics stuff. We’ll need to keep some kind of an occupation force here for some time; they can deal with that. We’ll have to get to work on Keegark, as soon as possible; after we’ve reduced Keegark, we’ll be able to reorganize for a campaign against the Free Cities on the Eastern Shore.”

“Begging your pardon, general, but reduce is a mild word for what we ought to do to Keegark,” Hans Meyerstein said. “We ought to raze that city as flat as a football field, and then play football on it with King Orgzild’s head.”

“Any special reason?” von Schlichten asked. “In addition to the Blount-Lemoyne massacre, that is?”

===Chinese===
「那些可以不管；那是小战术层面的事。此地还得留一支占领部队驻上些时日；残敌交给他们料理。基加克那头，我们得尽早动手；拿下基加克之后，我们就能重新整编，对东岸的自由城邦发动一场战役。」

「恕我直言，将军，『拿下』这个词，用来形容我们该对基加克干的事，还是太温和了，」汉斯·迈耶斯坦说。「我们该把那座城夷平，平得像座足球场，然后拿奥格齐尔德王的脑袋在上面踢足球。」

「有什么特别的缘故吗？」冯·施利希滕问。「我是说，除了布朗特—勒莫因那场屠杀之外？」

===Original===
“I should say so, general!” Themistocles M’zangwe backed Meyerstein up. “Bob, you tell him.”

Colonel Robert Grinell, the Intelligence officer, got up and took the cigar out of his mouth. He was short and round-bodied and bald-headed, but he was old Terran Federation Regular Army.

“Well, general, we’ve been finding out quite a bit about the genesis of this business, lately,” he said. “From up north, it probably looked like an all-Rakkeed show; that’s how it was supposed to look. But the whole thing was hatched at Keegark, by King Orgzild. We’ve managed to capture a few prominent Konkrookans”﻿—he named half a dozen﻿—“who’ve been made to talk, and a number of others have come in voluntarily and furnished information. Orgzild conceived the scheme in the beginning; Rakkeed was just the messenger-boy. My face gets the color of the Company trademark every time I think that the whole thing was planned for over a year, right under our noses, even to the signal that was to touch the whole thing off.﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「何止是特别的缘故，将军！」地米斯托克利·姆赞圭给迈耶斯坦帮腔。「鲍勃，你来讲。」

情报官罗伯特·格里内尔上校站起身，取下嘴里的雪茄。他个子矮、身圆、头秃，却是地球联邦正规军的老人马。

「是这样，将军，近来这桩事的来龙去脉，我们已经查明了相当不少，」他说。「从北边看，这大概像拉基德一个人的独角戏；局面包就设计成这个样子给人看。可整盘棋是在基加克孵出来的，孵蛋的是奥格齐尔德王。我们抓到了几个孔克鲁克的显贵」——他报了半打名字——「都已撬开了口，另有一些是自己投案、主动供料的。这套阴谋最初就是奥格齐尔德想出来的；拉基德不过是跑腿送信的。一想到整套计划筹谋了一年多，就在我们鼻子底下，连点火引爆全局的信号都事先定好了，我的脸就红得跟公司商标一个颜色……」

===Original===
“The poisoning of Sid Harrington, and our announcement of his death?” von Schlichten asked.

“You figured that out yourself, sir? Well, that was it.” Grinell went on to elaborate, while von Schlichten tried to keep the impatience out of his face. Beside him, Paula Quinton was fidgeting, too; she was thinking, as he was, of what King Orgzild and Prince Gorkrink were doing now. “And I know positively that the order for the poisoning of Sid Harrington came from the Keegarkan Embassy here, and was passed down through Gurgurk and Keeluk to this geek here who actually put the poison in the whiskey.”

“Yes. I agree that Keegark should be wiped out, and I’d like to have an immediate estimate on the time it’ll take to build a nuclear bomb to do the job. One of the old-fashioned plutonium fission A-bombs will do quite well.”

===Chinese===
「毒杀席德·哈灵顿，还有我们宣布他的死讯，对吗？」冯·施利希滕问。

「这个是您自己推想出来的，长官？不错，正是它。」格里内尔接着细讲，冯·施利希滕竭力不让脸上露出不耐。旁边的保拉·昆顿也坐立不安；她和他想着同一件事——奥格齐尔德王和戈克林克亲王此刻正在做什么。「而且我可以确凿地说，毒杀席德·哈灵顿的命令，出自本城的基加克使馆，经古古尔克和基卢克层层下达，最后落到这儿这个土佬手上——把毒药真正下进威士忌的，就是他。」

「是的。我同意，基加克该从地图上抹掉；我想立刻得到一个估算：造一颗核弹来干这活儿，要多久。老式的钚裂变原子弹就很好用。」

===Original===
Everybody turned quickly. There was a momentary silence, and then Colonel Evan Colbert, of the Fourth Kragan Rifles, the senior officer under Themistocles M’zangwe, found his voice.

“If that’s an order, general, we’ll get it done. But I’d like to remind you, first, of the Company policy on nuclear weapons on this planet.”

“I’m aware of that policy. I’m also aware of the reason for it. We’ve been compelled, because of the lack of natural fuel on Uller, to set up nuclear power reactors and furnish large quantities of plutonium to the geeks to fuel them. The Company doesn’t want the natives here learning of the possibility of using nuclear energy for destructive purposes. Well, gentlemen, that’s a dead issue. They’ve learned it, thanks to our people on Niflheim, and unless my estimate is entirely wrong, King Orgzild already has at least one First-Century Nagasaki-type plutonium bomb. I am inclined to believe that he had at least one such bomb, probably more, at the time when orders were sent to his embassy here, for the poisoning of Governor-General Harrington.”

===Chinese===
众人齐刷刷转过身来。一时鸦雀无声，随后，第四克拉冈来复枪团的埃文·科尔伯特上校——地米斯托克利·姆赞圭麾下军阶最高的军官——率先开了腔。

「如果这是命令，将军，我们一定办到。但首先我想提醒您，公司对这颗行星上的核武器是有一项政策的。」

「我知道那项政策，也知道它的缘由。由于乌勒尔缺乏天然燃料，我们被迫建起核动力反应堆，并向土佬们提供大量钚作燃料。公司不愿这里的土著得知核能还可以用于毁伤。可是，诸位，这一条已经作废了。他们已经知道了——多亏我们在尼福尔海姆的那些人；而且除非我估得大错特错，奥格齐尔德王手里已经至少有一颗第一世纪长崎型的钚弹。我倾向于相信，当初给他驻此地的使馆下达毒杀哈灵顿总督的命令时，他手里就至少有一颗这样的弹——多半还不止一颗。」

===Original===
With that, he selected a cigarette from his case, offered it to Paula, and snapped his lighter. She had hers lit, and he was puffing on his own, when the others finally realized what he had told them.

“That’s impossible!” somebody down the table shouted, as though that would make it so. Another﻿—one of the civil administration crowd﻿—almost exactly repeated Jules Keaveney’s words at Skilk: “What the hell was Intelligence doing, sleeping?”

“General von Schlichten,” Colonel Grinell took oblique cognizance of the question, “you’ve just made, by implication, a most grave charge against my department. If you’re not mistaken in what you’ve just said, I deserve to be court-martialed.”

“I couldn’t bring charges against you, colonel; if it were a court-martial matter, I’d belong in the dock with you,” von Schlichten told him. “It seems, though, that a piece of vital information was possessed by those who were unable to evaluate it, and until this afternoon, I was ignorant of its existence. Colonel Quinton, suppose you repeat what you told me, on the way down from Skilk.”

===Chinese===
说完，他从烟盒里拈出一支烟，递一支给保拉，「啪」地打着打火机。等她点着、他自己也吞云吐雾起来，众人才终于回过味来，他方才说了什么。

「不可能！」桌子那头有人喊道，仿佛嗓门一大，事实就会跟着改写似的。另一个人——文官那边的一个——几乎一字不差地重复了朱尔斯·基夫尼在斯基尔克说过的话：「情报处他妈的干什么去了，睡觉吗？」

「冯·施利希滕将军，」格里内尔上校绕着弯接住了这个问题，「您方才话里话外，是对我部门最严重的指控。如果您说的没有错，我就该上军事法庭。」

「上校，我起诉不了你；这要是军事法庭的案子，我就该跟你并肩站上被告席，」冯·施利希滕对他说。「不过，看来是有一份性命攸关的情报，攥在了掂不出它分量的人手里；直到今天下午，我连它的存在都不知道。昆顿上校，请你把从斯基尔克飞来路上对我讲的那番话，再讲一遍。」

===Original===
“Well, general, don’t you think we ought to have Dr. Gomes do that?” Paula asked. “After all, he constructed those bombs on Niflheim, and it’ll be he who’ll have to build ours.”

“That’s right.” He looked around. “Where’s Dr. Lourenço Gomes, the nuclear engineer who came in on the *Pretoria*, two weeks ago? Send out for him, and get him in here at once.”

There was another awkward silence. Then Kent Pickering, the chief of the Gongonk Island power-plant, cleared his throat.

“Why, general, didn’t you know? Dr. Gomes is dead. He was killed during the first half hour of the uprising.”

===Chinese===
「可是将军，您不觉得这活儿该请戈麦斯博士来干吗？」保拉问。「毕竟，那些炸弹就是他在尼福尔海姆造的，将来要造我们自己的，也非他不可。」

「说得对。」他环顾四周。「洛伦索·戈麦斯博士在哪儿——就是两周前搭『比勒陀利亚』号来的那位核工程师？派人去请，让他立刻到这里来。」

又是一阵难堪的沉默。随后，贡贡克岛电厂的总管肯特·皮克林清了清嗓子。

「哎呀将军，您不知道吗？戈麦斯博士死了。起义爆发后的头半个钟头，他就遇害了。」

## V You Can Depend on It It’s Wrong / 五 你可以放心，它准是错的

===Original===
Gongonk Island, with its blue-gray Company buildings, and the Terran green of the farms, and the spaceport with its ring of mooring-pylons empty since the *City of Pretoria* had lifted out, two days before, for Terra, was dropping away behind. Von Schlichten held his lighter for Paula Quinton, then lit his own cigarette.

“I was rather horrified, Friday afternoon, at the way you and Colonel O’Leary and Mr. Blount were blaspheming against Stanley-Browne,” she said. “His book is practically the sociographers’ Koran for this planet. But I’ve been checking up, since, and I find that everybody who’s been here any length of time seems to deride it, and it’s full of the most surprising misstatements. I’m either going to make myself famous or get burned at the stake by the Extraterrestrial Sociographic Society after I get back to Terra. In the last three months, I’ve been really too busy with Ex-Rights work to do much research, but I’m beginning to think there’s a great deal in Stanley-Browne’s book that will have to be reconsidered.”

“How’d you get into this, Miss Quinton?” he asked.

===Chinese===
贡贡克岛——蓝灰色的公司建筑、农田那地球式的绿意，还有那座太空港：系留塔围成的环里空空荡荡，两天前「比勒陀利亚城」号从那里起飞返回地球去了——正渐渐落在后面、越来越小。冯·施利希滕先替保拉·昆顿点着烟，再点自己的。

“星期五下午，你跟奥利里上校、布朗特先生那样亵渎斯坦利-布朗，我还挺吃惊的。”她说。“他那本书差不多是这颗行星上社会志学者的《古兰经》。可后来我一直在查证，发现凡是这里待得久的人似乎都笑话它，而且它满是些令人吃惊的错误论断。等我回到地球，我要么一举成名，要么被地外社会志学会送上火刑柱。过去这三个月，我忙着地外权利协会的活儿，实在没工夫做多少研究，但我已经开始觉得，斯坦利-布朗书里有许多东西非得重新考虑不可。”

“您是怎么入这一行的，昆顿小姐？”他问。

===Original===
“You mean sociography, or Ex-Rights? Well, my father and my grandfather were both extraterrestrial sociographers﻿—anthropologists whose subjects aren’t anthropomorphic﻿—and I majored in sociography at the University of Montevideo. And I’ve always been in sympathy with extraterrestrial races; one of my great-grandmothers was a Freyan.”

“The deuce; I’d never have guessed that, as small and dark as you are.”

“Well, another of my great-grandmothers was Japanese,” she replied. “The family name’s French. I’m also part Spanish, part Russian, part Italian, part English﻿ ﻿… the usual modern Argentine mixture.”

===Chinese===
